śāktānandataraṃgiṇī

prathamollāsaḥ

maṅgalācaraṇam

praṇamya prakṛtiṃ nityāṃ paramātmasvarūpiṇīm |
tanyate bhuktimuktyarthaṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇīm ||

prakṛtiśabdārthaḥ

atha kā prakṛtiḥ ? tathāhi � guṇatrayasāmyāvasthā prakṛtiḥ | tathā 
coktaṃ yāmale �

satvaṃ rajastama iti guṇatrayamudāhṛtam |
sāmyāvasthitireteṣāmavyaktaṃ prakṛtiṃ viduḥ |
saiva mūlaprakṛtiḥ syāt pradhānaṃ puruṣo'pi ca ||

anyatrāpi �

sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇānāṃ tritayaṃ priye |
yadā sā paramā śaktirguṇādhiṣṭhānamācaret |
prakṛtitvaṃ bhavettasyāḥ puruṣaḥ syāt sadāśivaḥ ||

nityāśabdārthaḥ 

nityāśabdārthamāha śaktiyāmale �

brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ bhavo yasyāṃ nijecchayā |
punaḥ pralīyate yasyāṃ nityā sā parikīrtitā || 

paramātmaśabdārthaḥ

	paramaścāsau ātmā ceti paramātmā, utkṛṣṭa ātmā ityarthaḥ | 
utkṛṣṭatvaṃ ca svecchayā brahmaviṣṇuśivādiśarīrāṇāmutpādakatvam | 
athavā tattadindriyarahito'pi tattadindriyajanyapratyakṣāśrayaḥ | tathā ca 
śrutiḥ (śvetā0 3/19) �

apāṇipādo javano gṛhītā paśyatyacakṣuḥ sa śṛṇotyakarṇaḥ |
sa vetti viśvaṃ nahi tasya vettā tamāhurādyaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam || iti |

	nityajñānakṛtyāśrayaḥ paramātmā | sa ca lāghavād eka eva | sa ca 
janyajñānaprakṛtyāśrayo jīvātmā, sa cānantaḥ, 
manuṣyapaśupakṣyādibhedāt | tathā śivaviṣṇudurgādīnāṃ 
śarīrabhedād paramātmā nānā evāstu iti vācyam, 
ghaṭādyupādhibhedenākāśasya nānātvabhramavat 
manuṣyapaśupakṣyādiśarīrabhedenātmano bhinnatvabhramāt 
suṣuptipralayādau śarīranāśe ātmana ekatvadarśanāt, tathā 
bhaktānugrahāya gṛhītaśarīrāṇāṃ śivādidaivatānāṃ nānātvena 
tatra nānātvabhramāt | nahi bhramād vastusiddhiriti |

	atha ullāsaviṣayanirṇayaḥ pratipādyamāha �

	ullāse prathame vakṣye śarīraṃ karmasambhavam |
	dīkṣā dvitīye vakṣyāmi tṛtīye yoganirṇayam ||

prātaḥkṛtyaṃ caturthe tu āsanaṃ pañcame tathā |
antaryāgavidhiṃ ṣaṣṭhe nityapūjā ca saptame ||

vasau mālāvidhānaṃ tu navame japalakṣaṇam |
mahāsetuṃ ca setuṃ ca kullukāṃ daśame tathā ||

mukhasya śodhanaṃ rudre dvādaśe ca puraskriyām |
saṃskāraṃ yantrarājasya balidānaṃ trayodaśe ||

phalaṃ caturdaśe caiva upacārādidānajam |
nāmasmaraṇapūjādiphalaṃ pañcadaśe tathā || 

kalau saṃsargadoṣādi prāyaścittaṃ tu ṣoḍaśe |
kuṇḍaṃ saptadaśe caiva homaṃ cāṣṭādaśe tataḥ ||

tataḥ sarvamaṅgalādināmnāmartho nirūpitaḥ |
durgādevyāśca māhātmyaṃ viśeṣeṇa pradarśitam |
gurupādarajo dhyātvā kṛta ullāsanirṇayaḥ ||

śarīrotpattikramaḥ

jñānabhāṣye devyuvāca-

śarīraṃ kīdṛśaṃ nātha muktirvā kena karmaṇā |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi brūhi me śaśiśekhara ||


īśvara uvāca 


śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi śarīraṃ karmasambhavam |
rajasvalā yadā nārī viśuddhā pañcame dine ||

pīḍitā kāmabāṇena tataḥ puruṣamīhate |
bhagaliṅgasamāyogānmaithunaṃ syāttayā tayoḥ ||

anyonyasparśanād devi ! jāyate ca mahatsukham |
kṣarate ca tathā retaḥ prāṇāpānādisaṃśritam ||

kṣitirāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca |
sarveṣāṃ tattvaṃ prāduḥ syād dehastharaktabījayoḥ ||

nābhirandhre tadā devi ! bhrāmyate ca samīraṇaiḥ |
kumbhakāro yathā cakre ghaṭate ca ghaṭādikam ||

tathā samīraṇo garbhe ghaṭate prāṇināṃ tanum |
kalalaṃ caikarātreṇa budbudaṃ pañcame dine ||

śoṇitaṃ daśarātreṇa māṃsapiṇḍaṃ caturdaśe |
māsaike'pi ca sampūrṇe māṃsapiṇḍo'ṅkurāyate ||

ādau saṃjāyate piṇḍo brahmāṇḍaḥ sadṛśāṅkuraḥ |
tasya madhye sumeruśca kaṅkāladaṇḍarūpiṇaḥ || 

carācarāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ devādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
ālayaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ merorabhyantare'pi ca ||

pradīpakalikākāro jīvo hṛdi sadā sthitam |
rajjubaddho yathā śyeno gato'pyākṛṣyate punaḥ ||

prāṇabaddhastathā jīvaḥ prāṇāpānena kṛṣyate |
jīvasya parameśāni parivāragaṇaṃ śṛṇu ||

akṣiṇī nāsike karṇau jihvā ca kamalānane |
hastau pādau maheśāni ! guhyopasthau kramāt priye ||

nābhiśca parameśāni ! manaśca parameśvari |
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyākhyāśceti dehe saṃsthitāḥ ||

indriyāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ manaḥ pramasārathiḥ |
pāpaiḥ puṇyairmaheśāni baddhaḥ syādātmanaḥ priye ||

sṅgatyā sadasatkarma jīvaḥ sarvaṃ karoti hi |
viśuddhasāttviko jīvaḥ sadasatkarmavarjitaḥ ||

manasā jīvasaṃyogāt satkāryaṃ kurute sadā |
māsadvaye tu sampūrṇe medastatra prajāyate ||

majjāsthīni tribhirmāsaiḥ keśāstvak ca catuṣṭaye |
karṇākṣināsikā vaktraṃ kaṇṭhodaraṃ ca pañcame ||

raktādutpadyate śukraṃ śukrād bindusamudbhavaḥ |
prāṇato vāyurutpannaḥ kālāgniḥ syādapānataḥ ||

śukrato nāḍikotpattiḥ śukrādagniḥ samudbhavaḥ |
māṃsataśca malotpattirmajjā cāpi tato bhavet ||

vāyunā prāṇaniṣpattirapānādagnisambhavaḥ |
śukreṇotpāditā jihvā nāsikā sarvadehinām ||

raktādutpadyate netraṃ vāmaṃ caiva tu dakṣiṇam |
prāṇādutpadyate śūnyaṃ prāṇarandhradvayaṃ tathā ||

ṣaṣṭhe mukhaṃ tathā pādau sarvāṅgāni ca saptame |
sandhiḥ sāmpūrṇatāṃ yāti aṣṭame māsi vai tataḥ ||

aṇḍādhārastu kaṅkāla ārabhya gudamūlataḥ |
dvātriśajjñānavijñeya granthiko varddhataḥ sadā |
tasya madhye sadā sarvā nāḍyastatra vyavasthitāḥ ||

śarīsthanāḍīnirṇayaḥ 

iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tṛtīyikā |
gāndhārī hastijihvā ca pūṣā caiva yaśasvinī ||

alambuṣā kuhuścaiva śaṅkhinī daśamī tathā |
anyāśca nāḍikāḥ kṣudrāḥ sahasrāṇāṃ dvisaptatiḥ ||

iḍā ca vāmabhāge tu dakṣiṇe piṅgalā tathā |
brahmarandhre suṣumnā ca gāndhārī vāmacakṣuṣi ||

dakṣiṇe hastijihvā ca pūṣā karṇe'tha dakṣiṇe |
vāme yaśasvinī caiva mukhe cālambuṣā tathā ||

kuhuśca liṅgamūle ca śaṅkhinī śirasopari |
evaṃ dvāraṃ samāvṛtya tiṣṭhanti daśanāḍikāḥ ||

kṣitiśca vāritejaśca vāyurākāśameva ca |
sthairyaṃ gatā ime pañca bāhyābhyantara eva ca |

bhūtaguṇāḥ 

asthi carma tathā nāḍī lomamāṃsastathaiva ca |
ete pañcaguṇāḥ proktāḥ pṛthivyāṃ ca vyavasthitāḥ ||

malamūtraṃ tathā śukraṃ śleṣmā śoṇitameva ca |
ete pañcaguṇāḥ proktā āpastatra vyavasthitāḥ ||

kṣudhā tṛṣṇā tathā nidrā pramohaḥ kṣāntireva ca |
ete pañcaguṇāḥ proktāstejastatra vyavasthitam ||

virodhākṣepaṇākuñcadhāraṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā |
ete pañcaguṇāḥ proktāḥ mārute ca vyavasthitāḥ ||

rāgo dveṣaśca mohaśca bhayaṃ lajjā tathaiva ca |
ete pañcaguṇāḥ proktā ākāśe ca vyavasthitāḥ ||

śarīrasthavāyunirṇayaḥ

prāṇāpānasamānaścodānavyānau ca vāyavaḥ |
nāgaḥ kūrmo'thā kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ ||

ete daśaguṇāḥ proktāḥ sarve prāṇasamātmakāḥ |
hṛdi prāṇo vasennityamapāno gudamaṇḍale ||

samāno nābhideśe ca udānaḥ kaṇṭhadeśataḥ |
vyānaḥ sarvaśarīre tu pradhānāḥ pañcavāyavaḥ ||

nagaḥ kūrmo'tha kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ |
ete nāḍīsahasreṣu vartante jīvarūpiṇaḥ ||

śarīrakoṣavarṇanam |

brahmāṇḍe ye guṇāḥ santi te tiṣṭhanti kalevare |
pātālaṃ bhūdharā lokā ādityādi navagrahāḥ ||

(bhūrādi saptasvargāśca nāmāśca sarvadehinām) |
piṇḍamadhye sthitāḥ sarve sthānaṃ teṣāṃ vadāmi te ||


śarīre saptapātālavarṇanam 


pādādhastvatalaṃ vidyāttadūrdhvaṃ vitalaṃ tathā |
jānunoḥ sutalaṃ caiva talaṃ ca sandhirandhrake |
talātalaṃ guda(lpha)madhye liṅgamūle rasātalam |
pātālaṃ kaṭisandhau ca pādādau lakṣayed budhaḥ ||


śarīre bhūrādilokakathanam 


bhūrloko nābhideśe tu bhuvarlokastathā hṛdi |
svarlokaḥ kaṇṭhadeśe tu maharlokaśca cakṣuṣi ||

janalokastadūrdhvañca tapoloko lalāṭake |
satyaloko mahāyonau bhuvanāni caturdaśe ||


śarīre saptācalavanarṇanam 


trikoṇe ca sthito merūrdhvaloke ca mandaraḥ |
kailāso dakṣiṇe koṇe vāmakoṇe himālayaḥ |
vindhyo viṣṇustadūrdhve ca saptaite kulaparvatāḥ ||


śarīrastha saptadvīpavarṇanam


asthi sthāne maheśāni ! jambūdvīpo vyavasthitaḥ |
māṃseṣu ca kuśadvīpaḥ krauñcadvīpaḥ śirāsu ca ||

śākadvīpaḥ sthitorakte prāṇiṇāṃ sarvasandhiṣu |
tadūrdhvaṃ śālmalidvīpaḥ plakṣaśca lomasañcaye |
nābhau ca puṣkaradvīpaḥ sāgarastadanantaram ||


śarīrasthasaptasāgaravarṇanam


lavaṇodastathā mūtre śukre kṣīrodasāgaraḥ |
majjā dadhisamudraśca tadūrdhvaṃ ghṛtasāgaraḥ ||


rasodake rasaḥ prokta iti pustakāntare pāṭhaḥ |


vasāpaḥ sāgaraḥ prokta ikṣuḥ syāt kaṭiśoṇitam |
śoṇiteṣu surāsindhuḥ kathitāḥ saptasāgarāḥ ||


śarīrasthagrahamaṇḍalam

grahāṇāṃ maṇḍalam caiva śṛṇu vakṣyāmi pārvati ! |
nādacakre sthitaḥ sūryo binducakre ca candrmāḥ ||

locane maṅgalaḥ prokto hṛdi somasutastathā |
udare ca guruścaiva śukre śukrastathaiva ca ||

nābhicakre sthito mando mukhe rāhuḥ sthitaḥ sadā |
pāde nābhau ca ketuśca śarīre grahamṇḍalam ||

garbhasthajīvasya pūrvajanmasmaraṇam


navame māsi garbhasthaḥ sarvān saṃsmarate manaḥ |
navadvāre pure dehi samayāṃśca vikārakān ||

sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā bhuktaṃ ca hṛdaye nṛṇām |
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ caiva yatkṛtaṃ pūrvajanmani ||

tatsarvaṃ saphalaṃ jñātvā ūrdhvapādastvadhomukhaḥ |
garbhastu saṃpraviṣṭā'sau stimite ghoradarśane ||

yadi mātā sukhaṃ bhuṅkte annapānādikaṃ tataḥ |
jananyā nābhideśe tu mukhaṃ dattvā pivatyasau ||

tato jīvati garbhe'sau anyathā maraṇaṃ bhavet |
abhyasyāmi śivaṃ jñānaṃ sāṃsārārṇavatāraṇam ||

(devadvijagurūṇāṃ hi pūjanaṃ śaṅkayānvitam) |
kariṣyāmi yathā bhāgyaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

cirayogī tato bhūtvā mukto yāsyāmi tatpadam |
evaṃ garbhasthito jīvo garbhayātanayārditaḥ |
nityaṃ bhāvayate citte labdhacaitanyalakṣaṇaḥ |
etasminnantare devi viśveṣāṃ garbhasaṅkaṭe ||

navame daśame māsi prabalaiḥ sūtimārutaiḥ |
niḥsāryate bāṇa iva jantuśchidreṇa sajvaraḥ |
patito'pi na jānāti mūrcchito'pi tataścyutaḥ ||

sūtivātasya vegena yonirandhrasya pīḍanāt |
vismṛtaṃ sakalaṃ jñānaṃ garbhe yaccintitaṃ hṛdi ||

yathā bhavati tattveṣu sūtibhūteṣu pīḍanāt |
mātaraṃ smarate nityaṃ bubhukṣā dṛḍhavedanam ||


strī-puruṣādi bhedakāraṇam


raktādhikā bhavennāro bhavecchukrādhikaḥ pumān |
napuṃsakaṃ tato jātaṃ sāmye ca rajabījayoḥ ||

pañcaitānyapi sṛjyante garbhasthasyaiva dehinaḥ |
āyuḥ karma ca vittaṃ ca vidyā nidhanameva ca ||


jīvāvasthākathanam 


bālaḥ śiśuśca pogaṇḍaḥ kiśorakastathaiva ca |
ataḥ paraṃ tu yuvakaḥ prauḍhaścaiva tataḥ param ||

atiprauḍhastathā vṛddhaścātivṛddhastataḥ param |
palitaṃ maraṇaṃ caiva avasthāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

tatkṣaṇādeva gṛhṇāti śarīramātivāhikam |
kevalaṃ tanmanuṣyāṇāṃ nānyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ kvacit ||

pretadehamiti proktaṃ kramādeva na saṃśayaḥ |
tataḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe bāndhavaiḥ satkṛte naraiḥ ||

pūrṇasaṃvatsare dehamato'nyaṃ samprapadyate |
tataḥ sa narake yāti svarge vā svena karmaṇā ||


tatkṣaṇāt mṛtyukṣaṇāditi |


jīvānāṃ karmaphalaprakāraḥ


devatvamatha mānuṣyaṃ paśutvaṃ pakṣitāṃ tathā |
kṛmitvaṃ sthāvaratvaṃ ca yāti jantuḥ svakarmabhiḥ ||

sthāvarā jaṅgamādyāśca pakṣiṇaḥ paśavo narāḥ |
jāyante vā mriyante vā saṃsāre duḥkhasāgare ||

karmaṇā jāyate jantuḥ karmaṇaiva pralīyate |
dehe vinaṣṭe tatkarma punardehaṃ prapadyate ||

tathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram |
tathā śubhāśubhaṃ karma kartāramanugacchati ||

prāktanaṃ balavatkarma ko'nyathā tatkariṣyati |
dehaḥ karmātmakaḥ proktastattad devi ! pratiṣṭhitam || 

karmayogānurūpeṇa nirmāṇaṃ vidhirādiśet |
carācaramidaṃ devi ! sarvaṃ karmātmakaṃ priye ||

mātā karma pitā karma karmaiva paramo guruḥ |
svargaṃ vā narakaṃ vāpi karmaṇaiva labhennaraḥ ||

sukhaduḥkhamayaiḥ svīyaiḥ puṇyapāpairniyantritaḥ |
tattajjātiyutaṃ dehaṃ sambhogaṃ ca svakarmajam ||

manuṣyajanmotkarṣakathanam

atra janmasahasraistu sahasrairapi pārvati ! |
kadācillabhate janturmānuṣyaṃ puṇyasañcayāt ||

nidrā ca maithunāhārāḥ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ samāḥ |
jñānavānmānavaḥ prokto jñānahīnaḥ paśuḥ priye ||

sampadaḥ svapnasaṅkāśā yauvanaṃ kusumopamam |
taḍidvat paramāyuśca yasya jñānavato dhṛtiḥ ||

caturaśītilakṣeṣu śarīreṣu śarīribhiḥ |
na mānuṣyaṃ vinānyatra tattvajñānaṃ tu labhyate ||

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādidevatābhūtajātayaḥ |
nāśamevānudhāvanti tasmācchreyaḥ samācaret ||

mohaprabhāvaḥ

svadehadhanadārādiniratāḥ sarvajantavaḥ |
jāyante ca mriyante ca hā hatā'jñānamohitāḥ ||

prabhavaṃ sarvaduḥkhānāmāśrayaṃ sakalāpadām |
ālayaṃ sarvapāpānāṃ saṃsāraṃ varjayet priye ||

pratikṣaṇamayaṃ kāyaḥ kṣīyamāṇo na lakṣyate |
āmakumbha ivāmbhastho viśīrṇā naiva bhāvyate ||

apatyaṃ me kalatraṃ me dhanaṃ me bāndhavāśca me |
lapantamiti marttyaṃ tamatti kālavṛko balāt ||

pṛthivī dahyate yena meruścāpi vidāryate |
śoṣyate sāgarajalaṃ śarīreṣvapi kā kathā ||

mohasya saṃsārakāraṇatvakathanam

lohapāśamayaiḥ pāśairnaro baddho'pi mucyate |
strīdhanādiṣu saṃsakto mucyate na kadācana ||

asakṛd dehakarmāṇi sukhaduḥkhāni bhuñjate |
paratrā'jñānino devi ! yāntyāyānti punaḥ punaḥ ||

arajjubandhanaṃ saṅgo duṣṭasaṅgo mahāviṣaḥ |
satsaṅgaśca vivekaśca nirmalaṃ nayanadvayam |
yasya nāsti naraḥ so'ndhaḥ kathaṃ na syādamārgagaḥ ||

mokṣakāraṇam

dve pade mokṣabandhāya nirmameti mameti ca |
mameti badhyate janturnimameti ca mucyate ||

mametyadhyāsanād bandho vimuktirnirmameti ca |
māṃsalubdho yathā matsyo lauhaśaṅkuṃ na paśyati ||

sukhalubdhastathā dehī yamabādhāṃ na paśyati |
kṛtvā pāpavinirbhinnaṃ siktaṃ viṣayasarpiṣā ||

rāgadveṣānalaiḥ pakvaṃ mṛtyuraśnāti mānavam |
svadehamapi jīvo'yaṃ tyaktvā yāti kuleśvari ||

strīmātṛdhanaputrādisambandhaḥ kena hetunā |

saṃsārasya duḥkharūpatvavarṇanam

śataṃ jīvanamatyalpaṃ nidrā tasyārdhahāriṇī |
bālyarogajarāduḥkhairarddha tadapi niṣphalam ||

duḥkhamūlo hi saṃsāraḥ sa yasyāsti sa duḥkhitaḥ |
tasya tyāgaḥ kṛto yena sa sukhī nāparaḥ priye ||

prabhāte malamūtrābhyāṃ madhyāhne kṣutpipāsayā |
rātrau madananidrābhyāṃ bādhyante mānavāḥ sadā ||

divyauṣadhaṃ na sevante mahāvyādhivināśanam |
tad vyādhirvaddhanāpathyaṃ kurvanti bahuśo janāḥ ||

sukarmaphaladaṃ hitvā duṣkarmāṇi karoti yaḥ |
kāmadhenuṃ samākramya hyarkakṣīraṃ sa mārgatiḥ ||

anityāni śarīrāṇi vibhavo naiva śāśvataḥ |
nityaṃ sannihito mṛtyuḥ kartavyo dharmasañcayaḥ ||

adhruveṇa śarīreṇa pratikṣaṇavināśinā |
yo dhruvaṃ nārjayed dharma sa martyo mūḍhacetanaḥ ||

nāmutra hi sahāyārthaṃ pitā mātā ca gacchati |
nāpi putro na vā jāti dharmastiṣṭhati kevalam ||

putradāramayaiḥ pāśaiḥ pumān baddho na mucyate |
paṇḍite caiva mūrkhe ca balinyapyatha durbale ||

īśvare ca daridre ca mṛtyoḥ sarvatra tulyatā |
rājataḥ salilādagneścaurataḥ svajanādapi ||

bhayamarthavatāṃ nityaṃ mṛtyoḥ pāpabhṛtāmiva |
śvaḥkāryamadya kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇikam ||

nahi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtamasya na vā kṛtam |
karmaṇā manasā vācā yaḥ sa karmanirataḥ sadā ||

aphalākāṅkṣi citto yaḥ sa mokṣamadhigacchati |

aphalākāṅkṣi svakīyabhogajanakāṃkṣārahitamityarthaḥ |

aho mohasya māhātmyaṃ tanmāyājanitasya ca |
kimanyamapi deveśi mohayedamarānapi ||

iti yāmalavacanāt |

mārkaṇḍeye �


mahāmāyā hareścaitattayā sammohyate jagat |

tayā mahāmāyayā jagat saṃsāraḥ mohyate | na kevalaṃ jagat sammohyate, 
devānāmapi cetāṃsi |

jñānināmapi cetāṃsi devī bhagavatī hi sā ||

balādākṛṣya mohāya mahāmāyā prayacchati |

jñānināmiti praśaṃsāyāmin, nityajñānināmityarthaḥ ||

mahāmāyāśabdārthaḥ

	mahatī cāsau māyā ceti mahāmāyā, brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnā 
mohajanakatvāt mahāmāyā | tathā coktaṃ yāmale �

saiva māyā prakṛtiryā sammohayati śaṅkaram |
hariṃ tathā virañci ca tathaivānyāṃśca nirjarān ||

kālikāpurāṇe (6, 61-63) �

garbhāntarjñānasampannaṃ preritaṃ sūtimārutaiḥ |
utpannaṃ jñānarahitaṃ kurute yā nirantaram ||

pūrvātipūrvasaṃskārasammohaṃ saṃniyojya ca |
āhārādau tato mohaṃ mamatvaṃ jñānasaṃśayam ||

krodhoparodhanādiṣu kṣiptvā kṣiptvā punaḥ punah |
paścāt kāme niyojyāśu cintāyuktamaharnniśam ||

mahāmāyābhedaḥ

	sā mahāmāyā dvividhā-vidyā'vidyā ca | yā mahāmāyā 
mukterhetubhūtā sā vidyā | yā mahāmāyā saṃsārabandhanahetubhūtā 
sā'vidyā | mārkaṇḍeye �

sā vidyā paramāmukterhetubhūtā sanātanī |
saṃsārabandhahetuśca saiva sarveśvareśvarī ||

anyatrāpi �

vidyā vāpyathavā'vidyā dve ete māyayāvṛte |
tatkarma yacca bandhāya sā'vidyā parikīrtitā ||

yanna bandhāya tatkarma sā vidyā parikīrtitā ||

vidyā tu sarvadā sevyā nāpyavidyā kathañcana |
avidyā karmabandhaḥ syād tayā jñānaṃ praṇaśyati ||

jñānanāśād bhaveddhānirhānāt saṃsaraṇaṃ punaḥ |
saṃsārāttu bhaved ghorād ghoraṃ narakameva ca |
tasmādavidyā kutrāpi na sevyāpi kadācana ||

vidyāpraśaṃsā

yā vidyā sā mahāmāyā sā tu sevyā sadā budhaiḥ |

'andhaṃ tamaḥ praviśanti ye'vidyāmupāsate' (īśa. 9) iti śruteḥ | 
anyatrāpi saṃsāraikaniyatirūpā'vidyā iti | rudrayāmale �

sukhadā mokṣadā nityā sarvabhūteṣu saṃsthitā |
yadā tuṣṭā bhavenmāyā tadā siddhimupālabhet ||

bandanīyā sadā stutyā pūjanīyā ca sarvadā |
śrotavyā kīrttitavyā ca māyā nityā nagātmajā ||

vṛthā na kālaṃ gamayed dyūtakrīḍādinā sudhīḥ |
gamayed devatāpūjājapayāgastavādinā ||

kimanyairasadālāpairyadāyurvyayatāmiyāt |
tasmānmantrādikaṃ sarvaṃ vijñāya śrīgurormukhāt |
sukhena mucyate devi ghorasaṃsārabandhanāt ||

śūdradīkṣāvicāraḥ

atra śūdradīkṣādhikāra śruteḥ na śūdrāya manuṃ dadyāt iti vacanaṃ

�

	iti vacanāt devatāntarasya mantre śūdrānāmadhikāraḥ |

mantroddhāraḥ

gurudīkṣātantre �

mṛtamapyanugacchettu vidyāmantro viśeṣataḥ |
mana eva manuṣyasya pūrvakarmāṇi śaṃsati ||

yadi na syānmaheśāni manuṣyatvaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
dīkṣāyāṃ ca kathaṃ tasya mano bhavati pārvati ||

tasmāttu yatnato devi pūrvavidyāṃ samuddharet |

mantralikhananiyamaḥ

gurudīkṣātantre �

bakulāśvatthavaṭakaṃ patraratnaṃ śṛṇu priye ! |
vaṭapatre maheśāni śaktimantraṃ likhet priye ||

aśvatthe viṣṇumantraṃ ca bakule śivamantrakam |
raktagandhena deveśi kāśmīrairvā maheśvari ||

śaktimantraṃ likhet devi candanairviṣṇumantrakam |
bhasmanā śivamantraṃ ca vilikhet parameśvari ||

vilikhediti saptasaptaṣu patreṣu tattaddevatāyāḥ mantraṃ likhedityarthaḥ |

prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ tanmantre kārayedyatnataḥ sudhīḥ |

tattad devatāyāḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kuryādityarthaḥ |

yathāśaktyupacāreṇa sampūjya parameśvari ||

tataḥ śiṣyaścārghyapātraṃ haste kṛtvā maheśvari |
anena manunā mantrī bhāskarāya nivedayet ||

arghyadravyam

arghyadravyamāha �

āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi ghṛtaṃ dadhi tathā madhu |
raktāni karavīrāṇi tathā raktaṃ ca candanam ||

aṣṭāṅgaṃ eṣako'rghyo vai bhānave parikīrtitaḥ |

gāndharve �

na dadyād bhāskarāyārghyaṃ śaṅkhatoyairmaheśvari |

arghyadānamantraḥ

oṃ bho deva pṛthivīpāla sarvaśaktisamanvita |
mamārghyaṃ ca gṛhāṇa tva pūrvavidyāṃ prakāśaya ||

arghyaṃ dattvā namaskṛtya kṛtāñjaliḥ paṭhettataḥ |
oṃ sūryaḥ somo yamaḥ kālo mahābhūtāni pañca vai |
ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ ||

sarve devā śarīrasthā mama mantrasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
pūrvajanmārjitaṃ vidyāṃ mama haste pradāpaya ||

śāktikīdīkṣā

paṭhitvedaṃ maheśāni sattvaraṃ patramuddharet |
uddhṛtya patramekaṃ tu gurorhaste pradāpayet ||

gurustu akṣaraśreṇīmadhītya parameśvari |
setuṃ dattvā maheśāni tanmantrāṣṭaśataṃ japet ||

śiṣyasya mastake hastaṃ dattvā cāṣṭaśataṃ japet |

(anyatra-gurustu prāṅmukho bhūtvā śiṣyaḥ pratyaṅmukhasthitaḥ |)

trivāraṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe vāmakarṇe tathā sakṛt |
strīśūdraviṣaye kuryād vaiparītyena cintanam ||

	etacca viṣṇvādiviṣayam | śaktau ca �

ācamya saṃyato bhūtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya ca |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā ṝṣyādikasamanvitam |
aṣṭakṛtvā vadenmantraṃ vāmakarṇe sureśvari ||

iyaṃ dīkṣā sarvatantre śāktikī parikīrtitā |
gurorlabdhvā mahāvidyāṃ aṣṭottaraśataṃ japet ||

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet |
gurave guruputrāya tatpatnyai vā pradāpayet ||

kulārṇave �

śrīgurau prītimāpanne devatāprītimāpnuyāt |
deve ca prītimāpanne mantrasiddhirbhaved dhruvam ||

patraratnapradānena dīkṣāṃ kuryāt kalau yuge |
tataḥ siddho bhavenmantrī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||

etajjñānaṃ vinā devi dīkṣāṃ kuryācca yo naraḥ |
dīkṣā tu viphalā tasya cānte hi narakaṃ vrajet ||

tataḥ śiṣyo maheśāni praṇamed daṇḍavad bhuvi |
tvat prasādādaha deva kṛtakṛtyo'smi sarvataḥ |
māyāmṛtyumahāpāśād vimukto'smi śivo'smi ca ||

evamābhāṣya deveśi ! śrīguroścaraṇāntike |
patitastanmanāḥ śiṣyastiṣṭhed bhūmau tu vāgyataḥ ||

anujñāṃ śrīguroryāvanna labhetotthituṃ priye ! |
utthāpayed guruḥ śiṣyaṃ mantrametaṃ samuccaran ||

uttiṣṭha vatsa ! mukto'si samyagācāravān bhava |
kīrtiḥ śrīḥ kāntimedhāyurbalārogyaṃ sadāstu te ||

yoginīhṛdaye �

mantraṃ dattvā copavāsaṃ gururnaiva samācaret |
mahāndhakāranarake kṛmirbhavati cānyathā ||

dīkṣāṃ labdhvā yadā mantrī copavāsaṃ samācaret |
tasya devaḥ sadā ruṣṭaḥ śāpaṃ dattvā vrajet puram ||

upadeśadīkṣā

tatvasāre �

candrasūryagrahe tīrthe siddhakṣetre śivālaye |
mantramātraprakathanamupadeśaḥ sa ucyate ||

rudrayāmale �

śyāmāyāṃ bhairavī tārācchinnamastāsu bhairave |
majjughoṣe tathā raudre pañcāṅgaṃ neṣyate budhaiḥ ||

	tatrāpi guhyakālīviṣaye pañcāyatanīdīkṣā'styeva | yathā 
viśvasāre �

bhūpureṣu catuṣkoṇe pūjayet kramaśaḥ sudhīḥ |
viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca sūryaṃ śaktiṃ yathākramāt |
pūjayecca maheśāni pradhānaṃ madhyato nyasan ||

dīkṣāyāṃ cakrādivicārasyānāvaśyakatvam ||

yaḥ kuryāccakragaṇanāṃ dīkṣāyāṃ paśupāmaraḥ |
sa bhraṣṭaḥ sa ca pāpiṣṭho viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ ||

kiṃ ṝṇaiḥ kiṃ dhanairvāpi rāśyādikavicāraṇaiḥ |
siddhasādhyasusiddhārivicāraṃ parivarjayet ||

nāsti satyaṃ maheśāni nakṣatryādivicāraṇā |
rāśyādigaṇanā nāsti śaṅkareṇeti bhāṣitam ||

āgamakalpadrume �

ravisaṃkramaṇe caiva sūryasya grahaṇe tathā |
tatra lagnādikaṃ kiñcinna vicāryaṃ kathañcana ||

yāmale �

śaratkāle yugādyāyāṃ grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
bodhane caiva durgāyāḥ kālākālaṃ na śodhayet ||

kālaviśeṣa-mantraviśeṣagrahaṇaniyamaḥ

matsyasūkte �

grahaṇe ca mahātīrthe nāsti kālasya nirṇayaḥ |
somagrahe viṣṇumantraṃ sūrye śaktiṃ na cācaret ||

yāmale �

sūryagrahe śaktimantraṃ na pradadyājjijīviṣuḥ |
na gṛhṇīyādapi tathā yadīcchedātmano hitam ||

tatra viśeṣavidhiḥ

atra śaktipadaṃ pañcamīparam, prakaraṇādityudayakaraḥ | ata eva �

śrīkāmakālībījāni lopādaurgaśca yo manuḥ |
sūryasyopagrahe labdho nṛṇāṃ śīghraphalapradaḥ ||

	iti yāmalavacanamapi saṅgacchate | parāśrīkāmabījānīti 
kulamūlāvatāre pāṭhaḥ | pūrvavacane śaktimantrapadaṃ 
śrībījādyatiriktamantraparamiti tu śivadīkṣāṭīkākṛtaḥ | yāmale �

lagne vāpyathavā'lagne yatra kutra tithāvapi |
gurorājñānurūpeṇa dīkṣā kāryā viśeṣataḥ ||

na tithirna vrataṃ pūjā na snānaṃ na japakriyā |
dīkṣāyāṃ kāraṇaṃ kiñcit svecchāprāpte ca sadgurauḥ ||

sarve vārāḥ grahāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi ca rāśayaḥ |
yasminnahani santuṣṭo guruḥ sarve śubhāvahāḥ |
yadaivecchā tadā dīkṣā gurorājñānusārataḥ ||

mantrāṇāṃ daśasaṃskāram

atha daśasaṃskāramāha śāradāyām (2.112) �

jananaṃ jīvanaṃ paścāttāḍanaṃ bodhanaṃ tathā |
athābhiṣeko vimalīkaraṇāpyāyane punaḥ ||

tarpaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ guptirdaśaitā mantrasaṃskriyāḥ |
mantrāṇāṃ mātṛkāmadhyāduddhāro jananaṃ smṛtam ||

	mātṛkāvarṇāstu akārādikṣakārāntāḥ | mātṛkāvarṇāstu 
akārādikṣakārāntā mātṛkārṇāḥ prakīrtitāḥ iti 
tantragandharvavacanāt | mātṛkāyantralekhanamāha �

bhūmau gomayaliptāyāṃ vilikhyā'ṣṭadalānvitam |
candanādyaiḥ kaṭhinyā vā tārttīyaṃ karṇikāgatam ||

dvirdviḥ svarān keśareṣu vargānaṣṭadaleṣu ca |

tārttīye hasauḥ �

kādimāntāḥ pañcavargā mātṛkāḥ kramaśoditāḥ |
yādivāntāḥ śādihāntā lakṣaṇe vilikhettataḥ ||

iti mātṛkāyantram |

tasmācca gandhapaṅkena bhūrjādaumantramuddharet |
praṇavāntaritān kṛtvā mantravarṇān japet sudhīḥ |
etajjīvanamityāhumantratantraviśāradāḥ ||

daśadhā śatadhā vā japaḥ | yathā viśvasāre �

pṛthak śataṃ vā daśadhā mantravarṇān japet sudhīḥ |
mantravarṇān samālikhya tāḍayeccandanāmbhasā ||

pratyekaṃ vāyunā mantrī tāḍanaṃ tadudāhṛtam |

tantrāntare �

tāḍanaṃ tāḍayed varṇānakhilāṃścandanāmbhasā |
śataṃ vā daśadhā vāpi bodhayettu manuṃ tataḥ |
vilikhya mantraṃ tanmantrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ |
tanmantrākṣarasaṃkhyātairhanyād yāntena bodhanam ||

yāntena ramiti bījena |

svatantroktavidhānena mantrī mantrārṇasaṃkhyayā |
aśvatthapallavairmantramabhiṣiñced viśuddhaye ||

tantrāntare �

mantrasya cāmukaṃ varṇamabhiṣiñcāmi hṛdyutam |
abhiṣiñcedaṣṭadhā vā pratyekamabhiṣecanam |
kuśodakena dugdhenā'bhiṣecanamudāhṛtam || iti |

sāñcintya manasā mantraṃ jyotirmantreṇa nirdahet |
mantre malatrayaṃ mantrī vimalīkaraṇaṃ tvidam |
tāraṃ vyomāgnimanuyuk daṇḍī jyotirmanurmataḥ ||

manuścaturdaśasvaro daṇḍī anusvāraḥ | tena oṃ hrauṃ iti |

kuśodakena japtena pratyarṇa prokṣaṇaṃ manoḥ |
tena mantreṇa vidhivadetadāpyāpanaṃ matam ||

anyatra �

amukamantraṃ tarpayāmi nama ityambhasā ca tam |
madhunā śaktimantre vaiṣṇave cendumajjalaiḥ ||

śaive ghṛtena dugdhena tarpaṇaṃ samudīritam |

daśadhā tarpayettāvaditi tarpaṇam |

tāramāyāramāyoge manordīpanamucyate |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ caiva dīpayet sādhakottamaḥ || iti |

tantrāntare saptadhā dīpanamiti |

japyamānasya mantrasya gopanaṃ tvaprakāśanam ||

iti mantrāṇāṃ daśasaṃskārāḥ |


iṣṭadevasya nityapūjyatvakathanam

viśvasāre �

grahītvā ca mahāvidyāṃ japejjīvāvadhi priye |
mahāgurunipātādau na pūjāyāṃ vikalpanā ||

mohādvā yadi vā daivāt pūjayenna ca sādhakaḥ |
tasya sarvavināśaḥ syānmārayettaṃ sadāśivaḥ ||

aśucau vā śucau vāpi sarvadeśe'pi sarvadā |
pūjayet parayā bhaktyā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||

rudrayāmale �

pūjayenmṛtake vā'pi janane sarujo'pi vā |
sarvatraiṣa vidhiḥ proktaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ ||

sūtakinaḥ pūjāvidhiḥ

atha sūtakinaḥ pūjāṃ vakṣyāmyāgamacoditām |
snātvā nityaṃ ca nirvartya mānasyā kriyayā tu vai |
bāhyapūjākrameṇaiva dhyānayogena pūjayet ||

	devīviṣaye bāhyapūjā kartavyā viśeṣavidhānāt | tathā coktaṃ 
vārāhītantre �

tārāyāścaiva kālyāśca tripurāyāśca suvrate |
sūtake mṛtake caiva na tyajeyurjapārcanam ||

yāmale �

aśucirvā śucirvāpi gacchaṃstiṣṭhan svapannapi |
na doṣo mānase jāpye sarvadeśe'pi sarvadā ||

viśvasāre �

jāgratśayāna uttiṣṭhan gacchan bhuñjāna eva vā |
siddhamantre na doṣaḥ syādaśauce'pi japet sadā |
na kalpanā divā rātrau na ca sandhyāvasānake ||

atha gurumāhātmyam

guruḥ sarvasurādhīśo guruḥ sākṣī kṛtākṛte |
sampūjya sakalaṃ karma kuryāttasyājñayā sadā ||

gamanaṃ pūjanaṃ jāpaṃ bhojanaṃ mananaṃ tathā |
gṛhītvājñāṃ guroḥ kuryāttasya siddhirvinā japāt ||

tantre �

trisandhyaṃ śrīgurordhyānaṃ trisandhyaṃ pūjanaṃ guroḥ |
trisandhyaṃ bhāveyennityaṃ guruṃ paramakāraṇam ||

svaguruṃ hi vinā devi ! nānyaṃ ca gurumarcayet |
pratyakṣo vā parokṣo vā pratyahaṃ praṇamed gurum ||

ekagrāme sthito nityaṃ trisandhyaṃ praṇamed gurum |
krośamātraṃ sthito bhaktyā guruṃ pratidinaṃ namet ||

arddhayojanataḥ śiṣyaḥ praṇamet pañcaparvasu |
ekayojanamārabhya yojanadvādaśāvadhiḥ ||

tattatsaṃkhyāgatairmāsaiḥ praṇamet śrīguruṃ priye |
yadi dūre ca cārvaṅgi śrīgururnaganadini |
saṃvatsarasya madhye tu dvivāraṃ pūjayed gurum ||

dvivāramiti ekadhottarāyaṇe ekadhā dakṣiṇāyane ityarthaḥ |

evaṃ yo nācared devi ! sa bhaved brahmarākṣasaḥ |
ekatra guruṇā sārddhaṃ svapityupaviśecca yaḥ ||

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśaḥ |

tantre �

gurumālokayan śiṣya uttiṣṭhannāsanaṃ tyajet ||

jātividyādhanāḍhyo'pi dūre dṛṣṭvā guruṃ mudā |
praṇamed daṇḍavad bhūmau pradakṣiṇamathācaret ||

āyāntamagrato gacched gacchantaṃ tamanuvrajet |
praṇamya pravaset pārśve tadā gacchedanujñayā ||

mukhāvalokī sevet kuryādājñādimādarāt |
asatyaṃ na vadedagre na bahupralapedapi ||

ṛṇadānaṃ tathādānaṃ vastūnāṃ krayavikrayam |
na kuryād guruṇā sārddhaṃ śiṣyo bhūtvā kathañcana ||

gururmātā pitā svāmī bāndhavāśca suhṛd guruḥ |
ityādhāya mano nityaṃ yajet sarvātmā gurum ||

guroragre pṛthak pūjāmauddhatyaṃ ca vivarjayet |
dīkṣāṃ vyākhyāṃ prabhutvaṃ ca guroragre parityajet ||

āsanaṃ śayanaṃ vastraṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ pādukāṃ tathā |
chāyāṃ kalatramanyad vā yad dṛṣṭaṃ tat supūjayet ||

yathā deve mantre yathā mantre tathā gurau |
yathā gurau tathā svātmanyevaṃ bhaktikramaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

guruśayyāsanaṃ yānaṃ pādukopānahau tathā |
snānodakaṃ tathāchāyāṃ laṅghayenna kadācana ||

anyatrāpi �

devacchāyāṃ gurucchāyāṃ śakticchāyāṃ na laṅghayet |
pramādato'pi ced devi ! guroragre prapūjayet ||

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ sā pūjā niṣphalā bhavet |
riktahastena nopeyād rājānaṃ devatāṃ gurum ||

phalapuṣpān varīdani yathāśaktyā samarpayet |
bhaktyā śaktyanusāreṇa gurumuddiśya yatkṛtam ||

svalpaṃ vā bahulaṃ tulyaṃ phalamāḍhyadaridrayoḥ |
gurvarthe kṛpaṇo devi ! rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||

guruvākyaṃ mṛṣā kṛtvā ātmavākyaṃ tu sthāpayet |
guruṃ jetumanā yaḥ saḥ pacyate narakārṇave ||

gurornāma na bhāṣeta japakālādṛte kvacit |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca naganandini ||

bhuñjate vibudhā bhaktyā gurorucchiṣṭamuttamam |
āgacched yadi cārvaṅgi ! guruḥ śiṣyasya mandire ||

śiṣyasya mandiraṃ devi koṭisūryagrahaiḥ samam |
candragrahaṇakālo hi taddinaṃ varavarṇini ||

gurordarśanamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
guruṃ vā guruputraṃ vā patnīṃ vā varavarṇini ||

vilaṅghya yadi cārvaṅgi gacchet sādhakasattamaḥ |
tatkṣaṇāccañcalāpāṅgi narakaṃ cottarottaram ||

uttarakalpe �

sākṣādvāpi parokṣe vā gurorājñāṃ samācaret |
(parokṣe tadanujñānaṃ vidhānaṃ śṛṇu śaṅkari) ||

pūjākāle ca cārvaṅgi āgacchecchiṣyamandiram |
gururvā tatsuto vā'pi tatpatnī vā maheśvarī ||

tadā pūjāṃ parityajya pūjayet svaguruṃ priye |
yadyalpaṃ hi gurordravyamadattaṃ svīkarotyapi ||

tiraścāṃ yonimāgacchet kravyādairbhakṣyate sadā |
sahasrāre guroḥ pādapadmaṃ dhyātvā prapūjya ca ||

stutvā karapuṭaṃ kṛtvā manasā dhyānatatparaḥ |
vihitaṃ vidadhe nātha ! vidheyaṃ yatkṛpāṃ kuru ||

aviruddhaṃ bhavatvatra tattvadīyaprasādataḥ |
iti mantreṇa samprārthya tadādiṣṭaṃ samācaret ||

mahiṣamardinītantre �

śrīdevyuvāca

devadeva mahādeva kṛpayā parameśvara |
gurupūjāvidhānaṃ me vistarād vada śaṅkara ! ||

īśvara uvāca

divyaṃ vīraṃ ca cārvaṅgi pūrvoktaṃ bahuśaḥ priye |
mānavasya kramaṃ devi saṃkṣepānnigadāmi te ||

guruḥ paraguruścaiva parāparagurustathā |
svaguruḥ parameśāni sākṣād brahma na saṃśayaḥ ||

tad guruḥ syāt paraguruḥ svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ kṣitau sadā |
parāparagurustasya guruḥ sākṣātmaheśvaraḥ |
gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ |
ata-eva maheśāni sākṣād brahmamayo guruḥ ||

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ sarvavyāpinamīśvaram |
sarveśaṃ sarvadaṃ devaṃ praṇamāmi punaḥ punaḥ ||

purastāt pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe namastubhyaṃ namonamaḥ |
trisandhyaṃ śrīgurordhyānaṃ trisandhyaṃ pūjanaṃ guroḥ ||

trisandhyaṃ bhāvayennityaṃ guruṃ paramakāraṇam |
guruṃ vinā varārohe ! nāsti siddhiḥ kadācana ||

guruṃ smṛtvā maheśāni divase divase priye |
pūjayed mānasairgandhairdhūpairdīpaistathottamaiḥ ||

bhakṣyairbhojyaistathā peyairdadhidugdhairanekadhā |
panasairnārikelaiśca tathā rambhāphalaiḥ priye ||

annairnānāvidhairdevi pūjayet svaguruṃ priye |
svaguruṃ hi vinā devi nānyaṃ ca gurumarcayet ||

(matsyairmāṃsairmaheśāni pūjayed bhaktitaḥ priye |)
gandhairmālyaiśca cārvaṅgi pūjayed bhaktitaḥ sadā ||

svarṇaiśca paṭṭavastraiśca tathā kārpāsasambhavaiḥ |
avicitrairvicitraiśca atisūkṣmairmanoharaiḥ ||

āsanairvividhairdevi raktakambalasaṃyutaiḥ |
tathā nānāvidhairdravyaiḥ pūjayet svaguruṃ sadā ||

alaṅkāraistathā devi vividhaiḥ svarṇanirmitaiḥ |
rājataiścaiva cārvaṅgi svaguruṃ pūjayet sadā ||

gurormantraṃ maheśāni prajayet suravandite |
guroḥ patnīṃ maheśāni pūjayed vidhinā'munā ||

guruvad guruputreṣu guruvat tatsutādiṣu |
pratyahaṃ pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ cāmunā vidhinā priye ||

gurorabhāve cārvaṅgi gurupatnīṃ ca pūjayet |
tadabhāve varārohe gurukanyāṃ ca pūjayet ||

tadabhāve maheśāni gurusnuṣāṃ prapūjayet |
eṣāmabhāve cārvaṅgi gurorgotraṃ prapūjayet ||

gotrābhāve varārohe tathā mātāmahaṃ guroḥ |
mātulaṃ mātulānīṃ vā pūjayed vidhinā'munā ||

yadi no pūjayed devi anena vidhinā priye |
prāyaścittī bhaved devi tatkṣaṇāt sa ca sādhakaḥ ||

saṃvatsarasya madhye tu na gacchet yadi sādhakaḥ |
mandiraṃ gurudevasya sadā kāśīpurīsamam ||

kāśī samaṃ maheśāni yaḥ paśyed gurumandiram |
śivatulyo bhaved devi tatkṣaṇāt sa ca sādhakaḥ ||

gurorgehaṃ samāsādya ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇaṃ caret |
tadaiva sahasā siddhiḥ sādhakasya bhavet priye ||

abhuktvā gurudevasya cocchiṣṭaṃ varavarṇini |
vidyāṃ vā parameśāni mantraṃ vā naganandini ||

na japettu kadācittu kutracit kvacideva hi |
tanmukhaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi viṣṭhākūpasamaṃ priye ||

ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇād devi mukhasya śodhanaṃ priye |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca naganandini ||

bhuñjate vividhaṃ bhaktyā gurorucchiṣṭamuttamam |
gurorucchiṣṭamannaṃ ca sadānandamayaṃ priye ||

guruṃ vā guruputraṃ vā patnīṃ vā varavarṇini |
vilaṅghya yadi cārvaṅgi gacchet sādhakasattamaḥ ||

tatkṣaṇāccañcalāpāṅgi narakaṃ cottarottaram |
mandiraṃ gurudevasya kuṭīraṃ yadi pārvati |
kailāśasadṛśākāraṃ tadeva naganandini ||

yad yadiṣṭatamaṃ loke sādhakasya śucismite |
tatsarvaṃ gurave dadyād bhaktyā paramayatnataḥ ||

tadaiva sahasā devi mantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate |
gurorājñāṃ samādāya prajapedaniśaṃ yadi ||

tadaiva sahasā siddhiraṣṭasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
pūjākāle ca cārvaṅgi āgacchecchiṣyamandiram ||

gururvā guruputro vā patnī vā varavarṇini |
tadā pūjāṃ parityajya pūjayet svaguruṃ priye ||

devatāpūjanārthaṃ ca gandhapuṣpādikaṃ priye |
tatsarvaṃ gurave dattvā pūjayennaganandini |
tadaiva sahasā devi devatā prītimāpnuyāt ||

rudrayāmale �

gururvā gurupatnī vā putro vāpi samāgataḥ ||

jyeṣṭho vāpyarcanāmadhye śiṣyaḥ sarvārcanāṃ tyajet |
ājñayā pūjayecchiṣya iti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ ||

gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathā dhūpaistathā naivedyakairapi |
pūjayed vividhairbhaktyā svaguruṃ tatsutaṃ ca vā ||

(gurudevo haraḥ sākṣāttatpatnī haravallabhā |
guruputro gaṇeśaḥ syādvibhāvya pūjanaṃ caret) ||

gurupatnī maheśāni sākṣād devīsvarūpiṇī |
gaṇeśasadṛśaṃ devi guruputraṃ vibhāvayet ||

śiṣyasya taddinaṃ devi koṭisūryagrahaiḥ samam |
candragrahaṇakālaṃ hitaddinaṃ varavarṇini ||

gurordarśanamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
tatkṣaṇāñcañcalāpāṅgi dānaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||

svarṇagotilavastrāṇā rajatasya viśeṣataḥ |
guroḥ prītiṃ samuddiśya dānaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

śrīgurau prītimāpanne devatāprītimāpnuyāt |
prītāyāṃ devatāyāṃ tu mantrasiddhirbhaved dhruvam ||

guroḥ samīpe cārvaṅgi na mithyā coccaret kvacit |
guroraṅgo maheśāni devatākāramuttamam ||

guroḥ kṛpā maheśāni pūjāmūlaṃ mahat padam |
gurorvākyaṃ mantramūlaṃ paraṃbrahma svayaṃ guruḥ ||

anena vidhinā devi pratyahaṃ bhāvayed gurum |
tadaiva sahasā siddhirjāyate kamalānane ||


iti śrīparivrājakācārya 
paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānandagirikṛtāyāṃ 
śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ dīkṣānirṇayo nāma dvitīyollāsaḥ || 2 ||




tṛtīyollāsaḥ

yoganirṇayaḥ

vinā copāsanāṃ devī na dadāti phalaṃ nṛṇām |

tantre �

dhyātaḥ smṛtaḥ pūjito vā tustovā namito'pi vā |
jñānato'jñānato vāpi pūjakānāṃ vimuktidaḥ ||

ityādiṣu pūjādikaṃ vinā caturvargaphalaṃ na sambhavatīti jñāyate |

vigrahasṛṣṭikāraṇam

	nirguṇasya brahmaṇaḥ kena prakāreṇa pūjādikaṃ kāryaṃ, 
śarīrarahitatvāt | kena prakāreṇa vā tanmuktyādikaṃ dātuṃ śakyate ? ata 
eva sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya saguṇa-nirguṇabhedād brahmaṇo 
dvaividhyamāha � śrīrāmatāpanīyaśrutau kulārṇave ca �

cinmayasyā'dvitīyasya niṣphalasyā'śarīriṇaḥ |
upāsakānāṃ kāryārthaṃ brahmaṇo rūpakalpanā ||

asyārthaḥ � cinmayasya jñānamayasya | mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe �

citirūpeṇa yā kṛtsnametadvyāpya sthitā jagat |

advitīyasya ekasya | tathā coktaṃ yoginīhṛdaye �

ekaṃ hi paramaṃ brahma nānātvena nirūpyate |
sthūla-sūkṣmavibhedena paraṃ brahmasvarūpiṇī ||

gopālatāpanīyaśrutirapi �

ekameva paraṃ brahma māyayā tu catuṣṭayam ||

	tasmād brahmaiva puṃrūpeṇa strīrūpeṇa ca māyayaiva naṭavad 
bahudhā bhavati |

bahurūpa ivābhāti māyayā bahurūpayā

	iti śruteḥ | niṣkalasya kalā māyā tayā rahitasya | āgneyapurāṇe �

sakalo niṣkalo jñeyaḥ sarvajñaḥ paramo hariḥ |
dehasthaḥ sakalo jñeyo niṣkalo dehavarjitaḥ ||

harirityupalakṣaṇam | yāmale �

saguṇā nirguṇā ceti mahāmāyā dvidhā matā |
saguṇā māyayā yuktā hīnā tu nirguṇā ||

	aśarīriṇaḥ mukhahastapādādyavayavāvacchinna śarīrarahitasya | 
bhūtaśuddhau �

niṣphalaṃ paramaṃ brahma kutaḥ prītiḥ kutaḥ sukham |
nirākāraṃ nirīhañca rahitaṃ tvindriyeṇa |
janmakarmāṇi sarvāṇi brahmaṇo nāsti bhāmini ||

	prakṛteḥ santi bhāvinīti pāṭhaśca | upāsakānāṃ 
sabhaktikajñānakarmayogavatāmityarthaḥ | laiṅge �

sarveṣāmeva martyānāṃ vibhordivyavayuḥ śubham |
sakalaṃ bhāvanāyogyaṃ yogināmapi niṣphalam ||

	yogināṃ karmayogajñānayoga bhaktiyogavatāmityarthaḥ | āgneya 
purāṇe �

sādhunāmapramattānāṃ bhaktānāṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ |
upakartā nirākārastadākāreṇa jāyate |
kāryārthaṃ sādhakānāñca caturvargaphalapradaḥ ||

tathācoktaṃ mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe �

ārādhitā saiva nṛṇāṃ bhogasvargāpavargadā |

bṛhannāradīye �

bhaktānāṃ mokṣadānāya bhavato mūrtikalpanā ||

ārādhanā-lakṣaṇam

	ārādhanā tu dhyānaṃ pūjā ca | te ca bhedajñānapūrvike 
bhogasvargaprade | abhedajñānapūrvike tu muktiprade | tadarthañca 
brahmakartṛkarūpakalpanā ityarthaḥ | dhyānantu 
tattaddevatāyāstattanmantra ghaṭakībhūta tattadvarṇotpanna 
mukhahastapādādyavayavācchinnaśarīraviṣayakajñānamiti tu 
niṣkarṣārthaḥ |

tathācoktaṃ garuḍe'pi �

amūrtaścet sthiro na syāt tato mūrti vicintayet ||

dhyānadvaividhyakathanam

yāmale'pi �

sthūlasūkṣmavibhedenadhyānantu dvividhaṃ bhavet |
sūkṣmaṃ mantrapapurjñānaṃ sthūlaṃ vigrahacintanam ||

karapādodarāsyādi rūpaṃ yatsthūlavigraham |
sūkṣmañca prakṛte rūpaṃ paraṃ jñānamayaṃ smṛtam ||

sūkṣmadhyānaṃ maheśāni kadācinna hi jāyate |
sthūladhyānaṃ maheśāni kṛtvā mokṣamavāpnuyāt ||

yāmale �

devatāyāḥ śarīrantu bījādutpadyate dhruvam |
tattadvījātmakaṃ mantraṃ japtvā brahmamayo bhavet ||

adiṣṭaṃ bhāvayeddevi yathoktadhyānayogataḥ |
varṇarūpeṇa sā devī jagadādhārarūpiṇī ||

bījātvarṇāt bījaparibhāṣāmāha kulacūḍāmaṇau �

ekākṣaraṃ samuddhṛtya pūrvabījaṃ paraṃ śaktiriti |

pūrvaṃ kamiti | paramīkāraḥ | rephaḥ kīlakam gāndharve �

nityamekākṣaraṃ brahma akṣaraṃ paramaṃ padam ||

sakṛjjaptvā'kṣaraṃ mantraṃ brahmabhūyāya kalpate |
japtvā taṃ sādhayet sarvaṃ bahujāpena kiṃ phalam ||

sthūlaḥ sūkṣma eka eva | tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

ghṛtasya dvividhaṃ rūpaṃ kāṭhinyaṃ svacchatā tathā |
kāṭhinye svacchatāyāntu ghṛtameva na saṃśayaḥ ||

pādme'pi � dīpādutpadyate dīpo yathā tadvad bhaviṣyati |

	iti vacanāt | athavā pūjya-pūjakayorabhedañjñānārthaṃ 
brahmaṇorūpakalpanā | tathācoktaṃ kaurme �

manyante ye tu cātmānaṃ vibhinnaṃ parameśvarāt |
na te paśyanti taṃ devaṃ vṛthāteṣāṃ pariśramaḥ ||

īśvara ityupalakṣaṇam | tathācoktaṃ rudrayāmale �

sarvadevamayīṃ devīṃ sarvamantramayīṃ parām |
ātmānaṃ cintayeddevi paramānandarūpiṇīm ||

yoginīrūpaṇam

atha prasaṅgādyogajñānaṃ likhyate |
athā'paraṃ pravakṣyāmi samādhiṃ bhavanāśanam ||

bhavanāśanaṃ janmanāśanamityarthaḥ |

hṛtpadmakarṇikāmadhye dhyāyet siṃhaṃ manoharam |
siṃhopari sthitaṃ padmaṃ raktaṃ tasyordhvagaṃ śivam ||

tasyopari mahādevī ramate kāmarūpiṇī |
sitapreto mahādevo raktapreto'pi padmajaḥ ||

brahmā lohipaṅkaja iti vā pāṭhaḥ |

harirharastu vijñeyo vāhanāni mahaujasaḥ |
dhyāyecca parameśāni yathokta dhyānayogataḥ ||

devyātmakaṃ svamātmānaṃ bhāvayedyatamānasaḥ |
asyānya(nu)rūpaṃ yadyat tat svakīyamiti bhāvayet ||

aivayaṃ saṃbhāvayennityaṃ svagurudevatātmanām |

śrīkrame'pi �

ātmānaṃ cintayeddevi śakti mādyāsvarūpiṇīm ||

manasā vacasā caiva kāyikena ca cintayet |

anyatrāpi �

ātmā'bhedena sañcintya yāti tanmayatāṃ naraḥ ||

so'hamityasya satataṃ cintanāt tanmayo bhavet |
ahaṃ devī na cānyo'smi mukto'hamiti bhāvayet ||

rudrasya cintanād rudro viṣṇu syād viṣṇu cintanāt |
durgāyāścintanād durgā bhavatyeva na cānyathā ||

evamabhyasyamānastu ahanyahani pārvati |
jarābharaṇaduḥkhādyairmūcyate bhavabandhanāt ||

dhyānayoga-praśaṃsā

dhyānayogaparasyā'sya pūjā nāsti kathañcana ||

vinānyāsairvinā pūjāṃ vinā jāpya-puraṣkriyām |
dhyānayogadbhavet siddhirnānyathā khalu pārvati ||

etatte kathitaṃ devi brahmajñānamidaṃ mahat |
vijñāya guruto devi saṃsārasāgaraṃ taret ||

ahaṃ brahmāsmi vijñānādajñānavilayo bhavet |
so'hamityeva sañcintya viharetsarvadā priye ||

yathā phenataraṅgādi samudrādutthitaṃ mune |
samudre līyate tadvad jagadātmani līyate ||

iti gandharvatantroktayogaḥ ||

	tasmātsādhakānāṃ hitārthāya brahma strīpuṃrūpaṃ dhatte | 
viṣṇuyāmale devīṃ prati viṣṇuvacanam �

mātastvatparamaṃ rūpaṃ tanna jānātikaścana |
kālyādi sthūlaṃ yadrūpṃ tadarcanti divaukasaḥ ||

strīrūpāṃ vā smareddevīṃ puṃrūpāṃ vā smaret priye |
smared vā niṣkalaṃ brahmasaccidānandarūpiyat ||

strīrūpāvatāra-lakṣaṇam 

stananyonyādyavayavāvacchinna śarīrāḥstrīrūpāvatārāḥ | tadyathā �

kālī nīlā mahādurgā tvaritā chinnamastakā |
vāgvādinī cānnapūrṇā tathā pratyaṅgirā punaḥ ||

kāmākhyāvāsinī bālā mātaṅgī śailavāsinī |
ityādyāḥ sakalā vidyā kalau pūrṇaphalapradā ||

anyatrāpi �

umeti kecidāhusvāṃ śaktiṃ lakṣmīti cāpare |
bhāratītyapare caināṃ girijetyambiketi ca ||

durgetibhadrakālīti caṇḍīṃ māheśvarīṃ tathā |
kaumārīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ vārāhīti caindrīti cāpare ||

brāhmīti vidyāvidyeti māyeti ca tathā pare |
prakṛtiṃ cāparāṃ caiva vadanti paramārṣayaḥ ||

puruṣāvatāra-lakṣaṇam 

śiśnādyavayavāvacchinnaśarīrāvacchinnāvatārāḥ puṃrūpāḥ | yathā 
brahmā-viṣṇu-śivādayaḥ | evaṃ �

matsya kūrmo varāhaśca nṛsiṃho vāmanastathā |
rāmo rāmaśca rāmaśca buddha-kalkī ca te daśa ||

ityādi | napuṃsakaṃ gṛhasthairanupāsyameva, phalājanakatvāt |

gṛhasthānāñca sarve syurbrahma vai brahmacāriṇām |

	gṛhasthānāñca sarve syu rityupādānāt śiva-durgā-viṣṇu 
puraskāreṇa upāsanā kāryā | tathā ca vimalānandabhāṣye 
kūrmapurāṇam �

manuṣyāṇāmumā devī tathā viṣṇuḥ sadāśivaḥ |
yā yāsyā'bhimatā puṃsaḥ sāhitasyaiva devatā |
kintu kāryā'viśeṣeṇa pūjitā sveṣṭadā nṛṇām ||

	nṛṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāmabhedena pūjākāryā | śaive devīṃ prati 

īśvaravākyam �

ekaṃ praśaṃsati yastu sarvāneva praśaṃsati |
ekaṃ nindati yasteṣāṃ sarvāneva vinindati ||

īśvaranindāphalam

	īśvarasya praśaṃsāyāṃ na sukhaṃ nindāyāṃ vā na dukhaṃ, 
sukhaduḥkha rahitattvāt | kintu nindakasya narakameva | tathocoktaṃ bhāṣye �

devī-viṣṇu-śivādīnāmekattvaṃ paricintayet |
bhedakṛnnarakaṃ yāti yāvadāhūtasaṃplavam ||

āhūtasaṃplavaṃ pralayakāla-paryantamityarthaḥ | vārāhe �

yathā durgā tathā viṣṇuryathā viṣṇustathā śivaḥ |
etaplayamekameva na pṛthag bhāveyet sudhīḥ ||

yo'nyathā bhāvayedetān pakṣapātena mūḍhadhīḥ |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ raukhaṃ pāpapuruṣaḥ ||

yāmale �

dhyānagamyaṃ prapaśyantirucibhedātpṛthag vidham |

tantre �

ekaiva himahāmāyā nāmabhedaṃ samāśritā |
vimohanāya lokānāṃ tasmāt samamanā bhavet ||

pravṛttimārgasaṅgastu dīkṣābhedena pūjayet |
nivṛttiṃ mārgamāṇastu bhedavādaṃ vivarjayet ||

śaktyupāsanā-praśaṃsā

	śivaviṣṇorupāsanāṃ tyaktvā devyā upāsanā kartavyā, 
komalāntaḥ karaṇatvāt bhuktimuktidātṛtvācca | śiva-
viṣṇurupāsanāyāṃ kāyakleśena muktimātram | tathā ca śāradāyāṃ 
bhuvaneśvarīṃ prati śivavākyam �

ādyāpyaśeṣajagatāṃ navayauvanāsi
śailādhirājatanayāpyatikomalāsi

samayātantre � kadācit kasyamuktiḥ syāt kasyacid muktireva ca |

etasyāḥ sādhakasyātha bhuktimuktiḥ kare sthitā |

rudrayāmale �

yatrāsti bhogo naca tatra mokṣo
	yatrāsti mokṣo na ca tatra bhogaḥ |
śivāpadāmbhojayugārcakānāṃ
	bhogaśca mokṣaśca kastha eva |

yo'nyebhyo darśanebhyaśca bhuktiṃ muktiñca kāṃkṣati |
svapnalabdhadhanenaiva dhanavān kiṃ bhavennaraḥ ||

śuktau rajatavibhrāntiryathā jāyeta pārvati |
athānyadarśanebhyaśca bhuktiṃ muktiñca kāṃkṣataḥ ||

iti śrīparivrājakācārya paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūta śrīmadbrahmānanda 
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāmabhedayoganirṇayo nāma 
tṛtīyollāsaḥ || 3 ||




caturthollāsaḥ

atha prātaḥkṛtyam

yāmale �

prātaḥkṛtyamakṛtvā tuyo devīṃ bhaktito'rcayet |
tasya pūjā ca viphalā  śaucahīnā yathā kriyā ||

brāhye muhūrte cotthāya cintayed gurudaivatam |
svamūrddhani sahasrāre śivākhya-parabinduke ||

brāhyamuhūrtastu rātrerupāntyo muhūrtaḥ | tathā ca yāmale �

dvau daṇḍaurātriśeṣe tu brāhyaṃ muhūrtakaṃ viduḥ ||

śrīgurudhyānam

gurordhyānaṃ yathā �

śaśāṅka yutasaṅkāśaṃ varābhayalasatkaram |
śuklāmbaraparīdhānaṃ śuklamālyānulepanam ||

vāmorau raktaśaktyā cayutaṃ devākhyamavyayam |
śivenaikyaṃ samunnīya dhyāyet paraguhaṃ dhiyā ||

evaṃ dhyātvā puraścaiva pañcabhūtamayairyajet |

śrīgurumānasapūjā

gandhatattvaṃ pārthivantu kaniṣṭhāṃguliyogataḥ |

śabdamayaṃ mahāpuṣpaṃ prathamāṃguliyogataḥ |
vāyurūpaṃ mahādhūpaṃ tarjanībhyāṃ niyojayet ||

tejorūpaṃ mahādīpaṃ madhyamādvatya yogatāḥ |
amṛta bhojanaṃ tadvadamṛtāṃguli yogataḥ ||

namaskāreṇā'ñjalinā vāgbhavaṃ tāmbūlaṃ smṛtam |
svasvabījena sarvantu namaskāreṇa yojayet |
gurormantraṃ prayatnena prajapet sudavanrite ||

gurumantraḥ

gurumantro yathā �

vāṇī ca bhuvaneśānī ramā caiva sureśvari |
tāratrayamidaṃ devi gurumantre pratiṣṭhitam ||

tataḥ svaguru nāmānte cānandanāthamālikhet |
raktaśaktipadānte ca ambā padamathālikhet ||

śrīpādukāṃ samuccārya pūjayāmīti saṃjapet |
tejorūpaṃ samarpyātha stavena toṣayed gurum ||

śyāmārahasye- 

manasā gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ sampūjya vāgbhavaṃ japet |
atha kubjikā tantroktāṃ stutiṃ kuryāt ||

gurustutiḥ

oṃ namastubhyaṃ mahāmantradāyine śivarūpiṇe |
brahmajñānaprakāśāya saṃsāraduḥkhatāriṇe ||

atisaumyāya divyāya vīrāyā'jñānahāhiṇe |
namaste kulanāthāya kulakaulonyadāyine ||

śivatattvaprabodhāya brahmatattvaprakāśine |
namo'stu gurave tubhyaṃ sādhakābhayadāyine ||

anācārācārabhāva bodhāya bhāvahetave |
bhāvābhāva vinirmukta-mūrtaye gurave namaḥ ||

bhāvābhāvavinirmuktaśāktāya iti vā pāṭhyayam |

namo'stu śambhave tubhyaṃ divyabhāvaprakāśine |
jñānānandasvarūpāya vibhavāya namo namaḥ ||

śivāya śaktināthāya saccidānandarūpiṇe |
kāmarūpāya kāmāya kagmakeli kalātmane ||

kulapūjopadeśāya kulācārasvarūpiṇe |
āraktanijasacchakti vāmabhāga vibhūtaye ||

namaste'stu maheśāya vidyānāthāya saṃvide |
sarvavidyāsvarūpāya namaste'stu namo namaḥ ||

idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhennityaṃ sādhako gurudiṅmukhaḥ |
prātarutthāya deveśi atovidyā prasīdati ||

kulasambhavapūjāyāmādau yo na paṭhedidam |
viphalā tasya pūjā syādabhicārāya kalpate ||


iti kubjikā tantroktaṃ śrīgurustotraṃ samāptam |


atha kavacamapi paṭhet | tatastu praṇamet |

śrīgurupraṇāma mantraḥ

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ vyāptaṃ yena carācaram |
tatpadaṃ darśitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ ||

ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjana śalākayā |
cakṣurunmīlitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ ||

ṣaṭcakra nirūpaṇam 

atha prasaṅgāt ṣaṭcakravyavasthā likhyate |

tisraḥ koṭyastadarddhena śarīre nāḍayo matāḥ |
tāsu mukhyā daśa proktāstitrastāsūttamā matāḥ ||

pradhānā merudaṇḍāntaścandrasūryārgnirūpiṇī |
śaktirūpā tu sā nāḍī sākṣādamṛtavigrahā ||

piṅgalākhyā ca yā dakṣe puṃrūpā sūryavigrahā |
merumadhyasthitā yā tu mūlādābrahmarandhragā ||

sarvatejomayī sā tu suṣumnā vahnirūpiṇī |
dāḍimīkusumaprakhyā śivākhyā cāparā matā ||

suṣumnāntargatā citrā candrakoṭisamaprabhā |
sarvadevamayī sā tu yogināṃ hṛdayaṅgamā |
tasyā madhye brahmanāḍī mṛṇālatanturūpiṇī ||

mūlādhāracakra kathanam

suṣumnāgranthisaṃsthāni ṣaṭpadmāni yathākramam |
ādhārākhyaṃ mūlacakramatiraktaṃ caturdalam |
vādisāntārṇasaṃyuktaṃ raktavarṇaṃ manoharam ||

karṇikāyāṃ sthitā yoniḥ kāmākhyā parameśvarī |
tadyoniḥ parameśāni icchājñānakriyātmikā ||

aparākhyo hi kandarpa ādhāre tattrikoṇake |
svayambhūliṅga tanmadhye sarandhraṃ paścimānanam ||

dhyāyecca parameśāni śivaṃ śyāmala-sundaram |
kuṇḍalī tena mārgeṇa yātāyātaṃ karoti hi ||

bhitvā bhitvā purīṃ yāti cāyāti kuṇḍalī sadā |
tatra vidyullatārūpā kuṇḍalī paradevatā ||

prasuptabhujagākārā sārddhatrivalayānvitā |
vāmāvartakramerṇeva veṣṭitā viṣatantuvat ||

śivaṃ veṣṭya maheśāni sarvadā paritiṣṭhati |
yena mārgeṇa gantavyaṃ paraṃ brahma nirāmayam |
mukhenācchādya taddvāraṃ prasuptā parameśvarī ||

svādhiṣṭhāna-maṇipūracakra vivaraṇam

liṅgamūle mahāpadmaṃ svādhiṣṭhānantu ṣaḍdalam |
yādi-lāntārṇa-saṃyuktaṃ nābhau tu maṇipūrakam |
ḍādi-phāntānvita-dalairaruṇairdaśabhiryutam ||

anāhatapadma-vivaraṇam

hṛdaye dvādaśadalamanāhatasaroruham |
kādi-ṭhāntadalairdevi taptahaṭakasannibham ||

tanmadhye bāṇaliṅgantu sūryāyutasamaprabham |
śabdabrahmamayo mantro'nāhatastatra dṛśyate |
tenāhatākhyaṃ tatpadmaṃ yogibhiḥ parikīrtitam ||

viśuddhacakra-nirūpaṇam

kaṇṭhadeśe viśuddhākhyaṃ dhūmravarṇaṃ manoharam |
akārādi-svaropetairdalaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryutam ||

viśuddhistanyate yasmājjīvasya haṃsalokanāt |
viśuddhapadmamākhyātamākāśākhyaṃ mahādbhutam ||

ājñācakra-vivaraṇam

ājñānāma bhruvormadhye akrañca dvidalaṃ param |
hakṣa-dvayakṣara-saṃyuktaṃ nirmalaṃ sumanoharam ||

itarākhyaṃ mahāliṅgaṃ tanmadhye kāñcanaprabham |
ājñāsaṃkramaṇaṃ tatra gurorājñetiviśrutam |
kailāsākhyaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ tu bodhinī tu tadūrdhvataḥ ||

sahasrāracakra-vivaraṇam

sahasrāraṃ mahāpadmaṃ nādabindusamanvitam |
śūnyarūpaḥ śivaḥ sākṣād vṛttaṃ paramakuṇḍalī |
sārddhatrivalayākārā koṭividyutsamaprabhā ||

yāmale �

vṛttaṃ kuṇḍalinī śaktirguṇatrayasamanvitā |
śūnyabhāgo mahādevi śivarūpo maheśvaraḥ ||

sarpākārā śivaṃ veṣṭya sarvadā tatra saṃsthitā |
śivaśaktyātmako bindurmuktimuktiphalapradaḥ |
nādarūpeṇa sā devī yonirūpā sanātanī ||

bhūtaśuddhau �

śivaviṣṇubrahmamayī binduyoniḥ śucismite |
sarvopari maheśāni bindurbrahmasvarūpakaḥ ||

gandharvamālikāyāṃ �

bhavo binduriti khyāto bhavanañca trikoṇakam |
bhavanaṃ bhavasambandhājjāyate bhuvanatrayam ||

pañcabhūtāni deveśi ṣaṣṭhe mānasamīśvari |
ṣaṭcakreṣu sthitānyeva kramāddevi vicintayet ||

sahasāraṃ śivapuraṃ ramyaṃ duḥkhavivarjitam |
sarvato'laṃkṛtairdivyairnityapuṣpaphalairdrumaiḥ ||

sadāśivapuraṃ ramyaṃ kalpavṛkṣaṃ suśobhitam |
pañcabhūtātmakaṃ tacca guṇatrayasamanvitam ||

caturvedacatuḥśākhaṃ nityapuṣpaphalānvitam |
pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā śvetaṃ raktaṃ puṣpañca pārvati ||

haritañca vicitrañca nānāpuṣpaṃ manoharam |
evaṃ kalpadrumaṃ dhyātvā tadadho ratnavedikām ||

tatropari suparyaṅkaṃ nānāratno paśobhitam |
mandārapuṣparacitaṃ nānāgandhānumoditam ||

tatropari mahādevaḥ sadā tiṣṭhati sundari |
dhyāyeta sadāśivaṃ devaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasannibham ||

bahuratnasamākīrṇaṃ dīrghabāhu manoharam |
sukhaprasannanayanaṃ smerāsyaṃ satataṃ priye ||

śravaṇe kuṇḍalopetaṃ ratnahāreṇa śobhitam |
śoṇa(gale)padmasahasrasya mālayā śobhitaṃ vapuḥ ||

aṣṭabāhuṃ trinayanaṃ śivaṃ padmadalekṣaṇam |
pādayornūpuraṃ ramyaṃ śabdabrahmamayaṃ vapuḥ ||

evaṃ sthūlavapustasya bhavayet kamalekṣaṇe |
padmamadhye sthitaṃ devaṃ nirīhaṃ śabdarūpiṇam ||

śabdarūpe mahādevekṛtyaṃ nāsti kadācana |
evaṃ sarveṣu cakreṣu śaktirūpaṃ vicintayet ||

brahmā rudraśca viṣṇuśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
tataḥ paraśivaścaiva ṣaṭ śivāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

śaktimāha �

viśuddhau ḍākinī devi anāhate tu rākiṇī |
lākinī maṇipūrasthā kākinī liṅgagocare ||

ādhāre śākinī devī ājñāyāṃ hākinī tathā |
yākinī brahmarandhrasthā sarvakāmaphalapradā ||

dhyāyet kuṇḍalinī devīṃ svayambhūliṅgasaṃsthitām |
śyāmāṃ sūkṣmāṃ sṛṣṭirūpāṃ sṛṣṭisthitilayātmikam |
viśvātītāṃ jñānarūpāṃ cintayedūrdhvarūpiṇīm ||

raktāmiti sundarī viṣaye jñeyam |

huṅkāravarṇasambhūtā kuṇḍalī paradevatā |
vibharti kuṇḍalī dehamātmānaṃ haṃsamantrataḥ ||

kuṇḍalinī yogaḥ

pravṛddhavahnisaṃyogamanasāmārutaiḥ saha |
ūrdhvaṃ nayet kuṇḍalinīṃ jīvātmasahitāṃ param ||

gachanti brahmarandhreṇa bhitvā granthiṃ caturdaśaḥ |
ṣaṭcakra-sandhimārgeṇa suṣumnā vartmanā tathā |
haṃsena manunā devīṃ sahasrāraṃ samānayet ||

sadāśivo mahādevo yatrāste parameśvari |
tatragatvā mahādevi kuṇḍalī paradevatā ||

devīrūpavatī kāma-samullāsavihāriṇī |
mukhāravindagandhena moditaṃ paramaṃ śivam ||

prabodhya parameśāni tatropari vaset priye |
śivasya mukhapadmaṃ hi cucumbe kuṇḍalī śive ||

sadāśivena deveśi kṣaṇamātraṃ ramet priye |
amṛtaṃ jāyate devi tatkṣaṇāt parameśvari ||

tadudbhavāmṛtaṃ devi lākṣārasa samāruṇam |
tenāmṛtena deveśi tarpayet paradevatām ||

ṣaṭcakradevatāstatra santarpyā'mṛtadhārayā |
ānayet tena mārgeṇa mūlādhāraṃ punaḥ sudhīḥ ||

yātāyātakrameṇaiva tatra kuyānmanolayam |
evamabhyasyamānastu ahanyahani pārvati ||

jarābharaṇaduḥkhādyairmucyate bhavabandhanāt |
ityuktaṃ paramo yogo yonimudrā prabandhanaḥ ||

yāmale �

kulayoṣit kulaṃ tyaktvā punareva kulaṃ viśet |
ramate seyamavyaktā punarekākinī satī ||

saṃketapaddhatyām �

piṇḍaṃ kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ padaṃ haṃsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
rūpaṃ binduriti jñeyaṃ rūpātītañca niṣphalam ||

etena ṣaṭcakrabhedakrameṇa kuṇḍalinīṃ sahasradala padme nītvā tatrastha 
sadāśivena saṅgamayyatadudbhavāmṛtena paradevatāṃ 
ṣaṭcakrasthaśivaśaktyādīṃścāplāvya so'hamiti mantreṇa punaḥ 
svasthānamānayediti tu vākyārthaḥ |

	so'miti ca mantreṇa svasthānamānayet sudhīḥ | iti yāmalavacanāt ||

devyuvāca

devadeva mahādeva sṛṣṭisthityantakārakaḥ |
mūrdhni padmaṃ sahasrāraṃ raktavarṇamadhomukham ||

tasya madhye sthitaṃ dhyāyed guruṃ śāntaṃ saśaktikam |
mūlādhāre mahāśaktiṃ kuṇḍalīrupa-dhāriṇīm ||

adhovaktra krameṇaiva sarvapadmeṣu bhāvanā |
tadā kathaṃ bhavet tatra cintanaṃ gurudevayoḥ ||

ādhāre cat sthitistatra tvadhobhāge kathaṃ bhavet |
adhovaktre sthitasyāpi cintanaṃ vā kathaṃ bhavet ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca

yathā yuktaṃ tvayā devi kathitaṃ vīravandite |
evameva tu sandeho jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

kathyate parameśāni sandehocchedakāraṇam |
tāni padmāni deveśi suṣumnāntaḥ sthitāni ca ||

paraṃbrahmasvarūpāṇi śabdabrahmamayāni ca |
tatsarvaṃ paṅkajaṃ devi sarvatomukhameva ca |
pravṛttiśca nivṛttiśca dvau bhāvajīvasaṃsthitau ||

pravṛttimārgaḥ saṃsāraḥ nivṛttiparamātmani |
pravṛttimāvacintāyāmadhovaktrāṇi cintayet ||

nivṛttiyogamārgeṣu sadaivordhvamukhāni ca |
evameva bhāvabhedādasandeho'bhijāyate ||

gṛhasthayogasādhanam

(athānyata saṃpravakṣyāmi gṛhasthānāñca sādhanam |
mūlādhāre sthitāṃ devīṃ kuṇḍalīṃ paradevatām ||

bhogakāle maheśāni ājihvāntaṃ vibhāvya ca |
śodhitāna matsyamāṃsādīn tanmukhe sthāpayed budhaḥ ||

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya juhomi kuṇḍalīmukhe |
pratigrāse maheśāni evaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

bhojanecchā bhavet tasyā nirlipto jīvasaṃjñakaḥ |)
evameva prakāreṇa ūrdhvapadmaṃ prajāyate ||

guroḥ sthitiśca cārvaṅgitadā samyak prajāyate |
bhidyate hṛdayagranthicchidyate sarvasaṃśayaḥ ||

gurvādi bhāvanāddevi tadā siddhiḥ prajāyate |
svagehe pāyasaṃ tyaktvā bhikṣāmaṭatidurmatiḥ ||

ata eva maheśāni vātulatvaṃ prajāyate |
ityetatkathitaṃ sāraṃ mama jñānavilokitam ||

prakārāntara-kuṇḍalinī-yogakathanam

atha yogaṃ pravakṣyāmi yena devamayo bhavet |
mūlapadme kuṇḍalinī yāvannidrāmitā priye ||

tāvat kiñcinna sidhyet mantrayantrārcanādikam |
svāpakālo vāmavāhaḥ prabodho dakṣiṇāvahaḥ ||

mantriṇāṃ svāpakāle tu japo'nartha phalapradaḥ |
prabodhakālaṃ jānīyādumayorapi pārvatiḥ ||

jāgartiyadi sā devi bahubhiḥ puṇyasañcayaiḥ |
tadā prasādamāyānti mantrayantrārcanādayaḥ ||

yogayogādbhavenmuktirmantrasiddhirakhaṇḍitā |
siddhe manau parāvāptiriti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ ||

jīvanamuktaśca dehānte paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt |
saṃsārottaraṇaṃ muktiryogaśabdena kathyate ||

prāṇāyāmairjapairyāgaistyaktanidrā jaganmayī |
caturdalaṃ syādādhāraṃ svādhiṣṭhānantu ṣaḍdalam |
nābhau daśadalaṃ padmaṃ sūryasaṃkhyādalaṃ hṛdi ||

kaṇṭhe syāta ṣoḍaśadalaṃ bhrūmadhye dvidalaṃ tathā |
sahasradalamākhyātaṃ brahmarandhramahāpathe ||

mātṛkākṣara saṃyuktaṃ sahasrāraṃ saroruham |
adhovaktraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ rakta kiñjilkabhūṣitam || iti ||

(sundarīviṣaye tu raktavarṇaṃ bodhyam | samayātantroktatvāt, anyathā | 
virodhāpatteḥ ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
tataḥ paraśivaścaiva ṣaṭ śivāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

ḍākinī rākinī caiva śākinī lākinī tathā |
kākinī śākinī caiva śaktayastāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

ādhāre hṛtpradeśe ca bhruvormadhye viśeṣataḥ |
svayambhūsaṃjño bāṇākhyastathaivetarasaṃjñakaḥ ||

liṅgatrayaṃ maheśāni pradhānatvena cintayet |
mūlādhāre sthitābhūmiḥ svādhiṣṭhāne jalaṃ priye ||

maṇipūre sthitaṃ tejo hṛdaye mārutastathā |
viśuddhau tu maheśāni ākāśaṃ kamalekṣaṇe ||

ājñācakre maheśāni manaḥ sarvārthasādhakam |
tadūrdhve parameśāni padmamūrdhvamukhaṃ sadā |
tasyopari maheśāni dhyāyet sadā śivaṃ gurum ||

ūrdhvamukhamiti | adhomukha sahasradala 
padmādhogaterdhvamukhadvādaśa-dala-padmopari śivarūpiṇaṃ 
śrīguruṃ dhyāyedityarthaḥ | tathā coktaṃ yāmale �

brahmarandhra sarasīruhodare nityalagnamavadātamadbhutam |
kuṇḍalīvivarakāṇḍamaṇḍitaṃ dvādaśārṇa sarasīruhaṃ bhaje ||

	ahaṃ dvādaśārṇa dvādaśadalaṃ sarasīruhaṃ padmaṃ bhaje | 
sarasīruhaṃ kiṃ viśiṣṭam ? kuṇḍalīvivara kāṇḍamaṇḍitaṃ | 
kuṇḍalyāṃ vivaraṃ mūlādhārapadmāt 
sahasradalakamalakarṇikāntargatabindurūpi-sadāśiva sannidhau 
gamanamārgaḥ, tasya kāṇḍaṃ tadādhārabhūtaṃ nālaṃ, 
citriṇīnāḍītyarthaḥ |

tasya maṇḍitaṃ padmanālavat citriṇīnāḍyāḥ śirobhūṣaṇamityarthaḥ | 
tadagre dvādaśārṇasya sarasīruhasya sthitatvāt bhūṣaṇamityuktiḥ | punaḥ 
kimbhūtam ? brahmarandhrasarasīruhodare nityalagnam, sahasrārapadma 
karṇikāsamīpe tadadhodeśe nityamavinābhāva sambandhena lagnaṃ 
sthitamityarthaḥ | punaḥ kimbhūtam ? avadaptaṃ gauram | punaḥ kimbhūtam 
? adbhutaṃ manoharam | tatra pūrvokta krameṇa śivarūpiṇaṃ śrīguraṃ 
dhyāyet ||

ṣaṭcakraṃ parameśāni | sadāśivapuraṃ samam |
śaktipuraṃ maheśāni sadāśivapuropari ||

sa eva nirvāṇākhya kaloparigataḥ nirvāṇaśakteḥ puram |

śivasthānaṃ śaivāḥ paramapuruṣaṃ vaiṣṇavagaṇā
lapantīti prāyo hariharapadaṃ kecidapare ||

padaṃ devyā devīcaraṇayugalānandarasikā |
munīndrā apyanye prakṛti puruṣasthānamamalam ||

kuṇḍalinī pratyāvartana prakāraḥ

ramitvā śambhunā sārddhaṃ kuṇḍalīṃ paradevatām |
mūlādhāraṃ maheśāni sahasrārāt samānayet ||

śambhunā tāṃ parāṃ śaktimekībhūtāṃ vicintayet |
dhyāyet kuṇḍalinīṃ tatra iṣṭadevasvarūpiṇīm ||

sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām |
navayauvanasampannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ||

pūrṇacandranibhāṃ raktāṃ sadā cañcalalocanām |
nānāratnayutāṃ dhanyāṃ pāde nūpuraśobhitām ||

kiṅkinī ca tathā kaṭyāṃ ratnakaṅkaṇamaṇḍitām |
kandarpakoṭi-lāvaṇyāṃ sadā madhurahāsinīm ||

evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ śive |
mātṛkāmālayā japtvā ājñācakraṃ samānayet ||

tatretareṇa liṅgena yojayet kuṇḍalīṃ parām |
dhyātvā brahmamayīṃ tatra śatamaṣṭotaraṃ japet ||

tato viśudau tāṃ nītvā śivena saha yojayet |
tāmiṣṭadevatāṃ dhyātvā japedaṣṭaśataṃ priye ||

hṛtpadme tāṃ tato nītvā bāṇena saha yojayet |
devīrūpāñca tāṃ dhyātvā japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam ||

maṇipūre tu tāṃ nītvā śivena saha yojayet |
devīrūpāṃ ca tāṃ dhyātvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet ||

svādhiṣṭhāne tato nītvā śivena saha yojayet |
yojayitvā japenmantraṃ devīṃ dhyātvā priyaṃ vade ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā mūdhādhāre tu tāṃ nayet |
tatra liṅgaṃ svayambhūñca dhyāyetkundasamaprabham ||

śuklavarṇaṃ caturbāhuṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam |
nānāratnayutaṃ ramyaṃ sārddhatrivalayānvitam ||

prasannavadanaṃ śāntaṃ nīlakaṇṭhavirājitam |
kapardinaṃ sphuratsarvabhūṣaṃ kundasamaprabham ||

ṣaṭcakre parameśāni dhyāyejjaganmayīṃ śivām |
bhujaṅgarūpiṇīṃ devīṃ nityāṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ parām ||

viṣayantumayīṃ devīṃ sākṣādamṛtarūpiṇīm |
avyaktarūpiṇīṃ divyāṃ dhyānagamyāṃ varānane ||

dhyātvā japtvā ca deveśi sākṣād brahmamayo bhavet |
evaṃ dvādaśadhā devi yātāyātaṃ karoti yaḥ ||

sa muktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo mantrasiddho na cānyathā |
yatra tatra mṛtaścāyaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ śvapacālaye |
brahmavid brahmabhūyāya kalpate nānyathā priye ||

atha prārthanā �

ahaṃ devi na cānyo'smi brahmaivāhaṃ na śokabhāk |
saccidānandarūpo'haṃ nityamuktaḥ svabhāvavān ||

hṛdisthayā paradevyā preritaśca karomyaham |
na me kiñcid kvacidvāpi kṛtyamasti jagattrayaye ||

jānāni dharmaṃ na ca me pravṛttijānāmyadharmaṃ na ca me nivṛttiḥ | 
tvayā mahādevi hṛdisthāyā'haṃ yathā yathāniyukto'smi tathā karomi ||

trailokya caitanyamayi triśakte śrīpārvati tvaccaraṇājñāyaiva |
prātaḥ samutthāya tava priyārthaṃ saṃsārayātrāmanuvartayiṣye ||

evaṃ sañcintya manasā gṛhānnirgatya saṃyataḥ |
ācamya prayatomantrī dantadhāvanamācaret ||

dantadhāvanavidhiḥ

tantragandharve �

dantakāṣṭhamakhāditvā pūjayedyastu devatām |
tatpūjā viphalā devi mṛte ca narakaṃ vrajet ||

mantratantraprakāśe �

vidhāyāvaśyakaṃ śaucamācāmaṃ dantadhāvanam |
mukhaprakṣālanādīni kṛtvā snānaṃ samācaret ||

	atha mukhaprakṣālanamantraḥ dakṣiṇāmūrtau � klīṃ 
kāmadevasarvajanapriyāya namaḥ |

klīmātmakaṃ kāmadeva-sarvajanamathālikhet |

priyāya hṛdayānto'yaṃ manurdantaviśuddhaye |
caturdaśākṣarairvaktraṃ kṣālayet siddhihetave ||

snānavidhiḥ

yāmale �

snānamūlāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ śrutismṛtyuditā nṛṇām |
tasmāt snānaṃ niṣeveta śrīpuṣṭyārogyavarddhanam ||

mantratantraprakāśe �

aruṇe'nuditemantrī tīrthe vā vimale jale |
vaidikaṃ snānamācarya tāntrikaṃ snānamācaret ||

parakhāte yatkartavyaṃ tadāha viśvasāre �

parakhāte tu kartavyaḥ pañcapiṇḍoddhāraḥ sadā ||

snānamantraḥ

mantramāha �

uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha paṅka tvaṃ tyaja puṇyaṃ parasya ca |
pāpāni vilayaṃ yāntu śāntiṃ dehi sadā mama ||

nīlatantre �

punarnimajya payasi saṃkalpañca samācaret |
tataḥ saṅkalpya matimānnābhimātrodake sthitaḥ ||

prītaye sveṣṭadevasya snānaṃ sarvatra kārayet |
iṣṭadevatāpūjārthaṃ snānaṃ kāryaṃ jalāśaye ||

mantratantraprakāśe �

astreṇānīya mṛtsnāṃ vai tribhāgaṃ tatra kārayet |
bhāgamekaṃ jale caiva kṣipenmantraṃ samuccaran ||

evaṃ mūddhādi nābhyantaraṃ paṭhan mūlaṃ vilepayet |
śeṣantu pādanābhyantaṃ tathaiva parilepayet ||

aṅge ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vinyasya prāṇāyāmapuraḥsaram |
hṛnmantrāṃ kuśamudrābhyāṃ tīthamāvāhya maṇḍalāt ||

maṇḍalāt sūryamaṇḍalāt ||

tīrthāvāhanamantraḥ

brahmāṇḍeyāni tīrthāni karaiḥ spṛṣṭāni terave |
tena satyena me deva tīrthaṃ dehi divākara ||

oṃ gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati |
narmade sindhu kāveri jale'smin sannidhiṃ kuru ||

oṃ āvāhayāmi devi tvāṃ snānārthamiha sundari |
ehi gaṅge namastubhyaṃ sarvatīrtha samanvite ||

evamāvāhya vidhivan mūlamantreṇa mantrayet |
āmantryā'mbhasi saṃyojya somasūryāgnimaṇḍalam ||

vicintya mantrī tanmadhye nimajjen mūlamuccaran |
utthāyācamya tatpaścāt ṣaḍaṅganyāsa saṃyutaḥ ||

acāmana mantraḥ

yāmale �

ātmavidyā śivaistattvairācāmet sādhakāgraṇīḥ |

vahnijāyāṃ pare dattvā śuddhena pāthasā priye | pāthasā jalena |

abhimantrya tatastoyaṃ mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ |
kṣālayet tena vapuṣaḥ kaluṣaṃ kumbhamudrayā ||

ātmānaṃ daśadhā sinñcen mudrayā kalaśākhyayā |
saptakṛtvo'bhiṣiñcedvā manunā mantritairjalaiḥ ||

jñānārṇave �

vāmahaste kṛtā muṣṭirdakṣahastasya pārvati |
kalaśākhyā bhavenmudrā sarvapāpaharā śubhā ||

gautamīye �

pīḍayitvā'mbaraṃ cāru prakṣālyācamya vāgyataḥ |
dhārayedvāsasī śuddhe parīdhānottarīyake ||

acchinne sadaśe śukle ācāmet pīṭhasaṃsthitaḥ |
mokṣārthī raktavastre dve bhogārthī śvetavāsasī ||

śāktatilakavidhiḥ

tilakaṃ raktagandhena candanena ca vā priye |
devyastraṃ vilikhedbhāle tārābījaṃ tato hṛdi |
śaktiṃ madhyagatāṃ kuryāt sādhako narapuṅgavaḥ ||

devyastraṃ svasvopāsita devyastraṃ likhedityarthaḥ |

tripuṇḍreṇavinā kuryād yāṃ kāñcidvaidikī kriyām |
sā niṣphalā bhaved bhūpa brahmaṇapi kṛtā yadi ||

iti bhaviṣyapurāṇavacanāt | kūrmapurāṇe �

vaiṣṇavo vātha śaivā vā śākto vā saura eva vā |
tripuṇḍreṇa vinā pūjāṃ kurvāṇo yātyadhogatim ||

śivadharme �

sitena bhasmanā kuryāllalāṭe yastripuṇḍrakam |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate ||

	bhasmetyupalakṣaṇam, dravyāntareṇāpi tripuṇḍraṃ kāryam | tathā 
ca bhaviṣye �

sarvastripuṇḍrakaṃ kuryādyajñasya bhasmanā sadā |
tadalābhe candanena mṛdā vā vāriṇāpi vā ||

yatkiñcit kurute karma vinā viprastripuṇḍrakam |
vyarthameva bhavetsarvaṃ bandhyāstrīsaṅgamo yathā ||

sacchidraṃ kurute yastu puṇḍraṃ paśumatirdvijaḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu tasya chidraṃ prajāyate ||

vaidikīṃ tāntrikīṃ sandhyāṃ tataḥ kuryāt samāhitaḥ |
aśūnyaṃ ca karaṃ kuyātsuvarṇarajataiḥ kaśaiḥ ||

suvarṇarajatañcaiva japapūjādi karmasu |
eṣa eva kuśaḥ śākte na darbho vanasambhavaḥ |
tarjanyāṃ rajataṃ dhāryaṃ svarṇaṃ dhāryamanāmayā ||

tāntrika-sandhyā

atha sandhyā pravakṣyāmi tāntrikīṃ sarvasiddhidām |
upaviśyācamenmantrī payomiharnībudbudaiḥ ||

tataśca ātmatattvāya vidyātattvāya tatparam |
śivatattvāya vai procya krameṇa vahnivallabhām ||

mūlāntairebhirācāmet pūrvottaramukhaḥ sudhīḥ |
ācamanaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret ||

ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācarya jale mūlaṃ japed daśa |
kuśena tajjalaṃ bhūmau trirmūrdhni sapta nikṣipet ||

mūlamuccārya deveśi vāmahaste jalaṃ tataḥ |
gṛhītvā tajjalaṃ devi tatra mūlaṃ samuccaran ||

śivavāyujalapṛthvīvahnibījaistridhā punaḥ |
abhimantrya ca mūlena saptadhā tattvamudrayā ||

galitāmbu kṣipenmūrdhni śeṣaṃ dakṣe nidhāya ca |
iḍayākṛṣya dehāntaḥ kṣālitaiḥ pāpasañcayaiḥ ||

kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tadudakaṃ dakṣanāḍayā virecayet |
dakṣahaste tu tanmantrī pāparūpaṃ vicintya ca |
purato vajrapāṣāṇe prakṣipedastramantrataḥ ||

tarpaṇavidhiḥ

jale yantraṃ samālikhya tapayet paradevatām |
pūjayitvā tatra devīṃ parivāra-samanvitām ||

gurupaṃktīḥ pratarpyātha tarpayediṣṭadevatām |
uttābhimukho bhūtvā devīmātraṃ pratarpayet ||

tṛpyatāṃ jagatāṃ mātā bhairavastṛpyatāṃ tathā |
mūlānte nāma coccārya tarpayāmi tataḥ param ||

svāhānte tarpaṇaṃ tvevaṃ pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
tarpaṇañca prakurvīta dvitīyāntamathoccaran ||

pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyātaṃ daśadhā vā tridhāpi vā |
ekaikāñjalitoyena parivārāṃstu tarpayet ||

sūryārghadānam

dineśāya kṣipettiṣṭhanvāriṇā cāñjalitrayam |
sūryamantraṃ samuccarya oṃ hrīṃ haṃsa ityapi ||

mārtaṇḍabhairavāyeti prakāśaśaktisaṃyutam |
ṅentaṃ samuccārya graharāśiyutāya ṭhadvayam ||

tridhāñjaliṃ kṣipenmantrī siddhaye sāṅkakarmaṇām |
toyāñjaliṃ tataḥ kṣiptvā sūryamaṇḍalamadhyagām ||

ādityamaṇḍale devīṃ dhyāyet sūryasvarūpiṇīm |
tattadgāyatrīmuccārya visṛjedanayā'rghakam ||

gāyatrīṃ bhāvayeddevīṃ sūyāsanakṛtāśrayām |
prātarmadhyāhna sāyāhne dhyānaṃ kṛtvā japetsudhīḥ ||

dhyānaṃ kṛtveti | bījatrayarūpāṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ dhyātvā japedityarthaḥ ||

kuṇḍalinīdhyānam

kuṇḍalinīṃ tridhā devi tathā bījatrayaṃ tridhā |
tṛtīyāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ mūrdhni nityānanda svarūpiṇīm ||

vāgbhavaṃ mūladeśe ca dravasvarṇanibhaṃ smaret |
vahnikuṇḍalinīṃ nityāṃ bālārkasadṛśāruṇām ||

hṛdaye kāmabījañca sūryakoṭisamaprabham |
sūryakuṇḍalinīṃ tatra nityānandasvarūpiṇīm ||

bhrūmadhye śaktibījañca koṭicandrasamaprabham |
candrakuṇḍalinīṃ tatra sravadamṛtavigrahām ||

bījatrayamayīṃ bindau turyāṃ bindutrayātmikām |
tūryakuṇḍalinīṃ devi kevalāṃ jñānavigrahām ||

prātaḥkāle mūlādhāre �

bālārkamaṇḍalābhāsāṃ bhānuvahnīndulocanām |
pāśāṃkuśau śarāṃścāpaṃ dhārayantīṃ śivāṃ smaret ||

madhyāhne hṛtpadme �

madhyāhne cintayeddevīṃ navayauvana śobhitām |

sāyāhne bhrūmadhye �

sāyahni cintayeddevīṃ trailokyaikaprabhāmayīm ||

navayauvanasampannāmujvalāṃ paramāṃ kalām |

rātrau sahasrāre �

tāmeva cintayedrātrau bhogī bhogaparāyaṇām ||

gāyatrījapavidhiḥ

gāyatrīṃ prajaped vidvānaṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
manasā prajapenmantraṃ gāyatrīñca viśeṣataḥ ||

gāndharve �

gāyantaṃ gāyate yasmād gāyatrī tena cocyate |
mahāpātakayukto'pi prajapeddaśadhā yadi ||

satyaṃ satyaṃ mahādevi mukto bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
aṣṭottara śatāvṛtyā gāyatrīṃ japate yadi ||

sarvapāpavinirmukto bhavet pūjādhikāravān |
aṣṭottara śatāvṛttyā mūlamantrañca saṃjapet ||

ārdravastreṇa yatkartavyaṃ tadāha yāmale �

nābhimātrodake sthitvā devīmarkagatāṃ smaran |
japedaṣṭottaraśataṃ labhate mahatīṃ śriyam ||

saṃhāramudrayā caiva tīrthamudvāsya vāgyataḥ |
tatomaunī viśuddhātmā hṛdi vidyāṃ parāmṛśan ||

avahirmānaso bhūtvā yāgabhūmimathāviśet |
śaktisandhyā mayā proktā kartavyā sādhakottamaiḥ |
sandhyāyāṃ patitāyāṃ tu gāyatrīṃ daśadhā japet ||

sandhyāñca trikālaṃ kuryāt | tathā śaivāgame �

prātarmadhyāhne sāyāhne sandhyāṃ kuryācca mantravit |

sandhyāyāsttvakaraṇe doṣamāha ca lakṣmīkulārṇave �

sandhyāṃ tu vihīno yo na dīkṣāphalamāpnuyāt ||

tāntrika sandhyāyāṃ śūdrasyāpyadhikāraḥ | viśuddheśvare �

sandhyātrayaṃ sadā kuryād brāhmaṇovidhipūrvakam |
tantroktavidhi pūrvāntu śūdraḥ sandhyāṃ samācaret ||


iti śrīparivrājakācārya paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūta śrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ prātaḥkṛtyanirṇayo 
nāma caturthollāsaḥ || 4 ||




pañcamollāsaḥ

āsananirṇayaḥ

āsanasya grāhyatvamāha gaurīyāmale �

salile yadi kūrvīta devatānāṃ prapūjanam |
athāpyāsana āsīno notyitastu tathācaret ||

āsanaṃ kalpayitvā tu manasā pūjayejjale |
āsanastho japet samyaṅ nantrārthagata-mānasaḥ ||

sammohanatantre �

raktāsanopaviṣṭastu lākṣāruṇa gṛhesthitaḥ |
manaḥkalpitarakte vā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ ||

kuśa(tūla) kambalavastrāṇi siṃhavyāghramṛgājinam |
kalpayedāsanaṃ dhīmān saubhāgyajñānavarddhanam ||

kauśeyaṃ vāthacārmaṃ vā cailataulamathāpi vā |
śarapatraṃ tālapatraṃ kambalaṃ darbhamā(dāra vā) sanam ||

kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhirmuktiḥ syādvyāghracarmaṇi |
kṛṣṇājine gṛhasthānāṃ nādhikāraḥ kathañcana ||

nādīkṣito viśejjātu kṛṣṇasārājine gṛhī |
viśedyatirvanasthaśca brahmacārī tu bhikṣukaḥ ||

vastrāsane vyādhināśaḥ kambale duḥkhanāśanam |
japadhyānatapohānirvastrāsanaṃ karoti yaḥ ||

atra �

vastraniṣedhaḥ kevalavastrāsanaparaḥ anyathā virodhāpatteḥ |
kuśāsane bhavedāyurmokṣaḥ syādvyāghracarmaṇi ||

ajine ca bhavet putrī kambale siddhiruttamā |
śāntike dhavalaḥ proktaḥ sarvāthaścitra-kambale ||

syātpauṣṭike tu kauśeyaṃ kambale duḥkhamocanam |
tripurāpūjane śastaṃ raktakambalamāsanam ||

naitad dvihastato dīrghaṃ sārddhahastānna vistṛtam |
na tryaṃgulāt samucchrāyaṃ pūjākarmaṇi saṃgrahe |
āsanañca tataḥ kuryānnātinīcaṃ na cocchritam ||

tantragandharve -

dhāraṇyāṃ duḥkhasambhūtidaurbhāgyaṃ dārujāsane ||

āmranimbakadambānāmāsanaṃ vaṃśanāśanam |
bakulekiṃśuke caiva panase ca hataśrīkaḥ ||

vaṃśeṣṭakāśmadharaṇītṛṇapallavanirmitam |
varjayedāsanaṃ mantrī dāridryavyādhiduḥkhadam ||

dārujāsanaṃ viśeṣayati gandharvatantre �

gāmbhārīnirmitaṃ śastaṃ nānyaddārumayaṃ śubham |
caturviṃśāṃgulaṃ dīrghaṃ kuryātkāṣṭhāśanaṃ śive ||

ṣoḍaśāṃgulavistīrṇamucchrāye caturaṃgulam |
kambalaṃ carmajaṃ cailaṃ mahāmāyā prapūjane ||

praśastamāsanaṃ proktaṃ kāmākhyāyāstathaiva ca |
tripurāyāśca rudrasya viṣṇoścāpi kuśāsanam ||

tṛṇāsane yaśohāniḥ pallave cittavibhramaḥ |
yathoktamāsanaṃ kuryāt sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
na yatheṣṭāsano bhūyāt pūjākarmaṇi sādhakaḥ ||

anyatra �

vaṃśāśmadharaṇīdārutṛṇapallavanirmitam |
varjayedāsanaṃ dhīmāndāridryavyādhiduḥkhadam ||

tantre �

kāṣṭhāsane bhavedrogī vaṃśe vaṃśakṣayo bhavet |
śailāsane ca vāgarodhaḥ pallave mativabhramaḥ ||

anyatrāpi �

dharaṇyāṃ śokasaṃyuktaḥ kāṣṭhe vyarthaśramo bhavet ||

padmāsanaṃ svastikākhyaṃ bhadraṃ vajrāsanaṃ tathā |
vīrāsanamiti proktaṃ kramādāsanapañcakam ||

padmāsanādilakṣaṇam

savyaṃ pādamupādāya dakṣopari nyasettataḥ |
tathaiva dakṣiṇaṃ savyasyopari ca vidhānavit ||

viṣṭamya koṭyāḥ pārṣṇī tu nāsāgranyastalocanaḥ |
padmāsanamidaṃ proktaṃ japakarmasu śasyate ||

jānūrvorantare samyakkṛtvā pādatale ubhe |
ṛjukāyo viśenmantrī svastikaṃ tatpracakṣate ||

sīvanyāḥ pārśvayornyasya gulphayugmaṃ suniścalam |
vṛṣaṇādhaḥ pādapārśvau pāṇibhyāṃ paribandhayet ||

bhadrāsanaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ munibhiḥ parikalpitam |
ūrvoḥ pādau kramānnyasya jānvoḥ pratyaṅmukhāṃgulī ||

karau nidadhyādākhyātaṃ vajrāsanamanuttamam |
ekapādamadhaḥ kṛtvā vinyasyorau tathetaram |
ṛjukāyo viśenmantri vīrāsanamitīritam ||

nityanaimittikapūjākathanam

rudrayāmale �

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ pūjanaṃ smṛtam |

nīlatantre �

nityasevārato mantrī kuryānnaimittikārcanam ||

nityapūjāṃ divā kuryāt rātrau naimittikārcanam |
ubhayoḥ kāmyakarmāṇi ceti śastrasya niścayaḥ ||

rudrayāmale �

rātrau pūjāṃ sadā kuryāt rātrau siddhirna saṃśayaḥ |
saphalā rajanīpūjā divāpūjā ca niṣphalā ||

śaktimantraṃ japedrātrau vināpi pūjanaṃ śuciḥ |
viśeṣato niśīthe tu tatrā'tiphalado japaḥ ||

bṛhattoḍalatantre �

niśāyāṃ yo'rcayetkālīṃ tārāñca bhairavīṃ tathā |
āsamudrakṣitīśānāṃ śreṣṭho bhavati sarvathā ||

anyatrāpi �

mātaṅgīñca tathā bālāṃ cāmuṇḍāṃ chinnamastakām |
bhadrakālīñca durgāñca jayadurgāṃ tathaiva ca ||

āsāṃ japaśca pūjā ca rātrau cet kriyate sadā |
bhuktvā bhogānaśeṣāṃśca so'vaśyaṃ yāti rudratām ||

samayātantre �

divā prapūjanaṃ devi yathoktaphaladaṃ bhavet |
pūjanaṃ lakṣaṇa-guṇitaṃ niśi nīrajalocane ||

ardharātrātparaṃ yacca muhūrtadvayameva hi |
sā mahārātriruddiṣṭā kṛtā tatrā'kṣayā bhavet ||

tantre �

gate tu prathame yāme tṛtīyaṃ praharāvadhi |
niśāyāñca prajaptavyaṃ rātriśeṣe japenna hi ||

prakaṭe śaktimantre ca hāniḥ syāduttarottaram |
paśusannidhimāsādya nityapūjāñca varjayet ||

paśoragre kṛtāyā tu pramādānniṣphalā bhavet |
nijasādhakamadhye tu na goptavyā kadācana ||

samayāmantre �

strīsamīpe kṛtāpūjā japaśca parameśvari |
kāmarūpācchataguṇāvavyayau samudīritau ||


iti śrīparivrājakācārya 
paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānandagirikṛtāyāṃ 
śāktānandataraṅgiṇyā māsananirṇayo nāma pañcamollāsaḥ || 5 ||




ṣaṣṭhollāsaḥ

antaryāgavidhiḥ

ātmasthāṃ devatāṃ tyaktvā vahirdevaṃ vicintayet |
karasthaṃ kaustubhaṃ tyaktvā bhramante kācatṛṣṇayā ||

pratyakṣīkṛtya hṛdaye vahisthāṃ pūjayecchivām |
yasya yasya ca devasya yathā bhūṣaṇavāhanam ||

tadeva pūjane tasya cintayet parameśvari |
athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye yena devamayo bhavet ||

sukhāsane samāsīnaḥ prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ ||

svakīya hṛdaye dhyāyetsudhāsāgara muttamam |

ratnadvīpañca tanmadhye suvarṇavālukāmayam ||

mandārapārijātādyaiḥ kalpavṛkṣaiḥ supuṣpitaiḥ |
sarvato'laṃkṛtaṃ divyairnityapuṣpaphalairdramaiḥ ||

nānāsugandhikusuma gandhāmoditadiṅmukham |
utphullakusumāmoda prahṛṣṭabhṛṅgasaṃkulam ||

kūjatkokilasaṃghena vācālitadigantaram |
sarvato'laṃkṛtaṃ divyairlasatkāñcanapaṅkajaiḥ |
mauktikaiḥ kusumaiḥ sragbhirdukūlaiḥ svarṇatoraṇaiḥ ||

tanmadhye saṃsmareddevi kalpavṛkṣaṃ manoharam |
caturvedacatuḥśākhaṃ guṇatrayasamanvitam ||

pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā śvetaṃ raktaṃ puṣpañca sundari |
haritañca vicitrañca nānāpuṣpairvirājitam ||

kokilairbhramarairdevi śobhitaṃ bahupakṣibhiḥ |
evaṃ kalpadrumaṃ dhyātvā tadadhoratnavedikām ||

tatropari mahadvyāptaṃ cintayedratnamaṇḍapam |
udyadādityasaṃkāśaṃ ratnasopānamaṇḍitam ||

dhvajāvalīsamākīrṇaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam |
nānāratnādiśobhāḍhyaṃ ratnaprākāramaṇḍitam ||

svasvasthānasthitaistaistairlokapālairadhiṣṭhitam |
middhacāraṇagandharvairvidyādharamahoragaiḥ ||

kinnarairapsarobhiśca krīḍadbhiḥ parivāritam |
nṛtyavāditraniratairamarastrīgaṇairyutam ||

kiṅkinījālasannaddhapatākābhiralaṃkṛtam |
mahāmāṇikyavaidūrya ratnacāmara bhūṣitam ||

sthūlamuktāphalonnaddhavitānasamalaṃkṛtam |
candanāgurukāśmīramṛganābhivilepitam ||

tanmadhye saṃsmareddevi mahāmāṇikyavedikām |
udyadarkendukiraṇāṃ catuṣkoṇa praśobhitām |
dhyāyetsiṃhāsanaṃ tatra brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam |
siṃhāsane maheśāni prasūnatulikāṃ nyaset ||

pīṭhapūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāt svakalpoktakrameṇa tu |
pretapadmāsane tatra cintayetparameśvarīm ||

ātmano'bhīṣṭadevatā dhyānaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ |

śrīratnapaduke dattvā nītvā tāṃ snānamandire |
siṃhāsanopaviṣṭhāyāmudvartanaṃ samācaret ||

karpūrāgurukastūryā tathā mṛgamadena ca |
kuṃkumarocanācūṇairnānāgandhasamanvitaiḥ ||

devyāṃ udvarjanaṃ kṛtvā gandhatailaṃ vilepayet |
devīnāṃ śatasāhasraiḥ svarṇakumbhasahasrakaiḥ ||

ānīya vāriṇā snātāṃ cintayetparadevatām |
dukūlairmājitaṃ gātraṃ dukūle paridhāpya ca ||

kaṅkatyā keśaṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivad bandhanaṃ caret |
paṭṭagucchaṃ keśapāśe nānāratnopaśobhitam ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ dadyāt sindūraṃ keśamdhyake |
nāgendradantakhacitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ dadyānmanoharam ||

haste keyūrakañcaiva kaṅkaṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ tathā |
svarṇāṃgurīyakaṃ dadyānnātāratnopaśobhitam ||

pādayornūpuraṃ dadyānāsāgre gajamauktikam |
nivedayedyathā śaktyā puṣpamālāñca bhūṣaṇam ||

sarvāṅge lepanaṃ kuryād gandhacandanasihlakaiḥ |
kāñcanāñcitakañculīśobhitāṃ hṛdayopari ||

samādhau cintayeddevīṃ bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ caret |
nyāsajālaṃ vidhāyātha samādhau pūjayetsadā ||

ṣoḍaśairupacāraistu hṛdisthāṃ pūjayecchivām |
ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ dadyātsvāgataṃ kuśalaṃ vadet ||

pādyañca pādayordevi śivasyarghyaṃ nivedayet |
parāmṛtācamanaṃ pradadyānmukhapaṅkaje ||

madhuparkaṃ mukhe dadyāt tridhā tvācamanaṃ mukhe |
hemapātragataṃ divyaṃ paramānnaṃ pariṣkṛtam ||

kapilādhatasaṃyuktamannaṃ vyañjanasaṃyutam |
(sudhāmbudhiṃ māṃsaśailaṃ matsyarāśiṃ phalāni ca ||)

bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā lehyaṃ carvyaṃ cūṣyaṃ tathaiva ca |
sakarpūrañca tāmbūlaṃ mānasaṃ parikalpayet ||

āvaraṇaṃ tato devyāḥ pūjayenmanasaiva hi |
itthamantaḥ samārādhyamanasaiva japenmanum ||

sahasrādi japaṃ kṛtvā devyai sodakamarpayet |
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ ||

eta eva mahādevyāḥ paryaṅkaṃ samudāhṛtam ||

payaḥ phenanibhāṃ śayyāṃ nānāpuṣpopaśobhitām |
puṣpaśayyāñca saṃskuryāt tatra devīṃ sureśvarīm ||

cintayet sādhako yogī nānāsukhavilāsinīm |
nṛtyagītaiḥ savādyaśca toṣayetparameśvarīm ||

tato homaṃ prakurvīta pūjāsārthakyatave ||

atha homaṃ pravakṣyāmi yena cinmayatāṃ labhet |
athādhāramaye kuṇḍe cidagnau homayettataḥ ||

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
etadrūpantu citkuṇḍaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvayet ||

ānandamekhalārabhyāṃ bindutrivalayāṅkitam |
arddhamātrāyonirūpaṃ brahmānandamaya bhavet ||

nāḍīmiḍāṃ vāmabhāge dakṣiṇe piṅgalāṃ punaḥ |
suṣumnāṃ madhyatodhyātvā kuryāddhobhaṃ yathāvidhi ||

dharmādhamau sādhakendro haviṣṭvena prakalpayet |
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tataḥ ślokaṃ japedamum ||

nābhau caitanyarūpāgnau haviṣā manasā srucā |
jñāna pradīpite nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dadyācca prathamāhutim |
mūlamantropari ślokamaparaṃ prapaṭhedamum ||

dharmādharmahavirdīpti ātmāgnau manasā srucā |
suṣumnā vartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dvitīyāhutimādiśet |
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tataḥ ślokaṃ japedamum ||

prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīsrucā |
jñāna pradīpite nityamakṣavṛtīrjuhomyaham ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa tṛtīyāhutimācaret |
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tataḥ ślokaṃ japedamum ||

	antarnirantaramanindhana medhamānemohāndhakāraparipanthini 
samvidagnau |

kasmiṃścidadbhutamarīcivikāśabhūmau viśvaṃ juhomi 
vasudhādiśivāvasānam ||

svāhāntonā'munā hutvā pūrṇāhutiranantaram |
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya ślokametamudīrayet ||

aha(ida)ntāpātrabharitamida (aha)ntā paramāmṛtam |
parāhantāmaye vahnau pūrṇahomaṃ juhomyaham ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dadyātpañca (pūrṇā)hutiṃ priye |
ityantaryajanaṃ kṛtvā sākṣād brahmamayo bhavet ||

evameva pūjayanti gṛhasthāḥ parameśvarīm |
yogino munayaścaiva pūjayanti sadāpriye ||

kevalaṃ mānasenaiva nahi siddho bhavet gṛhī |
savāhyena tu tenaiva siddho bhavati yad gṛhī ||

bhūtaśuddhau �

sarvāsu vāhyapūjāsu antaḥpūjā vidhīyate |
ataḥ pūjāṃ vinā naiva vāhyapūjāphalaṃ labhet ||

sāntaḥpūjā maheśāni vāhyā koṭiphalapradā |
kiṃ tasya vāhyapūjāyāṃ sarvaṃ vyarthaṃ kadarthanam ||

upacārādyabhāve ca vāhyapūjā kadarthanam |
vinopacārairyā pūjā sā pūjā niṣphalā bhavet ||

tantrāntare �

yadi vāhyārcanā dravyasampattirapi vartate |
antaryāgaṃ vidhāyetthaṃ vahiyagividhiṃ caret ||

yāmale �

arcābhāve maheśāni hṛdaye pūjayecchivām |

sarvapūjāphalaṃ devi prāpnoti sādhakaḥ priye ||

tantragandharve �

manasāyi mahādevyai naivedyaṃ dīyate yadi |
yonaro bhaktisaṃyukto dīrghāyuḥ sasudhī bhavet ||

mālyaṃ padmasahasrasya manasāyaḥ prayacchati |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇikalpakoṭiśatāni ca ||

sthitvā devīpure śrīmānsārva bhaumo bhavet kṣitau |
manasā'pi mahādevyai yastu kuryātpradakṣiṇām |
sa dakṣiṇe yamagṛhe narakāṇi na paśyati ||

manasā'pi mahādevyā yo bhaktyā kurute natim |
so'pi lokān vinirjitya devīloke mahīyate ||

mahāmāye mahādevīmarcayāmi ca bhaktitaḥ |
nānāvidhaistu naivedyairiti cintākulastu yaḥ ||

naivedyaṃ dehi me bhātariti yo bhāvayen muhuḥ |
so'pi lokān vinirjitya devīloke mahīyate ||


iti śrīparivrājakācārya paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūta 
śrīmadbrahmānandagiri kṛtāyāṃ 
śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃmantayagividhirnāma ṣaṣṭhollāsaḥ || 6 ||




saptamollāsaḥ

guptatantroktapūjāvidhiḥ 

athānandamayīpūjāṃ vakṣyāmi guptatāntrikīm |
yāṃ kṛtvā śivasāyujyaṃ labhete sādhakottamaḥ ||

pūjāgṛhaṃ samāsādya sādhakendro maheśvari |
prathamaṃ jalamānīya pādaprakṣālanaṃ caret ||

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā pādaprakṣālanaṃ caret |
divā pūrvamukho bhūtvā rātrau kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ ||

devyāḥ pūjyāṃ śivasyāpi sadā kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ |
praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya taditi ca padaṃ tataḥ ||

saditi tu samuccārya karma kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
smaraṇātkarmaṇāmādye brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||

sarvadā sarvakāryeṣu tāntrike vaidike tathā |
svavidyāṃ saṃsmarankuryātkriyāṃ sarvapracoditām ||

sthānaśodhanamāha gandharvatantre �

vīkṣaṇaṃ varmabījena yāgabhūmeḥ samīritam |
prokṣaṇaṃ cāstramantreṇa yāgabhūmeḥ samācaret ||

ajñātadūṣitaṃ sthānaṃ mārjanādau ca yadbhaved |
evamādīni sarvāṇi naśyet tallokanāt priye ||

madhukaiṭabhayorbhedaḥ saṃghātairdṛḍhatāṃ gatā |
medinī sarvadā'śuddhā surapūjāsu sarvataḥ ||

tasya doṣasya mokṣāya kāmabījaṃ kṣitaulikhet |
pañcavarṇarajaścitrā nānāgandhasamanvitā ||

puṣpaprakarasaṃkīrṇā ghaṇṭācāmarabhūṣitā |
bālārkasadṛśī ramyā manaḥsantoṣakāriṇī ||

evaṃ bhūmiṃ samāśritā pūjayetparameśvarīm |
mantrairācamanaṃ kuryāddevīṃ dhyātvā hṛdambuje |
pīṭhe copaviśeddevi baddhā vīrāsanādikam ||

dravyāsādanam

upaviśya tato mantrī dravyāṇi sthāpayetpuraḥ |
gandhapuṣpākṣatādīṃśca dakṣe dīpāṃśca sarvataḥ ||

naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme purato vā na pṛṣṭhataḥ |
ghṛtadīpaṃ dakṣiṇe tu tailadīpaṃ tu vāmataḥ ||

vāmatastu tathā dhūpamagre vā na tu dakṣiṇe |
nivedayet purobhāge gandhaṃ puṣpañca bhūṣaṇam ||

sarvaṃ svadakṣiṇe sthāpyaṃ vāme cārghyaṃ niveśayet |
sthāpayeccārvyaṃ cūṣyādi naivedyādīni sannidhau ||

karayokṣālanārthāya pṛṣṭhe pātraṃ vinirdiśet |
svasya śaktyānurūpeṇa sarvaṃ sampādya yatnataḥ ||

pūjā dravyāṇi saṃprokṣya mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ |
darśayeddhenumudrāñca dravyaśuddhiritīritā ||

annaṃ naivedyādikaṃ yatpuṣpagandhādikañca yat |
sarvamācchāditaṃ kāryaṃ yāvannāvāhayet param ||

rākṣasāḥ pratigṛhṇanti nirācchādanakaṃ yataḥ ||

atha śāntikumbhapramāṇam 

īśānyāsthāpayetkumbhaṃ svarṇatāmrādinirmitam |
dairghyaṃ viṃśatyaṃgulantu grīvā vedāṃgulānvitā ||

vāṣṭhamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ proktaṃ mukhamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ smṛtam |
dṛḍhaṃ samatalaḥ kāryo mānaṃ tatparikīrtitam ||

kumbhavidhānantu gautamīye �

haimaṃ raupyaṃ tathā tāmraṃ mārttikaṃ vā svaśaktitaḥ |
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kartavyaṃ kṛte naiṣphalyamāpnuyāt ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṃgulaṃ kumbhaṃ vistāronnatiśālinam |
ṣoḍaśaṃ dvādaśaṃ vāpi tato nyūnaṃ na kārayet ||

prokṣaṇīsthāpanamāha gāndharve �

pātramastrāmbubhiḥ prokṣya dakṣiṇe sthāpayettataḥ |
śuddhodakena sampūjya mūlamantraṃ japetsudhīḥ ||

prokṣayettena sakalaṃ prokṣaṇosthane vāriṇā |

ādhārastha jalaśodhanamāha gāndharve �

maṇḍalaṃ vā mataḥ kṛtvā jalena caturasrakam |
oṃ vaṣaṭkāra mantreṇa sādhāraṃ maṇḍale ghaṭam ||

sthāpayettatra vahnayarkasomānāṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhajet |
ānandabhairavaṃ tatra yajedānandabhairavīm ||

yadanyaddūṣaṇaṃ pātre toye vā'jñānato bhavet |
tatsarvaṃ nāśamāyāti pūjāryaṃ tajjalaṃ bhavet ||

arghyasthāpanakramaḥ

arghya dvāri pariṣkārya tatkramaḥ kathyate'dhunā |
astreṇa pātraṃ prakṣālya hṛnmantreṇa prapūrayet |
mantrayetpraṇavenaiva sāmānyārghyamidaṃ smṛtam ||

praṇavena daśadhā mantrayedityarthaḥ |

phaṭā dvāraṃ ca saṃprokṣya bījenāmyarccayet surān |
gāṃ vāṃ kṣāṃ yāṃ ca bījāni uktāni parameśvari ||

gaṇeśavaṭakakṣetrapālāṃśca yoginīṃ tathā |
pūjayetsādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ pratidvāramiti kramāt ||

viśvasāre �

eṣāṃ pūjāṃ vilaṃghyātha na siddhiḥ syādyugeyuge |
uttarādikrameṇaiva dvārapālān samarcayet |
brahmāṇaṃ vāstudevaṃ ca pūjayedgṛhamadhyetaḥ ||

āsane maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā sampūjyārohayetsudhīḥ |
viśodhya vākkāyacittaṃ bhūmiṃ samyagviśodhayet ||

oṃ pavitra vajrabhūme huṃ phaṭ svāheti mantrataḥ |
anantaraṃ deśikendro divyadṛṣṭyā'valokanaiḥ ||
