divyānutsārayedvighnānastrādbhiścāntarīkṣagān |
pārṣṇighātaistribhirbhaumāniti vighnān nivārayet ||

viśvasāre �

animeṣadṛśā dṛṣṭirdivyadṛṣṭiḥ prakīrtitā |

karaśuddhiryāmale �

prāgudīcīmukho vāpi sapuṣpairmārjayetkaram |
mūlamuccārya deveśi tatpuṣpaṃ vāmatastyajet ||

mantramāha yāmale �

bhautikaḥ śaśikalāsamanvito vahniṣoḍaśakalā samanvitaḥ |
ṅentamantramatha phaṭsamanvitaṃ śuddhaye manurayaṃ prakīrtitaḥ ||

	bhautikaḥ aikāraḥ | śaśikalā nādabinduḥ | vahnīrephaḥ | 
ṣoḍaśakalā aḥ kāraḥ | tena aiṃ raḥ astrāya phaṭ | śuddhaye karaśuddhaye 
ityarthaḥ || 

tantragandharve'pi �

gṛhītvā raktapuṣpañca sagandhaṃ sādhakottamaḥ |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa puṣpaṃ hastatalasthitam ||

sammārjya savyahastena vāmena pāṇinā tataḥ |
nirmañchayakāmabījena jighrettadvāgbhavena ca |
aiśānyāṃ nikṣipedetaccharabījena pārvati ||

tatraiva �

mārjanātkarayoḥ śuddhirnirmañchanātu pṛṣṭhayoḥ |
grāṇāddevāśca tuṣyanti tīrthānāñca samāgamaḥ ||

kṣepaṇāt sarvavighnānāṃ dūrasaṃsthānameva ca |
durgandhocchiṣṭa saṃsparśadūṣaṇaṃ karayostu yat ||

ajñātarūpaṃ tatsarvaṃ nāśayed vidhinā'munā |
karaśuddhiṃ samāsādya kuryāttālatraya tataḥ ||

ūrdhvordhvamastramastreṇa digbandhamapi deśikaḥ |
huṃ phaṭ svāheti mantreṇa ūrdhvordhvamapyadhastathā ||

kuryāttālatrayaṃ mantrī digbandhanamathācaret |
digbandhanaṃ choṭikāmirdaśabhiḥ kārayetsudhīḥ ||

vighnamutsāritaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ puṣpaṃ viśodhayet |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vāme gurutrayaṃ namet ||

gurutrayamāhatantre �

guruṃ paraṃguruñcaiva parāparaguruṃ tathā |
dakṣapārśve gaṇeśañca mūrdhni devīṃ nayet priye ||

gandharve �

bhūtaśuddhiṛṣinyāsaḥ pīṭhanyāsastathaiva ca |
karāṅgayoḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni mātṛkānyāsa eva ca ||

vidyānyāso maheśāni yaiśca devamayo bhavet |
etadeva hinityaṃ syātkāmyaṃ cānyatprakīrtitam ||

deva eva yadeddevaṃ nādevo devamarcayet |
adevaḥ pūjayan devaṃ na pūjāphalabhāgbhavet ||

vāsiṣṭharāmāyaṇe �

aviṣṇuḥ pūjayan viṣṇuṃ ca pūjāphalabhāgbhavet |
viṣṇurbhūtvā'rcayedviṣṇuriti smaran ||

bhārate �

nāviṣṇuḥ kīrtayedviṣṇurviṣṇumarcayet |
nāviṣṇuḥ saṃsmaredviṣṇuṃ nāviṇurviṣṇumāpnuyāt ||

bhaviṣye �

nārudraḥ saṃsmaredrudraṃ nārudro rudramarcayet |
nārudraḥ kīrtayedrudraṃ nārudro rudramāpnuyāt ||

nādevī kīrtayeddevīṃ nādevī tāṃ samarcayet |
nyāsāttadātmako bhūtvā devībhūtastu tāṃ yajet ||

āgneye �

rudrasya pūjanādrudro viṣṇuḥ syādviṣṇupūjanāt |
sūryaḥ syād sūryapūjātaḥ śāktyādiḥ śaktipūjanāt ||

	śaktipūjanāt śaktyādipūjanāt | ādipadāt gaṇeśādi parigrahaḥ |

dyenaiva nyāsamātreṇa devavajra jāyatenaraḥ |
prāṇāyāmaistathā dhyānairnyāsairdevaśarīramṛt |
nyāsānāṃ pracuratvena phalānāmapi bhūritā ||

bhūtaśuddhiḥ

tantragandharve �

svabhāvataḥ sadā'śuddhaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ vapuḥ |
malamūtrasamāyuktaṃ sarvadaiva maheśvari ||

tasyaiva hi viśuddhyarthaṃ vāyvagnisalilākṣaraiḥ |
candrabījena deveśi pṛthvībījena deśikaḥ ||

śoṣadāhau tathā bhasmaprotsārāmṛtavarṣaṇam |
āplāvanañca kartavyaṃ pūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ ||

śarīrākāraprāptānāṃ bhūtānāṃ yadviśodhanam |
avyaktabrahmasaṃsparśād bhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ śive ||

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāye tthamarghyādisthāpanaṃ caret |
vidadhyānmātṛkānyāsaṃ mantranyāsamanantaram ||

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryādṛṣyādinyāsamācaret |
nyāsau karāṅgayoḥ kṛtvātmānaṃ bhagavatīṃ smaret ||

prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryātpūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ |
tattrayaṃ tu vidhātavyamanulomavilomataḥ ||

arghyaṃ saṃsthāpayenmantrī yathānyāyaṃ vidhānataḥ |
trikoṇaṣaṭkoṇavṛttacaturasrāṇi kārayet |
puṣpairabhyarcya tanmantrī tatrādhāraṃ niveśayet ||

maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāyeti daśakalātmane namaḥ |
pūjayitvā arghyapātraṃ tu tatraiva sthāpayed budhaḥ ||

phaḍiti prakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā pūjayetsādhakottamaḥ |
aṃ arkamaṇḍalāyeti dvādaśānte kalātmane ||

nama ityantamantreṇa pūjayedarkamaṇḍalam |
mūlenāpūrya deveśi vimalena jalena tu ||

oṃ somamaṇḍalāyeti ṣoḍaśānte kalātmane |
nama ityantamantreṇa pūjayeccandramaṇḍalam ||

pūjayitvā ṣaḍaṅgāni nyasetsādhakasattamaḥ |
tatrākṣatāni puṣpāṇi dūrvādīne viniḥkṣipet ||

mūlamantraṃ japet spṛṣṭvā aṅgamantraṃ pravinyaset |
hṛnmantreṇā'bhisampūjya hastābhyāṃ chādayedapaḥ ||

hastābhyāmiti matsyamudrayā ityarthaḥ |

astramantreṇa saṃrakṣya kavacenāvaguṇṭhya ca |
dhenumudrāṃ samāpādya rodhayet tsvamudrayā |
amṛtaṃ tajjalaṃ cintyaṃ dravyasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ caret ||

gandhapuṣpākṣatayavakuśāgratilasarṣapaiḥ |
sadūrvaiḥ sarvadevānāmeta darghyamudīritam ||

śivaviṣaye gṛhiṇāṃ sagarbhaivi dūrvā | yathā �

antaḥ śūnyāṃ tripatrāñca yodadyānmacchiropari |
janmanyatra daridraḥ syādante ca narakaṃ vrajet ||

arghyapātrasthitaistoyairvinā yattu nivedanam |
devebhyo dīyate yadyad tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||

arghyasyottarataḥ sthāpyaṃ pādyamācamanīyakam |
tatpārśve madhuparkañca dadyāttu madhumiśritam ||

etat śyāmākadūrvābjaviṣṇukrāntābhirīritam |
pādyapātre ca dātavyamarghyañcaivārghyapātrake ||

jātīlavaṅgakaṃkolaṃ dadyādācamanīyake |
ādau dravyāṇi saṃskṛtya paścāttantroditānnyaset |
mātṛkā dvividhā proktā parācāpyaparā tathā |
suṣumnāntaḥ parājñeyā aparādehamāśritā ||

atha mātṛkāṣaḍaṅganyāsaḥ

jñānārṇave �

aṃ āṃmadhye kavargantu iṃ īṃ madhye cavargakam |
uṃ ūṃ madhye ṭavargantu eṃ aiṃ madhye tavargakam ||

oṃ auṃ madhye pavargantu aṃ aḥ madhye yavargakam |
nyāsaṃ kuyānmaheśāni hṛdayādi ṣaḍaṅgakam ||

mūlādi brahmarandhrāntaṃ dhyāyeddevīṃ cidātmikām |
bindusrata sudhāsāraistarpayanmātṛkāṃ nyaset ||

antarmātṛkānyāsaḥ

athā'ntarmātṛkānyāsaṃ śṛṇuṣva kamalānane |
dvyaṣṭapatrāmbuje kaṇṭhe svarānṣoḍaśa vinyaset ||

dvādaśacchadahṛtpadme kādīndvādaśa vinyaset |
daśapatrāmbuje nābhau ḍakārādīnnyaseddaśa ||

ṣaṭpatre liṅgamūle ca vakārādīnnyasecca ṣaṭ |
ādhāre caturo varṇānnyasedvādīn caturdale ||

hakṣau bhrūmadhyage padme dvidale vinyasetpriye |
ekaikaṃ varṇamuccārya manasā tu dhruvādikam |
namo'ntamiti vinyāsa āntaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||

dhruvādikaṃ praṇavādikamityarthaḥ |

śāradāyām �

bāhyaṃ caiva mātṛkānyāsaṃ śṛṇuṣvāvahitomama |
lalāṭamukhavṛttākṣiśrutighrāṇeśu gaṇḍayoḥ ||

oṣṭhadantottamāṅgasyadoḥpatsandhyagrakeṣu ca |
pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhato nābhau jaṭhare hṛdayeṃ'saṅke ||

kakudyaṃse ca hṛtpūrvapāṇipādayuge tathā |
jaṭharānanayornyasenmātṛkārṇān yathākramam ||

mātṛkānyāsamudrāmāha mānasollāse �

manasā vā nyasennyāsānpuṣpairevā'thavā nyaset |
aṃguṣṭhānāmikāyogānnyasedvā sarvakarmasu ||

gautamīye �

caturddhā mātṛkā prokta kevalā bindusaṃyuktā |
savisargā sobhayā ca rahasyaṃ kathayāmite ||

antyopāntyau svarau bindusargahīnau nyasetpriye |
vidyākarī kevalā ca sobhayā muktidāyikā |
savisargā putradātrī sabindurbindudāyinī ||

bindudāyinī mokṣadāyinītyarthaḥ |

dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ kalikalmaṣanāśanam |
yaḥ kuryānmātṛkānyāsaṃ sa eva śrīsadāśivaḥ ||

atha vidyānyāsaḥ

navaratneśvare �

mūrdhni mūle ca hṛdaye netratritaya eva ca |
śrotrayoryugale devi mukhe ca bhujayoḥ punaḥ ||

pṛṣṭhe jānvostathā nābhau vidyānyāsaṃ samācaret |
evaṃ nyāsakṛtaḥ sākṣātpaśuḥ paśupatiḥ svayam ||

phetkāriṇīyatantre �

oṃkārasampuṭīkṛtya mūlena vyāpakaṃ nyaset |
pañcadhā navadhā vāpi nyasedvā saptadhā'thavā ||

	mūlamuccārya śīrṣādipādaparyantaṃ pādādiśīrṣāntaṃ 
hṛdayādimukhāntaṃ vyāpakaṃ nyasedityarthaḥ | iti vidyānyāsaḥ ||

viśuddheśvare �

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryādvidyayā tadanantaram |
pūrakaṃ vāmanāḍyāṃ tu kuryād ṣoḍaśadhā japaiḥ ||

kumbhakaṃ madhyanāḍyāntu catuḥṣaṣṭijapāttataḥ |
recakaṃ piṅgalāyāntu tadarddhajapasaṃkhyayā ||

viparītaṃ punaḥ kuryādyathāśaktyā ca sādhakaḥ |
tadaśaktau caturddhāpi prāṇasaṃyamanaṃ caret ||

caturddheti mūlavidyāścaturvārajapena pūrakaṃ, ṣoḍaśavāra japena 
kumbhakamaṣṭavārajapena recakamirtyaḥ | tatrāpyaśaktau 
samayāṅkamātṛkāyām |

iḍayā pūrayedvāyuṃ sakṛcca mūlavidyayā |
madhyanāḍyā kumbhayecca vedasaṃkhyaṃ varānane ||

netrasaṃkhyākrameṇaiva recayet piṅgalādhvanā |
punaḥ punaḥ krameṇaiva yathā vāratrayaṃ bhavet ||

bāhyādāpūraṇaṃ vāyorudare pūrako bhavet |
sampūrṇakumbhavadvāyordhāraṇaṃ kumbhako bhavet |
vahiryadrecanaṃ vāyorudarādrecako hi saḥ ||

jñānārṇave �

kaniṣṭhānāmikāṃguṣṭhairyannāsāpuṭadhāraṇam |
prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyastarjanīmadhyame vinā |
prāṇāyāmaṃ vinā devi pūjane nāsti yogyatā ||

yāmale �

ṛṣiṃ nyasenmūrdhni deśe chandastu mukhapaṅkaje |
devatāṃ hṛdaye caiva bījaṃ tu guhyadeśake ||

śaktiṃ tu pādayoścaiva sarvāṅge kīlakaṃ nyaset ||

ṛṣi nyasediti �

tattatprakāraṇīyamṛṣicchanda ityādikaṃ nyasedityarthaḥ |
ṛṣicchando devatānāṃ vinyāsena vinā yadā |
japettu sādhako yastu na sa tatra phalaṃ labhet ||

karāṅganyāsamāha śāradāyām �

aṃguṣṭhādiṣvaṃguliṣu nyasedaṅgaiḥ sajātibhiḥ |

	aṅgaistattatkalpoktāṃgamantraiḥ | sajātibhiḥ nama ādibhiḥ | 
jñānārṇave �

namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huṃ vauṣaṭ phaḍantāḥ sajātayaḥ |
hṛcchiraḥ śikhā-kavaca-netratrayaṃ tathāstrakam ||

śāradāyām �

astraṃ tattalayornyasya kuryāttālatrayādikam |
diśastenaiva badhnīyācchoṭikābhiḥ samāhitaḥ ||

athāṅganyāsaḥ 

hṛdayādiṣu vinyasedaṅgamantrāṃstataḥ sudhīḥ |
hṛdayāya namaḥ pūrvaṃ śirase vahnivallabhā ||

śikhāyai vaṣaḍityuktaṃ kavacāya humīritam |
netratrayāya vauṣaṭ syādastrāya phaḍiti kramāt |
ṣaḍaṅgamantrānityuktān ṣaḍaṅgeṣu niyojayet ||

rudrayāmale �

hṛdayaṃ madhyamā'nāmātarjanībhiḥ smṛtaṃ śiraḥ |
madhyamārjanībhyāṃ syādaṃguṣṭhena śikhā smṛtā ||

daśabhiḥ kavacaṃ proktaṃ tisṛbhirnetramīritam |
proktāṃgulībhyāmastraṃ syādaṃkḷptiriyaṃ matā || iti |

tisṛbhiriti tarjanīmadhyamānāmābhiḥ |

tarjanīmadhyamānāmāḥ proktā netratraye kramāt |
yadi netradvayaṃ devi tadā tarjanīmadhyame ||

bhairavatantre �

ṣaḍaṅgāni nyasenmantrī triḥsakṛdvā yathākṣamam |

tantre �

aṅganyāsa karanyāsau ṣaḍdīrghamāyayā caret |

sārāvalyāṃ �

yadbījādyā bhavedvidyā tadbījenā'ṅgakalpanā |

kulacūḍāmaṇau �

ekākṣaramadhikṛtya pūrvaṃ bījaṃ paraṃ śaktiriti |
ṣaḍdīrghabhājābījena kuryādaṅgādikalpanā ||

kālīvidyāyāṃ svacchandasaṃgrahe �

svaraṃ vihāya bījasya dīrghaṣaṭkaṃ niyojayan |
ṣaḍaṅgāni vidadhyādvai sarvatrā'yaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||

pūjājapārcanāhomāḥ siddhamantraiḥ kṛtā api |
aṅganyāsena hīnāstu na dāsyanti phalānyamī ||

	ityaṅganyāsasya nityatvam | atha svasvakalpoktaṣoḍhānyāsaṃ 
kuryāt ||

ṣoḍhānyāsaphalam

ṣoḍhānyāsaśarīrastu bhaved gaṅgādharaḥ svayam |
avaśyaṃ pratyahaṃ kuryāt tataḥ pūjāṃ japaṃ tathā |
kṛte'pi sādhaka śreṣṭho mahādevasamo bhavet ||

kṛtanyāso'kṛtanyāsaṃ praṇamedyadi pārvati |
tatkṣaṇādakṛtanyāso vidīrṇahṛdayo bhavet ||

yaṃ namanti mahādevi ṣoḍhāpuṭita vigrahāḥ |
alpāyuḥ sa bhavetsadyo devatā kampate bhiyā |
nyāsaṃ nirvartayeddevi ṣoḍhānyāsapuraḥsaram ||

ātmadhyānam

tantragandharve �

ātmānaṃ sādhako dhyāyeddivyastrībhiralaṃkṛtam |
divyaṃ mūrdhni mahācchatraṃ sahasradala kalpitam ||

ratnāsanopaviṣṭantu lākṣāruṇagṛhasthitam |
tāmbūlaraktavadanaṃ nānāgandhasamanvitam ||

candanāgurukastūrī raktacandanabhūṣitam |
sarvālaṅkārabhūṣāḍhayaṃ devyā vigraharūpiṇam ||

sugandhipuṣpābharaṇaratnādibhiralaṃkṛtam |
tasya hastagatā siddhirnānyasya ca kadācana ||

devīdhyānam

tato devīṃ hṛdambhoje dhyāyettadgatamānasaḥ |
puṣpaṃ gṛhītvā deveśi mudrayā tu trikhaṇḍayā ||

tāṃ kuryād hṛdayāsannāṃ nimīlya locanadvayam |
samakāyaśirogrīvā bhūtvā sthiramanā budhaḥ ||

tatraiva saphaladhyānakartavyatvamāha �

dhyānaṃ samācarenmantrī sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
tato hṛtpadmagāṃ devīṃ mānasairupacārakaiḥ ||

gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpairnaivedyairbalibhistathā |
bhorgeśca pūjayedenāṃ sādhakastu yathāvidhi ||

tato vai mānasaṃ jāpaṃ kuryād homañca sādhakaḥ |
namaskṛtya tathā stutvā vahiryajanamācaret ||

tato hṛdayapatrāntaḥ sphurantīṃ parameśvarīm |
suṣumnāvartmanā nītvā śira(va)sthāne maheśvarīm ||

tatrānandena saṃyojya kevalānandarūpiṇīm |
tato vai hṛdayāsanne pūrvasthāne samānayet ||

tāmājñāsthānamānīya vahannābhyā virecayet |
nāsayā dakṣayā devi vāyubījena mantravit |
karasthakusume devīṃ sthāpayedāsanopari ||

devyāvāhanam

ehyehi bhagavatyamba bhaktānugrahavigrahe |
yoginībhiḥ samaṃ devi rakṣārthaṃ mama sarva(dā)thā ||

deveśi ! bhaktisulabhe ! parivāra samanvite |
yāvat tvāṃ pūjayāmīśe tāvattvaṃ susthirā bhava |
devīṃ dhyātvā samāvāhya tattanmudrāḥ pradarśayet ||

	tattanmudrā āvāhanyādi pañcamudrāḥ | 
śālagrāmādāvāhanasya niṣedhamāha �

śālagrāme maṇau cāpsu vahnau manasi puṣpake |
eṣu cāvāhanaṃ nāsti tatra devāḥ sadā sthitāḥ ||

dravyadānaniyamaḥ

pūjāprakāramāha yāmale �

ādau mūlaṃ samuccārya paścād deyaṃ samuccaret |
sampradānaṃ tadante tu tyāgārthakapadaṃ tataḥ |
evaṃ kalpakrameṇaiva pūjayet parameśvarīm ||

nārcayedekahastena pañcānāṃ nakhadarśanam |
niṣphalā kīrtitā sā hi sarvatrā'pi na śobhate ||

ṣaḍaṅgādyāvaraṇapūjā

caraṇādhāranābhyantarvakṣo mauliṣu pañcasu |
pañcāñjalīnprasūnaiśca vikīryā'tha maheśvari |
devīpādāmbuje dvandve tridhā puṣpāñjalīn kṣipet ||

śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmītyamuṃ mantraṃ samuccaran |
aṃguṣṭhā'nāmayā dakṣe tridhā puṣpāṇi pātayet ||

tarpaṇaṃ tu mukhe dadyāttrivāraṃ tattvamudrayā |
aṃguṣṭhānāmikāyogaḥ śivaśaktyātmakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

tayoḥ saṃyogamātreṇa dravyaṃ syādamṛtoparam |
tenāmṛtena divyena tarpayet paradevatām ||

vyaktamāha �

aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāñca vāmahastasya pārvati |
tarpayetsundarīṃ devīṃ samudrāñca savāhanām ||]
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pūjayet tatra devyā dehe'tha sādhakaḥ |
hṛdaye hṛdayāṅgaṃ tu śirasyeva śiro'ṅgakam ||

śikhāṃ śikhāyāṃ sampūjya kavacaṃ sarvadehake |
netratrayaṃ trinetre tu dikṣvastraṃ ca prapūjayet ||

namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huñca vauṣaṭ phaṭ jātisaṃyutam |
ṣaḍaṅgayuvatī nityaṃ devyo deheṣu saṃsthitā ||

tantre �

ijyaṃ hṛdayamāgneyyāmaiśānyāṃ tu śiro yajet |
nair-ṛtyāṃ tu śikhā pūjyā vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ yajet ||

abhyarcya purato netraṃ dikṣu cātrathārcayet |
pradhānatanurūpāṇi ṣaḍaṅgāni prapūjayet ||

śāradāṭīkāyām �

vāyavyādīśaparyantaṃ gurupaṃkti samarcayet |

gurupaṃktyājñāne yāmale �

avijñātagururdevi guruñca paramaṃ gurum |
parāparaguruṃ caiva parameṣṭhiguruṃ tathā |
āgneyādicatuṣkoṇe pūjayet parameśvari ||

āgneyādi koṇamāha tantragandharve �

īśānamagnikoṇaṃ syādvāyukoṇa tatheśakam |
rākṣasaṃ vāyukoṇaṃ syādagniśca rākṣasa bhavet ||

gandharvatantre �

athavā raśmayaḥ sarvā devīrūpā vicintayet |
niḥsaranti yathā nityaṃ sūryabimbānmarīcayaḥ ||

devyastathā samutpannā mahādevyāḥ śarīrataḥ |
śrīpātrāmṛtatoyena raśmivṛndaṃ pratarpayet ||

prācīṃ diśaṃ tu vijñāya pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ |
svasthānamāśritya devāḥ sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradāḥ |
svasthānavarjitā devāḥ śokaduḥkhaphalapradaḥ ||

pūrvādidignirūpaṇam

prācyādidiśamāha navaratneśvare �

pūjyapūjakamadhyaṃ tu pūrvāśaiva vyavasthitā |
pūjyasya dakṣiṇe dakṣā cottare cottarā tathā |
paścime paścimā jñeyā pūjāyāṃ sarvataḥ śive ||

sarvata iti ṣaḍaṅgapūjāyām |

ātmanaḥ sammukhañcaiva devatāyāśca sammukham |
devasya mastakaṃ kuryātkusumenācitaṃ sadā |
pūjākāle devatāyā nopari bhrāmayetkaram ||

tripurāviṣaye �

purandaramukho devī pūjayettripurāṃ yadi |
devīpṛṣṭhaṃ bhavet prācī pratīcī tripurāpuraḥ ||

	kṛtāñjaliḥ � śrīmatyamuki devi āvaraṇaṃ te pūjayāmi 
ityanujñāṃ labdhvā āvaraṇaṃ pūjayet |

padmapatre tataścakre devyā agradalāditaḥ |
vāmāvartena deveśi krameṇa paripūjayet |
svakalpokta krameṇaiva pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ ||

kulārṇave �

trivāraṃ pūjayedvāpi sakṛdvāpi yathecchayā |

yāmale �

devyastraṃ pūjayeddikṣu punardevīṃ prapūjayet |
savāhanāḥ savargāśca parivārāstataḥ param ||

tathā sarvopacāraiśca pūjitāstarpitāstathā |
santvityetanmanuṃ japtvā devyai pūjāṃ samarpayet ||

pūjāṃ sarpayeddevyai gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
viśeṣārghyodakenaiva tarpayetparameśvarīm ||

mantrajapaprakāraḥ

dhyātvā kāmakalāṃ dehe vidyājapaṃ samācaret |
mantrārthasmṛtipūrvaṃ tu sahasrādijapaṃ caret ||

bṛhacchrīkrame �

na japettriṃśatā nyūnaṃ sādhakastu kadācana |

tantre �

sahasraṃ vā śataṃ vāpi daśa vāpi japaṃ tathā |
kuryādaṣṭādhikaṃ teṣāmiti japyavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||

japaṃ samarpayeddevi gandhapuṣpārghyavāribhiḥ |
tejomayaṃ japaṃ devyā vāmahaste nivedayet ||

guhyātiguhyagoptrī tvamiti mantreṇa mantravit |
tato nīrājanaṃ kuryāddaśavāraṃ tu dīpakaiḥ ||

stuvan pradakṣiṇīkṛtya daṇḍavad praṇamed bhuvi |
ātmārpaṇena manunā kuryādātmārpaṇaṃ priye ||

ātmasamarpaṇam

taduktaṃ yāmale �

itaḥ pūrvamiti procya prāṇabuddhīti coccaret |
dehadharmādhikārato jāgratsvapnasuṣuptiṣu ||

sarvāvasthāsu manasā vācā ca karmaṇeti ca |
hastābhyāmatha padbhyāñca tathodareṇa saṃsmaret ||

śiśnā yatsmṛtamityetadyaduktaṃ yatkṛtaṃ tathā |
sarvamityapi tadbrahmārpaṇamastvagnivallabhā ||

praṇavañca madīyaṃ māṃ sakalaṃ sādhyadevatām |
ṅentāṃ samarpitaṃ tāraṃ tatsadityapi saṃsmaret ||

arghyodakākṣatairmūlairdevyai pūjāṃ samarpayet |
pūjitā'stviyanenaiva devyai pūjāṃ samarpayet |
devyā gṛhītamityevaṃ bhāvayedyatamānasaḥ ||

viśvasāre �

ājñānādvā pramādādvā vaikalyātsādhanasya ca |
yannyūnamatiriktaṃ vā tatsarvaṃ kṣantumarhasi ||

dravyahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ śraddhābhaktivivarjitam |
tatsarvaṃ kṛpayā devi kṣamasva tvaṃ dayānidhe ||

yanmayā kriyate karma mahadvā svalpameva vā |
tatsarvañca jagaddhātri kṣantavyamayamañjaliḥ ||

kulārṇave �

kṛtāñjalirmaheśāni rakṣāmantraṃ paṭhetsudhīḥ |

oṃ kālī vidadhyānmama putrarakṣāṃ tathā karālī mama deharakṣām |

durgā'ṭṭahāsairmama śatrunāśaṃ karotu tārā vidadhatu rājyam ||

paṭhetsahasranāmākhyaṃ stotraṃ mokṣasya sādhanam |
stotraiḥ stutvā paṭheddevi kavacaṃ sarvakāmadam |
kavacaṃ hi vinā devi śūdrastu japamācaret ||

kavacaṃ hi bineti | svāhāpraṇavasaṃyuktaṃ kavacaṃ vinetyarthaḥ |

devīṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskṛtya visarjayet |
vidhāya paścātsvāṃ vidyāṃ svīyahṛtsarasīruhe ||

suṣumnāvartmanā puṣpamaghrāyodvāsayettataḥ |
kṣamasveti ca mantreṇa hṛdi devīṃ visarjayet ||

bhairavītantre �

saṃhāra mudrayā devi kṣamasveti visarjayet |
tannaivedyaśatāṃśaṃ vā sahasrāṃśaṃ ca bhairavi |
dadyāducchiṣṭacāṇḍālyai svāheti manunā tataḥ ||

athavā �

nirmālyena yajeddevīmīśe nirmālyavāsinīm |
nirmālyaṃ śirasā dhāryaṃ sarvāṅge cānulepanam ||

naivedyañcopamuñjīta dattvā tadbhaktiśāline |
śatābhimantritaṃ puṣpaṃ candanaṃ mūrdhni bhālataḥ ||

dhṛtvā'vaśyaṃ nayedvaśyaṃ trailokyamapi darśanāt |
yaṃ yaṃ gacchāmi pādena yaṃ yaṃ paśyāmi cakṣuṣā ||

sa eva dāsatāṃ yātu yadi śakrasamo bhavet |
anena tilakaṃ kṛtvā trailokyamapi mohayet ||

sadyaḥ paryusitaṃ vāpi nirmālyaṃ pradūṣyati |
brahmaradhre guptasthāne yantralepaṃ tu dhārayet |
udakaṃ tarumūle vā nirmālyaṃ tasya saṃtyajet ||

rudrayāmale �

pūrvajanmārjitaiḥ puṇyairjñātvaināṃ paradevatām |
yo bhajedbhaktibhāvena sadyaḥ śrīsampadāṃ padam |
yadārādhanamātreṇa jīvanmuktiḥ prajāyate || iti vacanāt |

devyāḥ pūjā tridhā proktā sthūlamābhyantaraṃ tathā |
sthūlaṃ mantramayīṃ pūjāṃ sthūlavigrahacintanam ||

mānasairupacāraistu pūjā cābhyantaraṃ priye |
karmayogaṃ vinā devi jñānayogo na sidhyati |
jñānena karmaṇā vāpi siddhirbhavati nānyathā ||


iti śrīparivrājakācāryaparamahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ prātaḥkṛtyanirṇayo 
nityapūjāpramāṇanirṇayo nāma saptamollāsaḥ || 7 ||




aṣṭamollāsaḥ

mālānirṇayaḥ 

atha vakṣye maheśāni mālāyāḥ parinirṇayam |
nityaṃ japaṃ kare kuryād na tu kāmyaṃ kadācan |
kāmyamapi kare kuryād mālābhāve ca sundari ||

atha karamālā yāmale �

anāmāyāstrayaṃ parvaṃ kaniṣṭhāyāstriparvikā |
madhyamāyāstrayaṃ parvaṃ tarjanī mūlaparvaṃ ca ||

prādakṣiṇyakrameṇaiva japeddaśasu parvasu |
śaktimālā samākhyātā sarvamantrapradīpikā ||

parvadvayaṃ tu tarjanyā meruṃ tadviddhi pārvati |
tarjanyagre tathā madhye yo japet tatramānavaḥ |
catvāri tasya naśyanti āyurvidyāyaśobalam ||

śrīvidyāyām �

anāmāmadhyamāyāśca mūlāgrañca dvayaṃ dvayam |
kaniṣṭhāyāśca tarjanyāstrayaṃ parvaṃ maheśvari ||

anāmāmadhyayormadhyaṃ meruśca dvitayaṃ smṛtam |
prādakṣiṇyakramāddevi japet tripurasundarīm ||

karamālāyāṃ japaprakāraḥ

hṛdaye hastamāropya tiryak kṛtvā karāṃgulīḥ |
ācchādya vāsasā hastau dakṣiṇena sadā japet ||

aṃgulīnāṃ viyuñjīta kiñcidākuñcayettalam |
aṃgulīnāṃ viyogācca chidreṇa sravate japaḥ ||

aṃgulyagreṣu yajjaptuṃ yajjaptuṃ merulaṃdhane |
sarvasandhisu yajjaptuṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||

yāmale maṇiniyamamāha �

maṇyaṣṭakaśatenaiva nirmitā yā tu mālikā |
rājyaṃ vitanute nityaṃ dehānte mokṣadāyinī ||

mokṣadā pañcaviṃśatyā triṃśatā dhanavṛddhidā |
caturdaśamayī mālā mokṣadā bhogavarddhinī |
sarvārthāḥ saptaviṃśatyā pañcadaśyābhicārike ||

(pañcaviṃśatyābhicārake ityapi pāṭhaḥ) 

pañcāśatā kāryasaddhiścatuḥpañcāśatā tathā |
aṣṭottaraśatenaiva sarvasiddhirudāhṛtā |
tripurāyāṇapeśastā rudrākṣai raktacandanaiḥ ||

bhairavīviṣaye vārāhītantre �

suvarṇamaṇibhirmālā sphāṭikī śaṅkhanirmitā |
pravālairapi vā kāryā putrajīvaṃ vivarjayet ||

śmaśānadhustūrairmālāṃ kuryāddhūmāvatī vidhau |
raktena candanenā'pi bālāmālāṃ prakalpayet ||

dantena kālikāyāstu rājadantasya meruṇā |
ugratārājape śastā mahāśaṅkhasya mālikā ||

unmukhyāśca tathā jñeyā mālikā siddhidāyikā |
śāktānāṃ sphāṭikī mālā raktacandanasambhavā ||

rudrākṣamālikā jñeyā caturvargaphalapradā |

nirmitā raupyamaṇibhirjapamālepsitapradā ||

hiraṇyamaṇibhirmālā sarvānkāmānprayacchati |
pravālairvihitā mālā prayacchet vipulaṃ dhanam ||

saubhāgyaṃ sphāṭikī mālā mauktikairvihitā tathā |
nirmitā śaṅkhamaṇibhiḥ kurute kīrtimayavyām ||

suvarṇai racitā mālā sadā syānmuktaye nṝṇām |
gopanīyā'niśaṃ devi japamālepsitāptaye ||

muṇḍamālāyām �

rudrākṣairvā yadi japedindrākṣaiḥ sphāṭikaistathā |
nānyamadhye prayoktavyaṃ putrajīvādikañca yat ||

yadanyattu prayuñjīta mālāyāṃ japakarmaṇi |
tasya kāmaṃ ca mokṣa ca no dadāti priyaṅkarī ||

yāmale �

rudrākṣaiḥ śaktimantrañca mantrī yaḥ prajapet priye |
sa durgatimāpnoti niṣphalasya tasya tajjapaḥ ||

pratiprasavamāha tatraiva �

kālikā chinnamastā ca tripurā tāriṇī tathā |
etāḥ sarvā na dūṣyanti japād rudrākṣamālayā ||

rudrayāmale �

divā naiva prajaptavyaṃ rudrākṣamālayā kvacit |
pūrvaścaryādṛte cātra dūṣaṇañca varānane ||

arudrākṣadharaḥ kuryāttāntrikaṃ vaidikaṃ tathā |
japahomādikaṃ yadyattatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||

mālāviśeṣaphalamāha mantradevaprakāśikāyām �

parvasvaṣṭaguṇaṃ putrajīve daśaguṇaṃ bhavet |
śataṃ syācchaṅkhamālāyāṃ pravāle ca sahasrakam ||

sphāṭike daśasāhasraṃ lakṣaṃ tu mauktike bhavet |
padmākṣe daśalakṣantu koṭiḥ sauvarṇike tathā ||

kuśagranthau ca rudrākṣeṣvanantaguṇitaṃ bhavet |
śvetapadmākṣamālāyāṃ jape syādamitaṃ phalam ||

mālāvidhānam

samāsenā'kṣamālānāṃ vidhānamiha kathyate |
yathālābhaṃ yathārūpamakṣāṇyādāya yatnataḥ ||

anyonyasamarūpāṇi nātisthūlakṛśāni ca |
kīṭādibhiraduṣṭāni na jīrṇāni kadācana ||

gavyaiśca pañcabhistāni prakṣālayetpṛthak pṛthak |
dvijastrīnirmitaṃ sūtraṃ śubhraṃ granthivivarjitam ||

kārpāsanirmitaṃ vāpi paṭṭasūtramathāpi vā |
triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtya grathayecchilpaśāstrataḥ ||

sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ paṭṭaṃ sarvepsitapradam |
kārpāsasambhavaṃ sūtraṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadam ||

mūlāstre ca paṭhan sūtraṃ bījaṃ prakṣālayettataḥ |
maṇimekaikamādāya sūtre tatra tu yojayet ||

mukhe mukhantu saṃyojya pucche pucchaṃ niyojayet |
tatsajātīyamekākṣaṃ merutvenā'grato nyaset ||

ekaikamaṇimādāya brahmagranthiṃ prakalpayet |
grathayena mālikāṃ caiva hṛdi tāraṃ manuṃ smaran ||

svayameva japenmantramanyaḥ praṇavamuccaret |
sārddhatrayāvartanena granthiṃ kuryādatho dṛḍham ||

brahmagranthiṃ tato dadyānnāgapāśamathāpi vā |
gopucchasadṛśīṃ kuryādathavā sarpapucchavat ||

granthihīnā na kartavyā merupṛṣṭhe na dūṣyati |
dūṣaṇaṃ tvatra nāstyeva granthihīnaiva nityaśaḥ ||

kālyāśca tvaritāyāśca vajrākhyā ṣaṭkabhedake |
totalāvanavāsinyorvārāhyāśca viśeṣataḥ ||

nānyasyāścaṇḍikāyāśca granthihīnā vidhīyate |
evaṃ nirmāya mālāṃ vai śodhayet mantrisattamaḥ ||

apratiṣṭhitamālābhirmantraṃ japati yo naraḥ |
sarvaṃ tadviphalaṃ vidyātkruddhā bhavati caṇḍikā ||

mālāpratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ

athocyate pratiṣṭhā hi mālāyāstantravartmanā |
guruṃ tataḥ praṇamyādau saṃskuryājjapamālikām ||

śubhe lagne śubhe vāre śubherkṣe ca śubhe tithau |
pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayenmantrī svayaṃ vā guruṇā'thavā ||

nityaṃ karma tataḥ kṛtvā sāmānyārghyaṃ vidhāya ca |
pañcagavye kṣipenmālāṃ śivamantreṇa mantrayet ||

śivamantramāha yāmale �

śāntaḥ śatrusvarārūḍho nādabinduvibhūṣitaḥ |
kathitaḥ śivamantro'yaṃ sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca ||

śānto hakāraḥ, śatrusvaraḥ ukāraḥ |

śītalena jalenaiva snāpayettadanantaram |
kṣālayetpañcagavyena sadyojātena mārjayet ||

sadyojātamantrastu �

sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi sadyojātāya vai namaḥ |
bhave bhave nātibhave bhajasva māṃ bhavodbhavāya namaḥ ||

kṣālayedīśasūktena limpettatpuruṣeṇa tu |
gandhairanalpairmatimānaghoreṇa tu dhūpayet ||

aghoreṇa tu sūktena śatānyūnaṃ tu mantrayet |
vāmadevena mantreṇa samīkuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||

aśvatthapatranavakaiḥ padmākāraṃ prakalpayet |
tanmadhye sthāpayenmālāṃ mātṛkāmūlamuccaran |
saṃskṛtyaivaṃ budhomālāṃ tatprāṇāṃstatra yojayet ||

tatprāṇān ārādhyadevatāprāṇān |

tatra devīṃ prapūjyaiva parivāragaṇaiḥ saha |
anulomavilomena mātṛkārṇena mantrayet |
meruṃ pretena saṃmantrtra bhāvayeddevatātmikām ||

pretena pretabījena hasauḥ iti bījenetyarthaḥ |

vahniṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivadaṣṭottaraśataṃ hunet |
hutaśeṣaṃ pratikṛtau pradadyāddevatādhiyā ||

homakarmaṇyaśaktaśced dviguṇaṃ japamācaret |
tārākṣamālādhipate susiddhiṃ dehi dehi me ||

sarvamantrārthasādhini sādhaya dvitayaṃ tataḥ |
sarvasiddhiṃ parikalpaya parikalpaya me svāhā ||

itthamāśaṃsitā mālā japakarmaṇi sarvadā |
abhīṣṭakaṃ dadātyarthaṃ sarvakāmaphalaṃ tathā ||

mālāyāṃ japa vidhiḥ

guruṃ sampūjya taddhastād gṛhṇīyādakṣamālikām |
japādau pūjayenmālāṃ toyairabhyukṣya yatnataḥ ||

aiṃ hrīṃ akṣamālikāyai hṛnmantreṇa prapūjayet |
pūjayitvā tato mālāṃ gṛhṇīyāddakṣiṇe kare ||

hṛtsamīpe samānīya na tu vāmena saṃpṛśet |
madhyamāyā madhyabhāge sthāpayitvā samāhitaḥ ||

madhyamasthāmakṣamālamaṃguṣṭhāgreṇa cālayet |
aśucirna spṛśenmālāṃ karabhraṣṭhāṃ na kārayet ||

tarjanyā na spṛśedenāṃ gurorapi na darśayet |
bhuktau muktau tathā puṣṭau madhyamāyāṃ japetsudhīḥ ||

ekaikasya jape'pyevaṃ cālayeddeśikottamaḥ |
japtvā'kṣamālāṃ sakalāṃ bhrāmayedakhilānmaṇīn ||

pradakṣiṇaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā prārabhyaivaṃ samāpayet |
evaṃ krameṇa deveśi japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam ||

sthūlāvadhi japenmantraṃ sūkṣmabhāge samarpayet |
hastau ca vāsasācchādya dakṣiṇena sadā japet ||

evaṃ sūkṣmāvadhisthūlānto japaḥ saṃhāraḥ ||

na svayaṃ vāmahastena japamālāṃ tu saṃspṛśet |
japakāle japaṃ kṛtvā śuddhasthāne sadā nyaset ||

jīrṇesūtre punaḥ sūtraṃ grathayitvā śataṃ japet |
adīkṣitadvijenā'pi spṛṣṭā cet śuddhimācaret ||

na dhārayetkare kaṇṭhe mūrdhni ca japamālikām |
urupādādharaspṛṣṭā cāpasavyapracālitā ||

aguptā cālitā vāpi punaḥ saṃskāramarhati |
japamālā mayā devi kathitā bhuvi durlabhā |
sadā gopyā prayatnena yadi tvāṃ mama vallabhā ||

atha varṇamālā

mālā pañcāśikā proktā sūtraṃ śaktiśivātmakam |
kuṇḍalīgrathitā śaktiralānte mekasaṃsthitiḥ ||

citriṇī visatantvābhā brahmanāḍīgatāntarā |
tayā saṃgrathitā dhyeyā sākṣājjāgratsvarūpiṇī ||

antarvidrumabhāsamānabhujagīsaprotavarṇojvalā |
ārohapratirohataḥ śatamayī vargāṣṭakāṣṭottarā ||

anulomavilomena mantravarṇavibhedataḥ |
mantreṇā'ntaritānvarṇānvarṇenā'ntaritaṃ manum ||

kuryādvarṇamayī mālāṃ sarvamantra prakāśinīm |
caramārṇaṃ merurūpaṃ laṃghanaṃ naiva kārayet ||

sabinduṃ varṇamuccārya paścānmantraṃ japetsutdhīḥ |
akārādilakārāntamanuloma iti smṛtam ||

punarlakāramārabhya śrīkaṇṭhāntaṃ manuṃ japet |
viloma iti vikhyātaṃ kṣakāraṃ kevalaṃ japet ||

varṇānāmaṣṭavargeṇa aṣṭavāraṃ japetsudhīḥ |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mantraṃ jñānena saṃjapetsudhīḥ |
a ka ca ha ta pa ya śā ityevaṃ cāṣṭavargakāḥ ||


iti śrīparivrājakācārya paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ mālānirṇayo 
nāmā'ṣṭamollāsaḥ || 8 ||




navamollāsaḥ

japavidhiḥ

japavidhimahaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalānane |
japārthaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ vinyāsañca lipervinā ||

kṛtaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ vidyāttasmādādau nyasetpriye |
japādau ca japānte ca prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret ||

mānasādi japabhedaḥ

viśuddheśvare �

japaḥ syādakṣarāvṛttirmānasopāṃśuvācikāḥ |
nijakarṇagocaro yo mānasaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

upāṃśurnijakarṇasya gocaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
nigadastu janairvedyastrividho japa īritaḥ ||

anyatrāpi �

yadyuccanīcoccaritaiḥ spaṣṭaśabdavadakṣaraiḥ |
mantramuccārayedvyaktaṃ japayajñaḥ sa vācikaḥ ||

uccārayenmantramīṣat kiñcidoṣṭau pracālayan |
kiñcicchabdamayaṃ brūyādupāṃśuḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

dhiyā yadakṣaraśroṇyā varṇādvarṇaṃ padātpadam |
śabdānucintanābhyāsaḥ sa ukto mānaso japaḥ ||

uccairjapād viśiṣṭaḥ syādupāṃśurdaśabhirguṇaiḥ |
tasmādapi viśiṣṭaḥ syātsahasraṃ mānaso japaḥ ||

mantrajapapaddhatiḥ

devatāṃ cittagāṃ kuryātkuryācca hṛdayaṃ sthiram |
oṣṭhau tu sampuṭau kṛtvā sthiracittaḥ sthirendriyaḥ ||

dhyāyecca manasā varṇān jihvoṣṭhau na vicālayet |
na kampayecchirogrīvāndantānaiva prakāśayet |
mantroddhārakrameṇaiva mantraṃ japati sādhakaḥ ||

tadā siddhiṃ vijānīta na siddhiścānyathā bhavet ||

	mantroddhārakrameṇaiva mantraghaṭakībhūta svaravyañjana 
varṇajñāna krameṇetyarthaḥ | eva-kāro'vadhāraṇārthaḥ |

ādau dhyānaṃ tato mantraṃ dhyānasyā'nte manuṃ japet |
dhyānamantrasamāyuktaḥ śīghraṃ sidhyati sādhakaḥ ||

kulārṇave �

manasā paṭhitaṃ stotraṃ vācā vāpi manorjapaḥ |
ubhayaṃ niṣphalaṃ devi bhinna bhāṇḍodakaṃ yathā ||

bhūtaśuddhau �

yasya yasya ca mantrasya uddiṣṭā yā ca devatā |
cintayitvā tadākāraṃ manasā japamācaret ||

śanaiḥ śanairavispaṣṭaṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitam |
krameṇoccārayedvarṇānādyantakramayogataḥ ||

atihrasvo vyādhiheturatidīrgho vasukṣayaḥ |
akṣarākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ japenmauktika hāravat ||

kulārṇave �

tanniṣṭhastadgataprāṇastaccittastatparāyaṇaḥ |
tatpadārthānusandhānaṃ kurvanmantraṃ japet priye ||

rudrayāmale �

kathaṃ mantrāśca sidhyanti mantrārthājñāninaḥ priye |
paśurbhāvavihīnaśca na tasya bhajate phalam |
mantrārthasyā'nabhijño hi na japaphalamaśnute ||

mantrārthaḥ mantradevatayorabhedajñānam | tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

mantrārtho devatārūpa cintanaṃ parameśvari |
mantrātmakaśca devaḥ syād mantravācyā ca devatā ||

vācyavācakabhāvenaivā'bhedo mantradevayoḥ |
mantragacyā devatā hi mantro hi vācakaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vācake'pi ca vijñāte vācya eva prasīdati ||

prakārāntaramāha bhūtaśuddhau �

mantrārthaṃ parameśāni sāvadhāna'vadhāraya |
ādhāre cintayedvidyāṃ śuddhasphaṭikasannibhām ||

bandhūkarucirāṃ liṅge nābhau sphaṭika sannibhām |
hṛdi mārakata prakhyāṃ haridvarṇāṃ viśuddhake ||

ājñāyāṃ cintayedvidyāṃ caturvarṇānurañjitām |
ṣaṭcakre parameśāni dhyāyetsādhakasattamaḥ ||

mantrapuraścaraṇavidhiḥ

rudrayāmale �

mantraṃ nītvā guroḥ pārśve gurubhaktipuraḥsaraḥ |
mantraśrotrāsyahṛnnetraprāṇānvijñāya yatnataḥ |
mantrāṇāṃ kīlakaṃ jñātvā kuryānmantrapuraṣkriyām ||

nacaitadvacanaṃ puraścaraṇaviṣayameveti boddhavyam | śrotrādīnāṃ 
jñānābhāve mantrajapamātraniṣedhāt | tathā coktaṃ mantrakoṣe �

śrotrādīnāṃ jñānābhāve mantrajāpaṃ karoti yaḥ |
dāridryañca vipattiñca narakaṃ prāpnuyāttu saḥ ||

anyatrāpi �

hṛnnetravihīno mantro dāridryakleśadāyakaḥ |

tantrāntare �

śroṇāsyanetrahṛdayajñānānmokṣamavāpnuyāt |
sadyaḥ siddhiḥ sarvavidyā syātsākṣācchiva eva saḥ ||

bhūtaḍāmare �

indriyamanoviśuddhyā manorāsyādikaṃ vakṣye |
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi kālīmantratanukramam ||

kālīmantrādīnāṃ tanukramaḥ 

binduṃ śrotraṃ nādamāsyaṃ kakāraṃ hṛdayaṃ biduḥ |
vahniṃ netraṃ kīlakañca dīrghekāraṃ priyaṃvade ||

takāraṃ tāriṇīmantro hṛdayaṃ viddhi pārvati |
hakāraṃ viddhi sarvatra śaktimantre sureśvari ||

uttaratantre �

prāṇavidyā mahāvidyā sā vidyā muktidāyikā |

śyāmāyā dvāviṃśatyakṣaravidyāyāṃ viśeṣamāha uttaratantre �

krīṃ kāromastakaṃ devi krīṃkāraśca lalāṭakam |
netratrayaṃ krīṃkāreṇa hūṃkāreṇa ca nāsikā ||

hūṃkāro mukhapadyaṃ syād hrīṃkāraḥ karṇayugmakam |
hrīṃkāreṇa bhaved grīvā dakāraścidrukaṃ bhavet ||

kṣikāreṇa bhaveddanto ṇekāreṇauṣṭhayugmakam |
kākāreṇa stanadvandvaṃ likāraḥ pṛṣṭhadeśakam ||

kekāreṇa bhavedbāhuḥ krīṃkāreṇodaraṃ bhavet |
krīṃkāro nābhideśaḥ syāt krīṃkāraśca nitambakam ||

hūṃkāro yonirūpaḥ syād hūṃkāreṇoruyugmakam |
hrīṃkāro jānuyugmaṃ syād hrīṃkāro gulphadeśakaḥ |
svākāreṇa padadvandvaṃ hākāreṇa nakhāstathā ||

tārāvidyāyāṃ yāmale �

vāgdevyāḥ samudāyaḥ syādākṛtiḥ praṇavomukham |
māyāvadhusthitau bindu locane samudāhṛte ||

hasakāro śrutī dīrghasvarau hṛdayarūpiṇau |
phaṭakārau yonyudarāvakāreṇa stana dvayam ||

rephayugmaṃ padadvandvaṃ takāraṃ mālalocanam |
vedabhujasvarūpañca nādayugmamudāhṛtam ||

kūrcaṃ prāṇā ekajaṭā śarīraṃ sarvamiṣyate |
kūrcaṃ mukhantu vijñeyamanyamantreṣu pārvati ||

	anya mantreṣu ekajaṭā yā praṇavarahitamantreṣu kūrcaṃ mukhaṃ 
tena tattanmantraghaṭakībhūta 
tattadvarṇotpannamukhanāsauṣṭhadantādharahastapādastanayonyādya-
vayavāvacchinnaśarīraṃ jñānaviṣayīkṛtya japedityarthaḥ ||

kāminītattvam

kāmadhenutantre �

athānyatsaṃpravakṣyāmi kāminītattvamadbhutam |
śṛṇu tattvaṃ maheśāni kakārasyā'tidurlabham ||

rahasyaṃ paramāścaryaṃ trikoṇānāñca saṃśṛṇu |
vāmarekhā bhaved brahmāviṣṇurdakṣiṇarekhikā ||

adhorekhā bhaved rudro mātrā sākṣātsarasvatī |
kuṇḍalī cāṃkuśākārā madhyaśūnyaṃ sadāśivaḥ ||

jvāyāvakasaṅkāśā vāmarekhā varānane |
śaraccandrapratīkāśā daśarekhā samurtikā ||

adhorekhā bhaved rudro mahāmarakatadyutiḥ |
śaṅkha dugdhasamābhāsā mātrā sākṣātsarasvatī ||

aṃkuśākuṇḍalī yā tu koṭividyullatākṛtiḥ |
koṭicandrapratīkāśo madhyaśūnyaṃ sadāśivaḥ ||

śūnyeṣu parameśāni sarvavyāpī sadāśivaḥ |
iśvaro yastu deveśi kalācatuṣṭyātmakaḥ ||

icchāśaktirbhaved brahmā viṣṇustu jñānaśaktimān |
kriyāśaktirbhavedrudraḥ sarvaḥ prakatimūrtimān ||

ātmavidyāśivaistattvaiḥ sadāśivaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
śūnyeṣu saṃsthitā kālī kaivalyapadadāyinī ||

mardinī saṃsthitā tasya dakṣabhāge samūrtikā |
vāmabhāge sthitā lakṣmīścaturvargapradāyinī ||

tāsāṃ garbhe sthitā sā ca sundarī paradevatā |
tisṛṇāṃ garbhasambhūtā tripurā cāta eva hi ||

paramātmasvarūpatvāttāsāṃ garbhe pratiṣṭhitā |
anye ca kālikāyāḥ syuḥ sarvā bhedāśca pārvati ||

tatra sthitvā sṛjed brahmā viṣṇuḥ pālanatatparaḥ |
rudraḥ saṃhārakartā ca īśvarastusadāśivaḥ ||

īśvaro yastu deveśi trikoṇe tasyasthitiḥ |
trikoṇametatkathitaṃ yonimaṇḍalamuttamam ||

kakārājjāyaye devi sarvañca varavarṇini |
kakārātsarvamutpannaṃ kāmaḥ kaivalyameva ca ||

artho'pi jāyate devi so'pi dharmabalād tathā |
sarvāsāṃ devatānāñca kakāro mūlameva ca ||

āsanaṃ tripurādevyāḥ kakāraḥ pañcadaivataḥ |
kakārātkāmadā kāmarūpiṇī sphuradavyayā ||

mātā sā sarvadevānāṃ kaivalyapadadāyinī |
kaivalyaṃ prapade ya(syāḥ)smātkāminī sā prakīrtitā ||

kāminī dhyānam

javāyāvakasindūrasadṛśīṃ kāminīṃ parām |
caturbhujāṃ trinetrāñca bāhuvallī virājitām ||

kadamba korakākārastanadvayavibhūṣitām |
śaṅkhakaṅkaṇakeyūrairaṅgadairupaśobhitām ||

ratnahāraiḥ puṣpahāraiḥ śobhitāṃ parameśvarīm |
evaṃ hi kāminīṃ dhyātvā kakāraṃ daśadhā japet ||

praphullañca tato japtvā japasya phalabhāg bhavet |
etatte kathitaṃ devi kakāratattvamadbhutam ||

etattu kālikābījaṃ praphullaṃ śṛṇu sundari |
pṛthvībījaṃ tato dhṛtvā vāmākṣisaṃyutaṃ kuru ||

bindvarddhasaṃyuto bhūtvā praphullaṃ bhavati priye |
lakāraḥ pṛthivī sākṣātsarvaratnapradāyinī ||

pītāṅgīṃ pītavasanāṃ pītavidyullatākṛtim |
sukhaprasannavadanāṃ ratnakuṇḍala maṇḍitām ||

evaṃ hi saṃsmared bījaṃ tadūrdhve kāminī parām |
kakāra saṃyutaṃ kṛtvā praphullaṃ bhāvayetpriye ||

mardinī yā maheśāni sā vāmā parameśvari |
prataptakāñcanābhāsāṃ daśabāhusamānvitām ||

tribhaṅgalalitākārāṃ jaṭājūṭavibhūṣitām |
trilocanāṃ candrarekhāṃ mahiṣāsuramardinīm |
siṃhāsanagatāṃ devīṃ māvaye dvaiṣṇavottagaḥ ||

bahurūpamayīṃ devīṃ kakāraṃ kāminī parām |
śuklavarṇāṃ raktavarṇāṃ (pītavarṇāṃ) pītacampakahāsinīm ||

haridvarṇāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ nānācitrasvarūpiṇīm |
utpatteḥ kāraṇaṃ bhūmerdevānāṃ caiva pārvati ||

bījametanmahāguhyaṃ viṣṇorjanmasthalaṃ sadā |
tadūrdhve nādatattvañca yonirūpāṃ sanātanīm ||

prataptakāñcanābhāsāṃ trikoṇāṃ śaśiśekharām |
śṛṅgārarasasandohaiḥ pūri(ji)tāṃ parameśvarīm ||

tadūrdhve bhāvayedbinduṃ śivaśaktimayaṃ sadā |
śūnyarūpaḥ śivaḥ sākṣādbinduḥ paramakuṇḍalī ||

śūnyabhāgaḥ kalāyukto binduśca mokṣado'vyayaḥ |
sārddhatrivalayākārā koṭividyutsamaprabhā ||

sarpākārā śivaṃ veṣṭya tatraiva saṃsthitā sadā |
evaṃ hi saṃsmaredbhaktyā bījaśaktisamāśritām ||

bījāttu jāyate brahmā jñānātmā parameśvaraḥ |
śabda brahmamayo bhūtvā īśvaraḥ kāryakāraṇam ||

kṛṣṇasya cañcalāpāṅgi mātā sā kāminī parā |
bījāccaivā'ṅkure jāte bījaṃ niṣphalatāṃ vrajet ||

etadbījaṃ varārohe sadā sāramayaṃ vibhu |
lakārasaṃyutaṃ bhūtvā prasūte harimavyayam ||

svayaṃ śaktirharirbhūtvā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kakārasaṃyutā bhūtvā śaktirāvirabhūtsvayam ||

janmakarmāṇi sarvāṇi prakṛterasti bhāmini |
jape dhyāne ca pūjāyāṃ prakṛtiḥ supratiṣṭhitā ||

kakārasyordhvakoṇe tu prāṇo vāyuḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
apāno vāmakoṇe ca saṃsthitaśca sadā priye ||

samānodakṣiṇe koṇe śuddhasphaṭika sannibhaḥ |
udānastvaṃśākāre mātrāyāṃ vyāna eva ca |
etatte kathitaṃ devi kakāratattvamadbhutam ||

navatattvaṃ kakārasya jñātvā yaḥ kurute japam |
sa japaścañcalāpāṅgi japa eva na saṃśayaḥ ||

etattatvamavijñāya prajapedyadi koṭidhā |
na tajjaptaṃ varārohe sadā tvāvartanaṃ bhavet ||

navatattvanirūpaṇam

devatattvaṃ prāṇatattvaṃ bindutattvañca sundari |
jñānatattvaṃ śaktitattvaṃ yonitattvaṃ tathaiva ca ||

navatattvamidaṃ proktaṃ kāmadhenumataṃ priye |
kīlito nahidevo hi vidyā mantraśca sarvathā ||

na śaptaḥ parameśāni na viddho varavarṇini |
sarveṣāṃ jaṅgamādīnāṃ sthāvarāṇāntu yogini ||

devatā mātṛkā māyā sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇī |
ekākṣaravihīnatvād brahmahatyā varānane ||

kasya syādvaśagā devī hṛdaye bhāvayetpriye |
bhāvanādakṣaraśreṇyāḥ sākṣād brahma na saṃśayaḥ ||

akṣare dūṣaṇaṃ nāsti śaptādi kamalānane |
dūṣaṇaṃ yatkṛtaṃ devi hṛdisthaṃ bhāvayetpriye ||

rakṣaṇārthaṃ surāṇāñca hyātmano gopanāya ca |
mānavāḥ parameśāni varākāḥ kṣudrabuddhayaḥ ||

mānavasya ca rakṣārthaṃ rakṣārthaṃ pannagasya ca |
mayaiva nirmitau devi vidyāmantrau puraiva hi ||

ata-eva maheśāni hyasurāḥ kṣayamāgatāḥ |
tasmātsarva prayatnena japaṃ kuru varānane ||

mantrārthanirūpaṇam

yāmale �

devatāyāḥ śarīrantu bījādutpadyate dhruvam |

bhūtaśuddhau �

dhyānena parameśāni yadrūpaṃ samupasthitam |
tadeva parameśāni mantrārthaṃ viddhi pārvati ||

mantrasthānamāha tantre �

sthānasthā varadāmantrā dhyānasthāśca phalapradāḥ |
dhyānasthānavinirmuktāḥ susiddhā api vairiṇaḥ ||

mantrasthānam

mantrasthānaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi varānane |
sakalaṃ niṣphalaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tathā sakalaniṣphalam ||

kalābhinnaṃ kalābhītaṃ ṣoḍhāmantraṃ śivo'vravīt |
sakalaṃ brahmarandhrasthaṃ tadadho viddhi niṣphalam ||

mānasaḥ sūkṣmamākhyāto hṛtsthaḥ sakalaniṣphalaḥ |
bindusthitaḥ kalābhinnaḥ kalātītastadūrdhvataḥ ||

kalā kuṇḍalinī caiva nādaḥ śaktiḥ śivoditā |
ṣaṭkasthānasthitā mantrāḥ sthānasthāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

dhyānantu svaravyañjanabhedena 
mantraghaṭakībhūtavarṇacintanamevetyarthaḥ | 

bhūtaśuddhau �

caitanyarahitaṃ mantraṃ yo japetsa ca pāpakṛt |
mantrāścaitanyasahitāḥ sarvasiddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||

mantracaitanyādinirūpaṇam

caitanyaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ śṛṇuṣva kamalānane |
sahasrāraṃ śivapuraṃ kalpavṛkṣaṃ manoharam ||

caturveda catuḥśākhaṃ nityaṃ puṣpaphalānvitam |
pītaṃ raktaṃ tathā śvetaṃ kṛṣṇañca haritaṃ tathā ||

bhramaraiḥ kokilairdevi bahupuṣpopaśobhitam |
evaṃ kalpadrumaṃ dhyātvā tadadhoratnavedikām ||

tatropari maheśāni paryaṅkaṃ sumano haram |
nānāpuṣpasaṃyutayā racitaṃ hemamālayā ||

tatropari mahādevaṃ mahākuṇḍalinīyutam |
evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ mahādevi trivargadām ||

ānandāśraṇi pulako dehāveśaḥ sureśvari |
gadgadoktiśca sahasā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

sakṛduccarite'pyevaṃ mantre caitanyasaṃyute |
śate sahasre lakṣe vā koṭijāpena tatphalam ||

ityetatkathitaṃ devi mantracaitanyamuttamam |
viṣṇumantre tathā śaive śaktimantre sureśvari |
mantrārthaṃ mantracaitanyaṃ yatnataḥ samupācaret ||

yonimudrā

yonimudrāmāha tantramuktāvalyāṃ �

upaviśyāsane mantrīpraṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ |

ṣaṭcakraṃ cintayeddevi prāṇāyāmapuraḥsaram ||

caturdalaṃ syādādhāraṃ svādhiṣṭhānantu ṣaḍdalam |
nābhau daśadalaṃ padyaṃ sūryasaṃkhyadalaṃ hṛdi ||

kaṇṭhe syātṣoḍaśadalaṃ bhrūmadhye dvidalaṃ tathā |
sahasradalamākhyātaṃ brahmarandhre mahāpathe ||

ādhāre kandamadhyasthaṃ trikoṇamatisundaram |
trikoṇamadhye deveśi kāmabījaṃ sulakṣaṇam ||

kāmabījodbhavaṃ tatra svayambhūliṅgamuttamam |
tasyopari punardhyāyet citkalāṃ haṃsamāśritām ||

dhyāyetkuṇḍalinīṃ devīṃ svayambhūliṅga veṣṭitām |
citkalāṃ tāṃ kuṇḍalitāṃ tejorūpāṃ jaganmayīm ||

ādhārādīni padmāni bhittvā tejaḥ svarūpiṇīm |
haṃsena manunā devīṃ brahmarandhraṃ nayetsudhīḥ ||

sadāśivena devī sā kṣaṇamātraṃ rametpriye |
amṛtaṃ jāyate devi tatkṣaṇātparameśvari ||

tadudbhavāmṛtaṃ devi lākṣārasa samopamam |
tenāmṛtena deveśi tarpayetparadevatām ||

ṣaṭcakradevatāstatra santarpyā'mṛtadhārayā |
ānayottena māgreṇa mūlādhāraṃ punaḥ sudhīḥ ||

tatastu parameśāni akṣamālāṃ vicintayet |
citriṇī visatantvābhā brahmanāḍīgatāntarā ||

tayā saṃgrathitā madhye sākṣājjāgratsvarūpiṇī |
anuloma vilomena mantravarṇavibhedataḥ ||

mantreṇā'ntaritānvarṇānvarṇenā'ntaritaṃ manum |
kuryādvarṇamayīṃ mālāṃ sarvamantraprakāśinīm ||

caramārṇaṃ merurūpaṃ laṃghanaṃ naiva kārayet |
sabinduṃ varṇamuccārya paścānmantraṃ japetsudhīḥ ||

akārādi lakārāntamanuloma iti smṛtam |
punarlakāramārabhya śrīkaṇṭhāntaṃ manuṃ japet ||

viloma iti vikhyātaṃ kṣakāraṃ kevalaṃ japet |
varṇānāmaṣṭavarṇena tvaṣṭavāraṃ japetsudhīḥ ||

a ka ca ṭa ta pa ya śā ityevaṃ cāṣṭavargakāḥ |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mūlamantraṃ jñānena saṃjapet |
yonimudrā maheśāni tava snehātprakāśitā ||

mantrārthaṃ mantracaitanyaṃ yonimudrāṃ navetti yaḥ |
śatakoṭijapenā'pi tasya siddhirna jāyate ||

iti yonimudrā 

mantraśikhānirūpaṇam 

mantraśikhāmāha yāmale �

śṛṇudevi pravakṣyāmi sarvajñānottamottamam |
yasya vijñānamātreṇa kṣipraṃ vidyā prasīdati ||

mūlakande tu yā devī bhujagākārarūpiṇī |
tadbhramāvartavāto yaḥ prāṇa ityucyate budhaiḥ ||

bhillīvā'vyaktamadhurā kujanto satatotthitā |
gacchantī brahmarandhreṇa praviśanti svaketanam ||

yātāyātakrameṇaiva tatra kuryānmanotnayam |
tena mantraśikhā jātā sarvamantrapradīpikā ||

tamaḥpūrṇe gṛhe yadvat na kiñcitpratibhāsate |
śikhāhīnāstathā mantrā na siddhyanti kadācana ||

śikhāpadeśaḥ sarvatra gopitaḥ parameśvari |
vinā yena na siddhiḥ syādvarṣakoṭiśatairapi |
tasmāttvayāpi girije gopanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||

aśaucabhaṅgaḥ

rudrayāmale �

jātasūcakamādau syādante ca mṛtasūtakam |
sūtakadvayasaṃyukto na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ ||

	japādau jātasūtakaṃ japānte ca mṛtasūtakamiti 
sūtakadvayamityarthaḥ |

yāmale �

brahmabījaṃ manordattvā cādyante parameśvari |
saptavāraṃ japenmantraṃ sūtakadvayamuktaye ||

	sūtakadvayamuktaye manorādyante brahmabījaṃ praṇavaṃ dattvā 
japādau saptavāraṃ japānte ca saptavāraṃ taṃ manuṃ japedityarthaḥ |

sūtakadvayamukto yaḥ sa mantraḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ |
caturdaśa svaro devi puṇyaḥ siddhi pradāyakaḥ |
nādabindusamopeto dīrgha praṇava ucyate ||

strīśūdrāṇāmaśaicabhaṅgaḥ

tantroktaḥ praṇavaḥ so'pi strīśūdrāṇāṃ praśasyate |
tasmātstrīṇāñca śūdrāṇāṃ sa eva parikīrtitaḥ ||

bhūtaśuddhau �

tantroktaṃ praṇavaṃ devi vahnijāyāṃ sureśvari |
prajapetsatataṃ śūdro nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||

	tena praṇavapuṭitamantrajapasthale strī śūdraśca oṃkārapuṭitaṃ 
kṛtvaiva 

mantraṃ japet | tantroktā vahnijāyā tu hṛdayameva (namaḥ) svāhāsthāne 
ca namaḥ padaṃ prayojya nyāsajapādikaṃ kuryāditi jñeyam ||

gaṇanāvidhiḥ

gaṇanavidhimāhayāmale �

gaṇanāvidhimullaṃghya yo japet tajjapaṃ yataḥ |
gṛhṇanti rākṣasāstena gaṇayetsarvathā budhaḥ ||

nākṣatairhastaparvairvā na dhānyairna ca puṣpakaiḥ |
na candanairmṛtikayā japasaṃkhyāṃ tu kārayet ||

lākṣākuśīdasindūraṃ gomayañca karīṣakam |
viloḍya guṭikāṃ kṛtvā japasaṃkhyāṃ tu kārayet ||

daśamollāsaḥ

mahāsetunirūpaṇam

atha mahāsettvādīnāṃ prayojanamāha �

mahāsetuṃ vinā devi yo japet satu pāpabhāk |
ādau japtvā mahāsetuṃ tataḥ setuṃ tato manum |
evaṃ kramairvarārohe yatheṣṭaṃ japamācaret ||

setumaṅgalatantre �

yo japetparameśāni vinā setuṃ mahāmanum |
tasya sarvārthahāniḥ syānmṛte ca narakaṃ vrajet ||

mahāsetuḥ

mahāsetumāha yāmale �

mahāsetuśca deveśi sundaryā bhuvaneśvarī |
kālikāyāḥ svabījantu tārāyā kūrca ucyate |
anyeṣāntu vadhūbījaṃ mahāseturvarānane ||

vadhūbījamāha rudrayāmale �

ākāśādyaṃ caturthādyaṃ yakārāntaṃ ca saṃhatam |
lakṣmībinduyutaṃ devi vadhūbījamudāhṛtam ||

	ākāśādyaṃ sakāraḥ caturthādyaṃ takāraḥ, yakārāntaṃ rephaḥ 
| saṃhataṃ etattritaya saṃyuktam | lakṣmīḥ īkāraḥ binduranusvāraḥ, 
tābhyāṃ yutam | etena strīmiti |

mahāsetuṃ vinā devi na japtavyaṃ kadācana |
śatakoṭijapenā'pi tasya siddhirna jāyate ||

setunirūpaṇam

setumantraṃ maheśāni sarveṣāṃ kullukāṃ śṛṇu |
setuvidyā maheśāni sākṣād brahmasvarūpiṇī ||

ādāvante ca deveśi japettāṃ tu japānmanoḥ |
tataḥ siddho bhaveddevi mantro vidyā viśeṣataḥ ||

anyathā viphalaṃ devi niścayaṃ vacanaṃ mama |
pārśvayoḥ setumādhāya japakarma samācaret ||

niḥsetusalilaṃ yadvatkṣaṇānnimnaṃ prasarpati |
mantrastathaiva niḥsetuḥ kṣaṇātkṣarati yajvanām ||

	ādāvanteceti | manorjapāt ādau tadante ca setuvidyāṃ jape dityarthaḥ 
|

samānyasetuḥ

sāmānyasetumāha �

viprāṇāṃ praṇavaḥ setuḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tathaiva ca |
vaiśyānāṃ tu phaḍarṇaḥ syānmāyā śūdrasya kathyate ||

viśeṣasetuḥ

viśeṣasetumāha yāmale �

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sundaryāḥ setumuttamam |

punarmāyāṃ samuddhṛtya vidyeyaṃ tryakṣarī parā |
sundarīviṣaye setuḥ kathitaḥ parameśvari ||

sau svarūpam | bhāgyaṃ visargaḥ | mantro yathā � hrīṃ sauḥ hrīṃ |

	iyaṃ vidyā varārohe bhairavyāḥ seturūpiṇī | mantro yathā � hsa |

	eṣā ca dvyakṣarī vidyā tārāyāḥ seturucyate || mantro yathā � oṃ 
hrīṃ |

mahiṣamardinyāḥ �

atha vakṣye maheśāni mardinyāḥ setumuttamam |
haṃsa-(varṇaṃ)rūpaṃ samuddhṛtya raṃjinyupari saṃsthitam ||

	tryakṣarīyaṃ setuvidyā mardinyāḥ parikīrtitā | mantro yathā � 
hrīṃ svāhā |

viṣṇoḥ �

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya vāsavā dyaṃ tataḥ param |
indirāsaṃyutaṃ kuryādyatnataḥ parameśvari ||

p.207) ṣṇātmakamakṣaraṃ coktvā tataḥ paramudīrayet |
bālibījaṃ samuddhṛtya edhitā sayutaṃ kuru ||

	punaḥ praṇavamuddhṛtya viṣṇoḥ setuḥ śucismite | mantro yathā - oṃ 
viṣṇeva oṃ

śrīkṛṣṇasya �

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya manmathaṃ tadanantaram |
punaḥ praṇavamuddhṛtya setumantraṃ manoharam ||

	śrīkṛṣṇaviṣaye setustryakṣaraḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || mantro yathā - oṃ 
klīṃ oṃ

rāmasya �

śṛṇu kamalapatrākṣi setuṃ rāmasya sundaram |
praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya rājahaṃsaṃ tataḥ param ||

ācārya saṃyutaṃ kṛtvā bindvarddhasaṃyutaṃ kuru |
punaḥ praṇavamuddhṛtya vidyeyaṃ seturūpiṇī ||

tryakṣarīyaṃ mahāvidyā setuvidyā prakīrtitā | mantro yathā - oṃ rāṃ oṃ

śivasya �

haṃsayuktaṃ samuddhṛtya samudrarūpiṇaṃ tataḥ |
candrārddha saṃyutaṃ devi kuru yatnena pārvati |
eṣā ca dvyakṣarī vidyā śivasya seturūpiṇī ||

mantro yathā � haṃsaḥ |

anyeṣu devodeveṣu praṇavaḥ seturucyate |
sarveṣāṃ śūdrajātīnāmauṅkāraḥ seturucyate ||

atha kavacasetuḥ

yatra yatra vinirdiṣṭaḥ setumantraḥ śucismite |
tanmantraṃ triguṇaṃ kṛtvā setumantraṃ kuru priye ||

kavacasya maheśāni seturbhavati sundari |
setuṃ vinā maheśāni kavacaṃ yaḥ paṭhennaraḥ ||

sa bhakṣye jāyate devi yoginīnāṃ śucismite |
vaiṣṇave gāṇapatye ca śaive śākte śucismite |
ādāvante mahāsetuṃ dattvā tu kavacaṃ paṭhet ||

mukhaśodhanam

mukhaśodhanamāha sārasvatatantre �

mukhaśodhana mātreṇa jihvā'mṛtamayī bhavet |
anyathā viṣasaṃyuktā jihvā bhavati sarvadā ||

bhakṣaṇe dūṣitā jihvā mithyāvādena dūṣitā |
kalahairduḥkhitā jihvā nānādoṣeṇa dūṣitā ||

taṃ kathaṃ pāmaro loko jihvāyāṃ prajapenmanum |
saṃśodhanamanācarya na japetpāmaraḥ kvacit ||

śākto vā vaiṣṇavo vāpi gāṇapaḥ saura eva vā |
śaivo vā'pyanyabhakto vā kārayenmukhaśodhanam |

devo yadi japenmantramakṛtvā mukhaśodhanam | 
sarvaṃ tasya vṛthā devi mantrasiddhirna jāyate ||

tasmātprayatnato devi jihvāśodha mācaret |
anyathā prajapenmantramakṛtvā mukhaśodhanam ||

patanaṃ tasya deveśi yo japetsa ca pāpabhāk |
tasyātprayatnato devi jihvāśodhana mācaret ||

iti sārasvata tantroktamukhaśodhana vidhānam |

nidrābhaṅga

devyuvāca �

pūjākāle maheśāna yadi nidrāturo manuḥ |
tatkathaṃ sidhyate mantraḥ kiṃ kartavyaṃ tadā prabho ||

prajapetkena vidhinā na japedvā vada prabho |
nidrāyāścaiva deveśa lakṣaṇaṃ vada me prabho ||

īśvara uvāca �

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi |
iḍāyāñca gate vāyau śaktimantraṃ japet priye |
rātrau japaikamātreṇa caṇḍikā varadā bhavet ||

rudrayāmale �

piṅgalāyāṃ gate vāyau tadā nidrāturaḥ priye |
iḍāyāñca gate vāyau tadā nidrāturo manuḥ ||

etatte kathitaṃ devi nidrāyā lakṣaṇaṃ priye |
prajapedyadi nidrāyā kiṃ tasya japa pūjane ||

sarvaṃ tasya vṛthā devi araṇye rodanaṃ yathā |
rahasyānena cārvaṅgi tyaktanidrā sanātanī ||

nidrābhaṅgamantraḥ

ādaukāmakalābījaṃ svamantrānte'pi tad japet |
prāyaścittamidaṃ devi kṛtvā mantraṃ japedyadi |
kiṃ tasya dakṣiṇo vāyustasya nidrātureṇa kim ||

mantravidyālakṣaṇam

viśvasāre �

	yonimantra īṅkāraḥ | tāmyāṃ puṭiṭaṃ mūlaṃ saptavāraṃ

tathācoktam �

yonimantraḥ

yonimantramāha yāmale �

tūryasvaro binduyuto nādena paribhūṣitaḥ |

dvādaśollāsaḥ

puraścaraṇalakṣaṇam

puraścaraṇalakṣaṇamāha haṃsamāheśvare �

japo homastarpaṇañca seko brāhmaṇabhojanam |
pañcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamiṣyate ||

yāmale �

pañcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke śāktavaiṣṇava bhedataḥ |
puraścaraṇamityuktaṃ śivena paramātmanā ||

puraścaraṇa prayojanam

yāmale �

jīvahīno yathā dehī sarvakarmasuna kṣamaḥ |
puraścaraṇahīno'pi tathā mantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
tasmādau svayaṃ kuryād guruṃ vā kārayed budhaḥ ||

rudrayāmale � puraścaraṇa sampanno mantro hi phaladāyakaḥ |
tataḥ puraskriyāṃ kuryād mantravit siddhikāṃkṣayā ||

kiṃ homaiḥ kiṃ japaiścaiva kiṃ mantranyāsa vistaraiḥ |
rahasyānāñca mantrāṇāṃ yadi na syātpuraskriyā |
puraskriyā hi mantrāṇāṃ pradhāno jīva (bījamu) ucyate ||

puraścaraṇapūrvadinakṛtyam

haviṣyenaiva bhoktavyaṃ kṛtvā dehaviśodhanam |
prātaḥ snātvā tu sāvitrīṃ japetpañcasahasrakam ||

trisahasraṃ sahasraṃ vā japedaṣṭottaraṃ śuciḥ |
jñātājñātasya pāpasya kṣayārthaṃ prathamaṃ tataḥ |
viprānsantoṣayedannabhojanācchādanāsanaiḥ ||

tatrādau bhūmiparigrahaḥ kāryaḥ | taduktaṃ vaiśampāyana saṃhitāyām 
�

ādāvamukamantrasya puraścaraṇasiddhaye |
mayeyaṃ gṛhyate bhūmirmantro me sidhyatāmiti ||

bhūmeḥ parigrahaṃ kuryāt parimāṇācca sarvaśaḥ |
grāme krośamitaṃ sthānaṃ nadyādau svecchayā mitam ||

nagarādā vapi krośaṃ krośayugmamathāpi vā |
āhārādivihārārthaṃ tavatīṃ bhūmimāśrayet |
dīpasthānaṃ samāśritya kṛtaṃ karma phalapradam ||

dīpasthānam

dīpyate puruṣo yatra dīpasthāna taducyate |
caturasrāṃ bhuvaṃ bhittvā koṣṭhānāṃ navakaṃ likhet ||

pūrvakoṣṭhādiṣu likhetsaptavargānanukramāt |
lakṣamīśe likhenmantrī svaranyāsakramaṃ śṛṇu ||

punarevaṃ madhyakoṣṭhaṃ navadhā vibhajetsudhīḥ |
madhya pūrvādikoṣṭheṣu svarānyugmakramāllikhet ||

yatra pūrvādikoṣṭheṣu grāmādyākṣara saṃsthitiḥ |
mukhantu tasya jānīyād hastāvubhayataḥ sthitau ||

koṣṭhe kukṣī ubhe pādau dve śiṣṭaṃ pucchamīritam |
mukhastho labhate siddhiṃ karasthaḥ svalpajīvanaḥ ||

udāsīnaḥ kukṣisaṃsthaḥ pādastho duḥkhamāpnuyāt |
pucchasthaḥ pīḍyate mantrī bandhanoccāṭanādibhiḥ ||

kūrmacakramidaṃ proktaṃ mantriṇāṃ siddhidāyakam |
nirmāya vidhivatkuryād japaṃ tatra śubhe dine ||

candratārānukūle ca śuklapakṣe śubhe'hani |
ārabheta puraścaryāṃ harau supte na cācaret ||

	harau supte dakṣiṇāyane ityarthaḥ | tena yaddakṣiṇāyanaṃ 
niṣiddhamuktaṃ, tadviṣṇuviṣayam | śaktiviṣaye dakṣiṇāyane'pi 
puraścaraṇaṃ kartavyam | tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

śaratkāle mahāpūjā kriyate yā ca vārṣikī |
tasminpakṣe viśeṣeṇa puraścaraṇa mācaret ||

anyatrāpi �

śaratkāle caturthyādinavamāntaṃ viśeṣataḥ |

bhaktitaḥ pūjayitvā tu rātrau cāṣṭasahasrakam |
ekākī nirjane deśe japeñca timirālaye ||

	aṣṭasahasrakamiti | aṣṭādhikasahasraṃ pratyahaṃ japedityarthaḥ | 
timirālaye andhakāravad gṛhe, na tvāloka yukte ityarthaḥ | kecittu timirālaye 
rātrāviti vadanti | tadasat rātrau cāṣṭasahasrakamiti rātreḥ pūrvaṃ 
prāptatvāditi dik ||

atha puraścaraṇadina-kṛtyam

bahubhiḥ rvastrabhuṣābhiḥ sampūjya gurumātmanaḥ |
ārabhet japaṃ paścāt tadanujñāpuraḥ saram ||

prātaḥ snātvā maheśāni kīlānādāya sādhakaḥ |
kuṭīnikaṭa māgatya kuryāttantroditāṃ kriyām ||

kṣīrivṛkṣodbhavān kīlānastramantrābhimantritān |
nikhaneddaśadigbhāge teṣvastrañca prapūjayet ||

kṣetre tu kīlite mantro na vighnaiḥ paribhūyate |
aśvatthoḍumbaraplakṣavaṭāśca kṣīriśākhinaḥ ||

kṣetrapālānpūjayitvā baliṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ |
dikpatibhyo baliṃ dattvā tataḥ kṣetraṃ samāśrayet ||

kṣetrapalamantramāha tantre �

varṇānta au binduyutaḥ kṣetrapālāya hṛnmanuḥ |
tārādyo vasuvarṇo'yaṃ kṣetrapālasya kīrtitaḥ ||

mantraḥ - oṃ kṣauṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ |

ṣaḍdīrghabhājā bījena ṣaḍaṅganyāsa mācaret |

	nīlāñjanādrinibha mūrddhapiśaṅga keśaṃ vṛttogralocanamupātta 
gadākapālam |

	āśāmbaraṃ bhujagabhūṣaṇamugradaṃṣṭraṃ 
kṣetreśamandutamahaṃ praṇamāmi devam ||

	iti dhyātvā kṣetrapalamāvāhya aṣṭadalapade pūjayet |

analākṣamagnikeśaṃ karālaṃ tadanantaram |
ghaṇṭākhaṃ mahā (krodhaṃ) kopaṃ piśitāśanamapyatha ||

piṅgalākṣamūrddhakeśaṃ patreṣu pūrvato'rcayet |
lokapālāṃstadastrāṇi yathāpūrvaṃ prapūjayet ||

tato māṣabhaktabaliṃ dadyāt ||

mantramāha śāradāyām �

pūrvamehidvayaṃ paścād vidu(dvi)ṣi syātsuradvayam |
bhañjaya dvitayaṃ bhūyo tarjaya dvitayaṃ tataḥ ||

tato vighnapadadvandvaṃ mahābhairava tatparam |
kṣetrapālabaliṃ gṛhṇadvayaṃ pāvakasundari |
balimantro'yamākhyātaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ ||

baddhāñjali �

oṃ tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭra mahākā(la)ya kalpānta dahanopama |
bhairavāya namastubhyamanujñāṃ dātumarhasi ||

ityanujñāṃ labdhvā indrādidikpālānpūjayitvā bhāṣabhaktabaliṃ 
dadyāt |

śāradāyām � kuṭīnikaṭamāgatya sāmānyārdhaṃ vidhāya ca |
dvārapūjāṃ vidhāyātha japasthānaṃ viśodhayet ||

vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ matam ||

puraścaraṇa saṃkalpa

sanatkumārasaṃhitāyām �

praṇavaṃ tatsadadyeti māsapakṣatithīrapi |
amukagotro'muko'haṃ mūlamuccārya tatparam ||

siddhikāmo'sya mantrasya iyatsaṃkhyajapaṃ tataḥ |
daśāṃśaṃ havanaṃ homāddaśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tataḥ ||

daśāṃśaṃ mārjanaṃ tasyāddaśāṃśaṃ viprabhojanam |
puraścaraṇamevaṃ hikariṣye prāgudaṅmukhaḥ ||

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyādau prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
ṛṣyādikaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kalpoktanyāsamācaret ||

tataḥ pūjādikaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi japaṃ caret |
śanaiḥ śanairavispaṣṭaṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitam ||

krameṇoccārayedvarṇānādyanta kramayogataḥ |
devatāṃ cittagāṃ kuryātkuryācca hṛdayaḥ sthiram |
prātaḥkālaṃ samārabhya japenmadhyandināvadhi ||

kulārṇave �

yatsaṃkhyā samārabdhaṃ tajjaptavyaṃ dine dine |
nyūnādhikaṃ na kartavya māsamāptaṃ kadācana ||

nyūnātirikta karmāṇi na phalanti kadācana |
yathāvidhi kṛtānyeva tatkarmāṇi phalanti hi ||

snānaṃ trisavanaṃ proktamaśaktau dviḥsakṛcca vā |
mantraṃ sādhayamānastu trisandhyaṃ devamarcayet ||

dvikālamekakālaṃ vā na mantraṃ kevalaṃ japet |
upacārai yathāśakti devatāmanvahaṃ yajet ||

na kṣujjṛmbhaṇahikkādi vikalīkṛtamānasaḥ |
mantrasiddhimavāpnoti tasmādyatnaparo bhavet ||

	yadi daivād jṛmbhaṇādikaṃ bhavati, tadā ācamya prāṇāyāmaṃ 
ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ ca kṛtvā śeṣaṃ japet | sūryaṃ dṛṣṭvā vā japet | tathā 
ca 

yoginī hṛdaye �

patitānāmantyajānāṃ darśane bhāṣaṇe kṛte |
kṣute'dhovāyugamane jṛmbhaṇe japamutsṛjet ||

tathācamya ca tatprāptau prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgakam |
kṛtvā samyagjapeccheṣaṃ yadvā sūryādi darśanam ||

kṛtvā japediti pareṇānvayaḥ | ādipadāddeva brāhmaṇādīnāṃ 
parigrahaḥ ||

śayīta darbhaśayyāyāṃ vinyasya bhuvi cātmanaḥ |
tadvāsaḥ kṣālayennityamanyathā vighnamā (vahet) patet |
na divā śayanaṃ kuryātkukurādīnna saṃspṛśet ||

na seveta striyaṃ māṃsaṃ madhu vā sādhakottamaḥ |
etāni sevamānasya na sidhyanti puraskriyāḥ ||

bhakṣyādi niyamaḥ

kulārṇave � bhuñjāno vā haviṣyānnaṃ śākaṃ yavakameva vā |

payo mūlaṃ phalaṃ vāpi yatra yaccopalabhyate ||

bhikṣāśī vā japed yadvā kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādikṛt |
āmramāmalakañcaiva phalaṃ keśarisambhavam ||

rambhāphalaṃ tintiḍīkaṃ kamalā nāgaraṅgakam |
phalānyetāni bhojyāni tadanyāni vivarjayet ||

vihitaśākaṃ yathā �

kalāyaṃ kālaśākañcavāstukaṃ hilamocikā ||

haviṣyānna lakṣaṇam

haviṣyānnaṃ yathā �

haimantikaṃ sitāsvinnaṃ dhānyamudgāstilā yavāḥ |
kalāyakaṃ gunīvārā vāstukaṃ hilamocikā ||

yaṣṭikā kālaśākañca mūlakaṃ kemuketavat |
lavaṇe saindhava sāmudre gavye ca dadhisarpiṣī ||

payo'nuddhṛtasārañca panasāmraharītakī |
pippalī jīrakañcaiva nāgaraṅgañca tintiḍī ||

kadalī lavalī dhātrī phalānyaguḍamaikṣavam |
atailapakvaṃ munayo haviṣyānnaṃ pracakṣate ||

mūlaṃ kemuka kendūnāṃ varjayedvihitaṃ mune |
dhṛtaṃ dadhi phalaṃ vāpi nārikelaṃ yathocitam ||

haviṣyānnaṃ tathā'śnīyācchaktuṃ yava samudbhavam |
nendriyāṇāṃ yathā vṛddhistathā bhuñjīta sādhakaḥ ||

gṛhasthānāṃ vadānyānāṃ bhikṣāśino'grajanmanām |
puraścaraṇa madhye tu yadisyānmṛtasūtakam ||

tathāpi kṛtasaṅkalpo japaṃ naiva parityajet |
svakalpokta krameṇaiva japaṃ kṛtvā varānane ||

homayettaddaśāṃśena taddaśāṃśena tarpaṇam |
tarpaṇasya daśāṃśena cābhiṣiñcejjaganmayīm ||

abhiṣeka daśāṃśena kuryād brāhmaṇa bhojanam |
gukhe dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā dīnāndhakṛpaṇān bahūn ||

jñātīndvijānparānbhaktyā bhojayecca yathepsitān |
evaṃ kṛtapuraścaryaḥ sādhayediṣṭamātmanaḥ ||

homādi niyamaḥ

gautamīye � japānte pratyahaṃ mantrī homayettaddaśāṃśataḥ |
tarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣekañca tattaddaśāṃśato mune ||

pratyahaṃ bhojayedviprān nyūnādhikyapraśāntaye |
athavā sarvasampūrtau homādikamathācaret ||

athavā hemapatrādau yantraṃ kṛtvā tataḥ param |
pūjayitvā tatra devīṃ parivāra samanvitām ||

tarpaṇa vidhiḥ 

tarpayettāṃ parāṃ devīṃ tatprakāramihocyate |
tarpayitvā gurūnādau mūladevīñca tarpayet ||

mūlānte nāma coccārya tarpayāmi tataḥ param |
svāhā'nte tarpayenmantrī yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vidhānataḥ ||

yoginī hṛdaye � tarpaṇañca prakurvīta dvitīyāntamathoccaran |
ekekamañjaliṃ dattvā tarpayedraśmivṛndakam ||

tarpaṇadravyam

tarpaṇadravyamāha viśuddheśvare �

tarpaṇaṃ cendumattoyaistīrthatoyaistathā punaḥ |
gurūpadiṣṭa vidhinā madhunā vātha tarpayet ||

tantrāntare �

tīrthatoyena dugdhena sarpiṣā madhunā'pi vā |
gandhodakena vā kuryātsarvatra sādhakottamaḥ ||

kālāgurudravaireva vaśayejjagadādikam |
sacandanena toyena saubhāgyaṃ labhate naraḥ ||

toyaiḥ kuṃkamamiśraiśca stambhayedakhilaṃ jagat |
sitamiśritatoyena bṛhaspati samo bhavet ||

karpūrākta jalenaiva surānnākarṣayennaraḥ |
rocanāyuta toyena sarvavighnātpramucyate ||

dhyātvā devīṃ mukhe tasyāstarpaṇañca samācaret |
sarvaśāstreṣu kathitaṃ tarpaṇaṃ śubhadāyakam ||

etattu tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā'bhiṣekaṃ taddaśāṃśataḥ |
ātmānaṃ devabuddhyā tu sampūjya tanmayaḥ sudhīḥ ||

mūlavidyāṃ samuccāryā'mantāṃ ca devatābhidhām |
tadante cābhiṣiñcāmi namo'ntenā'bhiṣecayet ||

iti mantrī svakaṃ mantraṃ cintayitvā svamūrddhani |
abhiṣekaṃ svīyasaṃkhyaṃ vidadhyāt tadanantaram ||

tatra sañcintayeddevīṃ sāṅgāvaraṇadevatām |
kṣipettoyaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ gaṇānsiñcetsakṛtsakṛt ||

abhiṣekaṃ samāpyaivamabhiṣeka daśāṃśataḥ |
brāhmaṇān devabuddhyā ca bhojayetsādhakottamaḥ ||

yāmale � brāhmaṇān bhojayeddevi tathaiva ca kumārikāḥ |
sādhakaḥ paśutāmeti kumārī bhojanāhate ||

tato mantrayutānviprān bhojayeddevatādhiyā |
tataḥ sampūjayedbhaktyā sambhārai vividhairgurum ||

dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyādyathā vibhavavistaraiḥ |
dattvā ca sādhaka śreṣṭhomahāpujāṃ samācaret |
siddhamantro bhavenmantrī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||

tantre �

vibhave sati yo mohānna kuryādvidhivista(raiḥ)ram |
naitatphalamavāpnoti devadrohī sa ucyate ||

aṅgahīnejapavidhiḥ

muṇḍamālāyām-

yadhadaṅgavihīnaṃ syāttatsaṃkhyādviguṇo japaḥ |
kartavyaḥ sāṅgasiddhyarthaṃ tadaśaktena bhaktitaḥ ||

rudrayāmale �

homakarmaṇyaśaktānāṃ viprāṇāṃ dviguṇo japaḥ |
itareṣāntu varṇānāṃ triguṇādika īritaḥ ||

yoginī hṛdaye �

homāśaktau japaṃ kuryād homasya dviguṇo budhaḥ |
brāhmaṇāditrivarṇānāṃ strīṇāṃ saṃkhyā vidhīyate ||

yaṃ varṇamāśritaḥ śūdro dīkṣāṃ kuryādyathepsitām |
tasyastrīnāntu yā saṃkhyā sā saṃkhyā tasya vidyate |
śūdrasya yādṛśī saṃkhyā dviguṇā sā striyaḥ priye ||

anyatrāpi �

yaṃ varṇamāśritaḥ śūdraḥ sa ca tasya vidhiṃ caret |
anāśritasya śūdrasya diksaṃkhyākaḥ samīritaḥ ||

śūdrasya viprabhṛtyasya tatpatnayāḥ sadṛśo japaḥ |
homaśūnyasya viprasya yo japaḥ sa tu tatstriyaḥ |
itareṣāntu varṇānāṃ sarveṣāṃ triguṇādikaḥ ||

	triguṇādika iti homasaṃkhyā triguṇa japaḥ kṣatriyeṇa kāryaḥ | 
vaiśyena caturguṇaḥ śūdreṇa ca pañcaguṇo bodhyaḥ | śūdrasya 
dviguṇaḥ śaktiviṣaye jñeyaḥ | vaiṣṇavānāṃ catuvarṇānāṃ 
caturguṇaṣaḍguṇāṣṭadaśaguṇo boddhavyaḥ | anyathā kutrāpi 
dviguṇādika kutrāpi caturguṇādi iti virodhāpatteḥ |

tathācoktaṃ gautamīye �

homābhāve japaḥ kāryo homasaṃkhyā caturguṇaḥ |
viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāñca rasasaṃkhyāguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ vasusaṃkhyāka eṣāṃ strīṇāmayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |

yāmale �

yadi home na śaktaḥ syātpūjāyāṃ tarpaṇe'pi vā |
tāvatsaṃkhyajapenaiva sarvasiddhiḥ prajāyate ||

yāmale �

kutrāpi yadi hīnaṃ syāddaśakasyā'ṅgakarmaṇi |
tattaddaśaiva karyāṇi daśanyūnaṃ na kārayet ||

yāmale �

lakṣamekaṃ japedvidvān haviṣyāśī sadā śuciḥ |
tatastu taddaśāṃśena homayeddhaviṣā priye ||

tarpayettaddaśāṃśena tīrthatoyena pārvati |
cābhiṣiñcettatastoyaistarpaṇasya daśāṃśataḥ ||

taddaśāṃśaṃ haviṣyānnairbhaktito bhojayed dvijān |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādyathā vibhavavistaraiḥ |
pāśavaḥ kathitaḥ kalpaḥ śṛṇu vīramataḥ param ||

vīrakalpaḥ

muṇḍamālāyāṃ �

matsyamāṃsāśane śaktaḥ kuryānmantrapuraskriyām |
rātrau prāgāsyaḥ śayyāyāṃ prajapellakṣamānataḥ ||

tatastu taddaśāṃśena homayeddhaviṣānale |
daśāṃśaṃ tarpayed dravyairmāṃsamiśraiḥ susādhakaḥ ||

tarpaṇasya daśāṃśena cābhiṣiñcejjaganmayīm |
daśāṃśaṃ bhojayeddevi sādhakaṃ devatāpriyam ||

madhumāṃsañca matsyañca carvaṇañca pradāpayet |
tatastu toṣayedbhaktyā guruṃ svarṇādibhiḥ priye |
etatkalpadvayāddevi mantraḥ sidhyati niścatam ||

atra lakṣapadaṃ svasvakalpoktasaṃkhyāparam | tathācoktaṃ kumārītantre �

tasminkāle sādhakendraḥ svakalpoktaṃ japaṃ caret |

tasminkāle puraścaraṇakāle | yattu kumārītantre �

lakṣamekaṃ japenmantraṃ haviṣyāśī divā śuciḥ |

rātrau tāmbūlapūrāsyaḥ śayyāyāṃ lakṣamānataḥ |
evaṃ lakṣadvayaṃ japtvā taddaśāṃśena mantravit ||

	iti vacanādviśiṣṭa puraścaraṇe lakṣadvayajapa iti vadanti | tantra 
manoramam | yaddine haviṣyāśī, taddine matsyādyaśane 
haviṣyānnavyāghātān nānācārasya prasakteśca | tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

	nānācāro na kartavyo nānācārarato yataḥ | iti vacanāt | 
tasmātkumārī tantrokta vacanasya puraścaraṇadvaye tātparyam | 
etatkalpadvayaṃ divyavīrayoḥ paryāyeṇa kartavyam | divyena tu 
etatkalpadvayaṃ yugapatkartumapi śakyam | yataḥ sa tattvajñānī san 
mānasakriyāvān, ato nāsya mānasakiyayā vāhyakiyāyā virodhaḥ | 
vīrastu atattvajñānī sanbāhyāntarobhayakriyāvān | 
asyaitatkalpadvayayaugapadya nāsti, uddhatamānasattvāt, 
ācārasāṅkaryāpātācceti sarvamavadātam | tathācoktaṃ 

tantre � divyastu devavatprāyo vīraścoddhatamānasaḥ |

yoginīhṛdaye �

sarvahiṃsā vinirmuktaḥ sarvaprāṇihite rataḥ |
kāmakrodhalobhamoharāgadveṣa vivarjitaḥ ||

pūjāpamāne santuṣṭo'pyadhikārī sa eva hi |
so'smin śāstre'dhikārī syāttadanyo bhraṣṭasādhakaḥ ||

	paśustu saṃśayajñānī sankriyāvān | matsyamāṃsādikaṃ na 
grāhyam, na striyaṃ manasā'pi smaret, na tāmbūla bhakṣayet, kintu 
haviṣyānnaṃ bhakṣayet | ṛtukālaṃ binā na striyamapi gacchet | 
dakṣiṇamārgeṇa pūjā kartavyā | tathācokta yāmale �

yo dākṣiṇyaṃ vinā devi mahā māyāṃ samarcati |
sa pāpaḥ sarvalokebhyaścyuto bhavati nānyathā ||

	anena yaddivāvidhānaṃ, taddivyavīra viṣaye'pi boddhavyam | 
tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

divā dakṣiṇamārgeṇa vāmena ca tathā niśi |

yadi tūrṇaṃ phalāvāptau yuṣmākaṃ matameva ca || iti vacanāt ||

atha grahaṇapuraścaraṇa

śrībījārṇavatantre ṣoḍaśa paṭale devīṃ pratiśivavākyam �

ekadā parameśānī kāmākhyāyāṃ maheśvarī |
dṛṣṭvoparāgaṃ yatkāryaṃ tat pṛcchati maheśvaram |
ye naiva vidhinā deva siddho bhavati nānyathā ||

śrīśiva uvāca �

kutaḥ snānaṃ kutaḥ sandhyā prāṇāyāmaḥ kutaḥ priye |
bhūtaśuddhiḥ kuto bhadre kutaḥ pūjā varānane ||

kālātītabhayāddevi sarvaṃ santyajya kāmini |
saṅkalpaṃ mānasaṃ kṛtvā japaṃ kuryādvarānane ||

pañcāṅga vidhinā devi siddho bhavati nānyathā |
mantro vidyā maheśāni kavacaṃ stava eva vā ||

dhyānaṃ vā parameśāni nyāso vā kamalekṣaṇe |
ekoccāreṇa deveśi bhavanti daśakoṭayaḥ ||

asaṃkhyastajjapo devi grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
tat kathaṃ parameśāni japasaṃkhyā vidhīyate ||

ata eva maheśāni homo nāsti śucismite |
abhiṣekaśca deveśi tathā ca tarpaṇādikam ||

bhojanaṃ ca maheśāni nāsti vai kamalānane |
sūryacandragrahe devi pañcāṅgaṃ nāsti kāmini ||

pañcāṅgena vihīno'pi siddho bhavati nānyathā |
saṅkalpaṃ viddhi deveśi mānasaṃ yadyupasthitam ||

tatsaṅkalpaṃ vijānīyād grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
tasmāttu cañcalāpāṅgi saṅkalpaṃ naiva kārayet ||

saṅkalpo mānaso devi caturvargaphalapradaḥ |
ata eva maheśāni saṅkalpo mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

sthūlo hi parameśāni saṅkalpo vyartha ucyate |
saṅkalpena vinā devi yatkiñcitkurute sudhīḥ |
vyarthameva hi deveśi tatsarvaṃ mānaso na hi ||

grahaṇe bhojanakālaḥ

prathamaprahare bhadre candragrāso yadā bhavet |
tadaiva divase bhuktvā satvaraṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||

niśīthe ca maheśāni yadaiva grahaṇaṃ bhavet |
tadaiva divase bhuktvā pītvānandamayo bhavet ||

candragrahaṇakāle tu japayajñādikaṃ caret |
divase ca yadā bhadre bhāskaragrahaṇaṃ bhavet |
rātrau bhuktvā ca pītvā ca japayajñādikaṃ caret ||

saureṣu (sarveṣu) viṣṇumantreṣu śaive (saure) gāṇapatyau tathā |

śaktimantro maheśāni praśastaḥ satataṃ japet |
iti bījārṇave tantre śivenaiva prakīrtitam ||

	etatsarvaṃ jñānināmeva kartavyam | ājñānināmapi paśūnāṃ 
kartavyamāha 

gandharvatantre �

athavā'nyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |
grahaṇe'rkasya cendorvā śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ ||

nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ nābhimātrodake sthitaḥ |
grahaṇādi vimokṣāntaṃ japenmantraṃ samāhitaḥ ||

sanatkumāratantre �

dṛṣṭvā snātvā susaṅkalpo vimokṣāntaṃ japaṃ caret |
japasya ca daśāṃśena homaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi ||

homārthaṃ dviguṇaṃ vāpi japenmantraṃ samāhitaḥ |
homasya tu daśāṃśena tarpaṇaṃ samupācaret ||

tarpaṇasya daśāṃśena tvabhiṣekaṃ samācaret |
abhiṣeka-daśāṃśena kuryād brāhmaṇa bhojanam ||

tadante mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kuryātsādhaka sattamaḥ |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyadbhaktyā viprānpratarpayet ||

śyāmāvidyāyāṃ viśeṣamāha kālītantre �

athavā'nyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |
candrasūryagrahe caiva grāsāvadhivimuktitaḥ |
yāvatsaṃkhyaṃ manuṃ japtvā tāvad homādikaṃ caret ||

mahiṣamardinītantre �

candrasūryagrahe japtvā kaivalyaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ |
akṛtvā mantrajāpaṃ ca satvaraṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||

guptadīkṣātantre �

candrasūryagrahe yastu samyag jāpaṃ na cācaret |
sa du(bhra)ṣṭaḥ sa ca pāpiṣṭhaḥ sahasā śūkaro bhavet ||

tasyānnamudakaṃ devi mūtraśoṇita viṭsamam |
jāyate nātra sandeho mama vākyādvarānane ||

anyatrāpi �

japayajñaṃ vinā devi yaḥ karotyānyacintanam |
sa bhaved raurave magno yāvadāhūta saṃplavam ||

rauravātpunaragatya pāpayoniṣu jāyate |
niṣkṛtirnāsti cārvaṅgi tasyāpi ca kadācana ||

sūrya parva yathā devi candraparva tathā priye |
sarvaṃ tyaktvā maheśāni japayajñaṃ samācaret ||

	ityādi nānātantra vacanebhyo japaṃ vinā kāryāntarasya 
nindāśruterjapasyaivāvaśyakatvam || rāśyā digaṇanāyāṃ doṣamāha

pūrvajanmārjitaṃ puṇyaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati ||

kāmadhenutantre �

candraparva sūryaparva na vicāryaṃ kadācana |
sūryaparvaṃ varārohena paśyedyadi pāmaraḥ |
astu tāvatparodharmaḥ pūrvadharmo vinaśyati ||

janma saptāṣṭa - ṛpphāṅkadaśamasthe niśākare |
dṛṣṭā'riṣṭaprado rāhurjapapūjāṃ vinā bhavet ||

bhairavatantre �

atha vaśye maheśāni kavacānāṃ puraskriyām |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā puraścāryāṃ samācaret ||

daśāṃśato'ṅgakarmāṇi homādīni pṛthak pṛthak |

svayamaśaktau pratinidhidvārā kartavyam | jñānapradīpe �

dvādaśollāsaḥ || 12 ||




trayodaśollāsaḥ

yantra-saṃskāraḥ

vinā yantreṇa pūjāyāṃ devatā na prasīdati |
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ yantre pūjā praśasyate ||

suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ madhyamathādhamam |
tāmraṃ lakṣaguṇaṃ proktaṃ raupyaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet ||

svarṇaṃ tvanantaphaladaṃ sphāṭikañca tathā bhavet |
ekatolaṃ dvitolaṃ vā tritolaṃ pañcatolakam ||

rasatolaṃ catustolaṃ saptatolaṃ palaṃ tu vā |
sādhakasya manuṃ jñātvā kṛtvā pīṭheṣu sādhakaḥ ||

	athavā pratimāṃ kṛtvā nijadevasvarūpiṇīm || pūjayediti śeṣaḥ || 
sammohanatantre �

mūlamuccarayansamyagālikhedyantramuttamam |

tantre �

tanmadhye vilikhedyantraṃ suvarṇena kuśena vā |

ūrdhvāmnāyatantro �

prāṇanātha ! jagannātha ! brahmaviṣṇuprapūjit |
idānīṃ cakrarājasya pratiṣṭhā karma me vad ||

śrī-īśvara uvāca �

yathā mantrasya saṃskāraṃ tathā yantrasya kalpayet |
asaṃskṛtau yantramantrau rogaśokabhayapradau ||

kathito mantrasaṃskāro daśadhā sarvatantrake |
yantrasaṃskāra madhunā śṛṇu devi samāhitā ||

yantrasaṃskāra saṅkalpaḥ

cakrarājaṃ vinirmāya tataḥ saṃskāra mācaret |
pratiṣṭhā dvividhā devi madhyamā cottamā tathā ||

snātvā saṅkalpayenmantrī gurorvākyena cādarāt |
praṇavaṃ tatsadadyeti māsapakṣatithorapi ||

amuko'mukagotrānte'mukadevyāśca prītaye |
cakre'sminnamukadevyāḥ prāṇajīvendriyeti ca ||

pratiṣṭhākarmaśabdānte kariṣye prāgudaṅmukhaḥ |
tato guruñca vṛṇuyādvastrālaṅkāracandanaiḥ ||

bhūtaśuddhyādikānnyāsānvinyasettadanantaram |
pañcagavyaṃ nijairmantraiḥ śivamantreṇa mantritam ||

tasmin cakraṃ kṣipenmantrī praṇavena vilokayet |
tataścakraṃ samuddhṛtya sthāpayeccakrabhājane ||

yantrasnānam

śaṅkhatoyena deveśi tathā puṣpodakena ca |
vāriṇā candanenā'pi snāpayetparameśvarīm ||

nārikelodakaiścaiva sarvoṣadhijalairapi |
pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyaiḥ snāpayetparameśvarīm ||

taptaṃ śītaṃ jalaṃ varjyaṃ kiñciduṣṇena snāpayet |
atyuṣṇe vajrapātaḥ syāttasmāttaparivarjayet ||

pañcāmṛtamāhayāmale �

dhṛtaṃ dadhi tathā kṣīraṃ śarkarā madhusaṃyutam |
pañcāmṛtamidaṃ khyātaṃ pratyekantu palaṃ palam ||

pañcagavyaparimāṇam

pañcagavyaparimāṇamāha tantre �

palamātraṃ dugdhabhāgaṃ gomūtraṃ tāvadiṣyate |
ghṛtañca palamātraṃ syād gomayaṃ tolakadvayam ||

dadhi prasṛtimātraṃ syātpañcagavyamiti smṛtam |
athavā pañcagavyātāṃ samāno bhāga iṣyate ||

atikrāntaṃ tu ṣaḍrātraṃ dadhi snāne vivarjayet |
saṃvatsarātparaṃ tvājyaṃ ṣaṇmāsānte ca mākṣikam |
guḍañca śarkarāṃ caiva sarvaṃ brīhiñca vatsarāt ||

vivarjayediti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | etāni na dadyādityarthaḥ ||

devānāṃ pratimā yatra ghṛtāmyaṅgakṣamā bhavet |
palāni tatra deyāni śraddhayā saptaviṃśatiḥ ||

aṣṭottaraśatapalaṃ snāne deyañca sarvadā |
dve sahasre palānāntu mahāsnāne tu saṃkhyayā ||

palaṃ tu laukikairmānaiḥ sāṣṭaratti dvimāsakam |
tolakatritayaṃ jñeyaṃ jyotirjñaiḥ smṛtisammatam ||

palaṃ palaṃ pañcagavyaṃ nityasnāne tadarddhakam |
aśaktānāṃ vidhiṃ vaśye kṛcchrāṇāṃ parameśvari ||

guṇatolakahīnaṃ ca na dadyāt snānakarmaṇi |
snānaṃ samāpya tāṃ devīṃ sthāpayet svarṇapīṭhake ||

tasmāduddhṛtya matimānnābherūrdhvaṃ niveśayet |
tatraiva pīṭhaṃ sampūjya cārghapātrādikaṃ caret |
spṛṣṭvā yantraṃ kuśāgreṇa gāyatryā cābhimantrayet ||

gāyatrīmāha �

praṇavaṃ yantrarājāya vidmahe tadanantaram |
mahāyantrāya dhīmahi tanno yantraḥ pracodayāt |
āvāhya pañcamudrābhiḥ prāṇasthāpanamācaret ||

jñānārṇave �

anāmikāmūlaparvasaṃsaktāṃguṣṭhayugmakam |
uttānaṃ hastayugalaṃ yojayet sādhakāgraṇīḥ ||

ūrdhvāñajalimadhaḥ kuryādiyamāvāhanī bhavet |
iyaṃ tu viparītā syānmudrā sthāpanakarmaṇi ||

yuktocchritāṃguṣṭhamuṣṭī mudrā syātsannidhāpanī |
aṃguṣṭhagarbhiṇī saiva mudrā syātsannirodhinī ||

uttānamuṣṭiyugalā sammukhīkaraṇī matā |
devatāṃge ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ nyāsaḥ syat sakalīkṛtiḥ ||

karāvekatra saṃyojya adhobhūtāviva priye |
paramīkaraṇaṃ nāma mudreyantu tataḥ param |
vaṃ bījenāmṛtīkuryāt mudrayā dhenusaṃjñayā ||

anyonyābhimukhe śliṣṭe kaniṣṭhānāmike punaḥ |
tathaiva tarjanīmadhye dhenumudrā prakīrtitā ||

dhenumudrā mahādevī amṛtīkaraṇe bhavet |
pratiṣṭhāpyā'rcayed devīmanyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||

pratiṣṭhāpyeti � yantre pratimāyāṃ vā prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ 
vidhāyetyarthaḥ || prāṇamantramāha yāmale �

uccārya bhuvaneśānīṃ pāśāṃkuśapuṭāṃ tataḥ |
yādyāḥ saptamṛtāṅkāṭyā vyoma satyendusaṃyutam ||

vyoma bindusamāyuktaṃ sargāvān bhṛgurityayam |
nāmnā devyāstataḥ prāṇā iha prāṇāstataḥ priye ||

punarmantraṃ puraskṛtya tathaiva sādhakottamaḥ |
nāmnā ca devatāyāstu tato jīva iha sthitaḥ ||

tathaiva devadeveśi ! uktvā sarvendriyāṇi ca |
vāṅmanaścakṣurityante śrotraghrāṇapadaṃ tataḥ ||

tataḥ prāṇā ihāgatya sukhamuktvā ciraṃ paṭhet |
tiṣṭhantu vahnijāyāntaḥ prāṇamantra udāhṛtaḥ ||

sva sva nāmnā maheśāni mantro'yaṃ sarvadaivataḥ |
iti prāṇān pratiṣṭhāpya tataḥ pūjāṃ samārabhet ||

svakalpokta vidhānena mudrāṃ pradarśya sādhakaḥ |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdevīṃ prapūjayed kramāt ||

devyājñayā parīvārānpūjayet parameśvari |
tato japet sahasraṃ tu śatamaṣṭottaraṃ priye ||

balidānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā praṇameccakrarājakam |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ homaṃ kuryācca sādhakottamaḥ ||

nijamantreṇa deveśi juhuyāccakrasiddhaye |
āhutyante cakrarāje hutaśeṣaṃ vinikṣipet ||

purṇān dattvā tu homānte tajjalairabhiṣecayet |
mantrābhiṣiktaṃ cakraṃ tatsarveṣāṃ siddhidāyakam ||

guruve dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād gāṃ ca dadyātpayasvinīm |
bhūmiṃ vṛttikarīṃ dadyāt putrapautrānuyāyinīm ||

saṃhāramudrayā devyā visarjanamataḥparam |
pratiṣṭhayeccakrarājamanena vidhinā yadi ||

puraścaryāphalaṃ tasya sarvasiddhiyu tasya ca |
gurorājñāṃ pramāṇena yantraṃ mūrdhni nidhāpayet ||

gṛhītaṃ yantramevedaṃ kvāpi naiva prakāśayet |
yantra mantra prakāśe tu kruddhā bhavati pārvatī ||

nijamantrābhiṣiktañca gurorapi na darśayet |
yantragrahaṇakāle ca yadi syāt meghagarjanam ||

ulluludhvanirākasmādathavā śaṅkha nisvanaḥ |
tadā mantrī jhaṭityeva siddhakāryo na saṃśayaḥ ||

ayane viṣuve caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
grahaṇaṃ yantramantrāṇāṃ śubhadaṃ tat prakīrtitam ||

atha balidānam 

muṇḍamālāyām �

naraśchāgastathā meṣo mahiṣaḥ śaśakastathā |
śallakī śūkaraścaiva balayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

narabalistu rājñāmeva �

rājā narabaliṃ dadyānnānyo hi parameśvari |
siṃhavyāghra narāndattvā brāhmaṇo rauravaṃ vrajet ||

yuvānaṃ vyādhihīnaṃ ca suśrīkaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam |
sarvāvayava sampannaṃ baliṃ dadyāt suśobhanam ||

taruṇaṃ sundaraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṣatādidoṣavarjitam |
snāpayitvā baliṃ tatra bhūṣayet puṣpacandanaiḥ ||

bhūṣayed raktamālyena sindūreṇa viṣeśataḥ |
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā baliṃ pūrvamukhaṃ tathā ||

samānīya svavāme ca mūlena prokṣaṇaṃ caret |
saṃprokṣaṇaṃ vidhāyaiva baliṃ sampūjayedatha ||

brahmarandhre ca brahmāṇaṃ tannāsāyāñca medinīm |
karṇayośca tathākāśaṃ jihvāyāṃ sarvatomukham ||

jyotīṣi netrayorviṣṇuṃ vadane paripūjayet |
lalāṭe pūjayeccandraṃ śakraṃ dakṣiṇagaṇḍataḥ ||

vāmagaṇḍe tathā vahniṃ grīvāyāṃ samavartanam |
romakūpe dhṛtiṃ caiva bhruvormadhye pracetasam ||

nāsāmūle ca śvasanaṃ skandhe caiva maheśvaram |
hṛdaye sarparājendraṃ pūjayitvā paṭhedidam ||

mahātapobhirjñānaiśca yajñeryatsādhyate nṛbhiḥ |
tanme dehi mahābhāga ! satvaraṃ cāpnuyāṃ śriyam ||

śivabuddhyā ca sampūjya utsṛjecca tataḥ param |
tato devīṃ samuddiśya kāmamuddiśya cātmanaḥ ||

ityutsṛjya baliṃ paścāt karavālaṃ prapūjayet |
khaḍmagre pūjayenmantrī brahma-vāgīśvarīṃ tathā ||

madhye ca pūjayeddevi ! lakṣmīnārāyaṇāvapi |
mūle ca pūjayenmantrī umayā ca maheśvaram ||

evaṃ vidhānaiḥ sampūjya namaskuryāt prayatnataḥ |
khaḍgatvaṃ śivarūpo'si krodhabhairava saṃjñakaḥ ||

durgā prītikaro nityaṃ kālīśakterivā'parā |
khaḍgāya kharaśānāya śaktikāryārtha tatpara ! ||

paśucchedyastvayā śīghraṃ khaḍganātha ! namo'stute |
evaṃ sampūjya taṃ khaḍgamuttolya sādhakottamaḥ ||

chettā pūrvamukho bhūtvā balimuttaravaktrakam | (?)
āṃ huṃ phaṭ iti mantreṇa chedayitvā tataḥ paśum ||

tato balīnāṃ rudhiraṃ toyasaindhavasatphalaiḥ |
madhubhirgandhapuṣpaiśca svadhivāsya prayatnataḥ ||

gandhapuṣpokṣitaṃ kṛtvā cotsṛjenmūlamuccaran |
praṇavaṃ vāgbhavaṃ lakṣmīṃ tataḥ kauśikīṃ śabdataḥ ||

rudhireṇa tataḥ paścādāpyāyatāṃ samuccaret |
nivedya rudhiraṃ devi ! śive dadyāt pradīpakam |
tato nivedayenmantrī tāmbūlaṃ sumanoharam ||

rudhira-mastaka sthānapana kramaḥ

rudhiramastaka sthāpanakramamāha tantre �

nāraṃ savye śiroraktaṃ devyāḥ samyagniyojayet |
chāgaṃ tu vāmato dadyānmāhiṣaṃ vitaret puraḥ |
dakṣiṇe vā mato dadyādagrato dehaśoṇitam ||

yāmale � yadā kaṭakaṭāśabdo dantānāṃ śrūyate kvacit |
tadā tu maraṇaṃ vidyāddhāniṃ tatra vinirdiśet |
yadāśru dṛśyate netre tadā hāniṃ vinirdiśet ||

balimastakapatana phalam

pūrvottare ca digbhāge patate yadi mastakam |
sarvasampatkaraṃ vidyād rājñāṃ rājyaṃ vinirdiśet ||

īśānāgnyormadhyabhāge patate yadi mastakam |
tataḥ svalpena kālena sarvasiddhirbhaved dhruvam ||

yadi vāyavyadigbhāge nair-ṛte dakṣiṇe'pi vā |
mastakaṃ patate jātu tasya hāniṃ vinirdiśet ||

balimastake dīpadānam

yāmale �

tāvatkālaṃ vasetsvarge tasmādyatnena dāpayet ||

upacāravidhiḥ

upacāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati ! sādaram |
vinopacārairyā pūjā sā pūjā na prasīdati ||

aṣṭādaśopacārāstu sarveṣāmuttamāḥ priye |
ṣoḍaśeti pradhānāśca daśadhāstadanu smṛtāḥ |
pañcadhāstadanu proktāḥ kartavyā bhūtimicchatā ||

phetkāriṇītantre �

āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanaṃ tathā |
snānaṃ vāsaścopavītaṃ bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvaśaḥ ||

gandhapuṣpa dhūpadīpau tathānnaṃ cāpi darpaṇam |
mālyānulepanaṃ caiva namaskāra visarjanam |
aṣṭādaśopacāraistu mantrī pūjāṃ samācaret ||

tantre � āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanasnānavasanābharaṇāni ca ||

gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyavadanaṃ tathā |
prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa ||

pādyārghyācamanīya ca madhuparkācamaṃ tathā |
gandhādayo naivedyāntā upacārā daśa smṛtāḥ ||

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca |
pradadyāt parameśāni ! pañca pūjopacārakān ||

pādyādinirūpaṇam

pādyārthamudakaṃ pādyaṃ candanāguru saṃyutam |
etacchyāmākadūrvābja viṣṇukrāntāmitīritam ||

sāmānyaḥ sarvadevānāmargho'yaṃ parikalpitaḥ |
abhāve dadhidugdhādyairmānasaṃ parikalpayet ||

antaḥśūnyāṃ tripatrāñca dūrvāṃ cārghena nikṣipet |
jātīlavaṅgakakkolairdadyādācamanīyakam ||

tat taijasena pātreṇa śaṃkhena vā pradāpayet |
udakaṃ dīyate yadyatsugandhaṃ phenavarjitam ||

ācamanīyakaṃ devyai tadācamana mucyate |
dadyādācamīyaṃ tu sugandhasalilaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||

bṛhatśrīkrame � nārikelodakaṃ svalpaṃ sitā dadhi ghṛtaṃ samam |

gandhakathanam

sarveṣāṃ gandhajātīnāṃ prakṛṣṭau malayodbhavaḥ |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena dadyānmalayajaṃ sadā ||

madhyamānāmikāgrāmyāmaṃguṣṭhāgreṇa pārvati |
dadyācca vimalaṃ gandhaṃ mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ ||

puṣpaprakaraṇam

sarveṣāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ raktaṃ śastaṃ varānane ! |
devī prītikaraṃ prājñe ! sarvakāmaphalapradam ||

raktapuṣpañca deveśi ! tathā svarṇādi nirmitam |
raktapadmaṃ ca vajrañca kṛṣṇā tu cāparājitā ||

pañcadevamayaṃ puṣpaṃ karavīraṃ manoharam |
viṣṇurlambodaraḥ sūryo brahmā ca kālikā tathā ||

pañcadevāḥ pañcadale sadā tiṣṭhanti nānyathā |
javāpuṣpaṃ maheśāni ! karavīrāparājite ||

mahādevyai nivedyaiva koṭipūjāphalaṃ labhet |
eṣāṃ madhye vased brahmā eṣāṃ mūle janārdanaḥ |
eṣāmagre vased rudraḥ sarve devāḥ sthitā dale ||

eṣāṃ karavīrāparājitājavāpuṣpāṇam ityarthaḥ |

vṛkṣe vikasite kāle devatādigvinirṇayaḥ |
paścimasthadale viṣṇuḥ uttare gaṇanāyakaḥ ||

aiśānyāṃ sūryadevaśca pūrve brahmā prakīrtitaḥ |
dakṣiṇe kālikā devī yā tu muktipradāyinī ||

karavīraṃ yathā devi ! javāpuṣpaṃ tathaiva hi |
yathā śubhraṃ tathā raktaṃ haritaṃ kṛṣṇameva ca ||

gaṅgādi sarvatīrthāni tiṣṭhanti bindugahvare |
tanmadhye śivaliṅgañca mahākuṇḍalinīyutam ||

gahvaraṃ bindurūpaṃ ca kaivalyapadamuttamam |
śivaśaktimayaṃ puṣpaṃ caturvargaphalapradam ||

sarvapuṣpāṇi caikatra javābjapārijātakaiḥ |
na samāni bhavantyeva lakṣakoṭi śatānyapi ||

yatrā'parājitāpuṣpaṃ karavīraṃ javāpi ca |
tiṣṭhanti tatra vai devā brahmāviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ ||

gaṅgādi sarvatīrthāni tanmūle nivasanti vai |
tanmūlaṃ siñcitaṃ tena pūjitāstena devatāḥ ||

aparājitāmāhātmyaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate mayā |
mallikāmutpalaṃ ramyaṃ śamīṃ punnāgacampake ||

aśokaṃ karṇikārañca droṇapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |
karavīraṃ javāpuṣpaṃ kuṃkumaṃ nāgakeśaram ||

yaḥ prayacchati durgāyai sa gacchet paramaṃ padam |
puṣpamūle vased brahmā puṣpamadhye janārdanaḥ ||

puṣpāgre ca vasedrudraḥ sarve devāḥ sthitā dale |
carācarāśca sakalāḥ sadā puṣpavaśāḥ smṛtāḥ ||

sarvadevamayaṃ puṣpaṃ tasmāddevyai samarpayet ||

puṣpairaraṇyasambhūtaiḥ patrairgirisamudbhavaiḥ |
aparyuṣitaniśchadraiḥ prokṣiterjalavarjitaiḥ ||

ātmārāmodbhavairvāpi puṣpaiḥ sampūjayecchivām |
pāropita vṛkṣebhyaḥ puṣpāṇyānīya yo'rcayet |
avijñāpyaiva tasyaiva niṣphalaṃ tasya pūjanam ||

iti tu sākṣātsvāmiparam |

devārthe kusumasteyamasteyaṃ manuravravīt | iti vacanāt ||

puṣpādīnāṃ paryuṣitakālaḥ

sarvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ varjyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam |
avarjyaṃ jāhnavītoyamavarjyaṃ tulasīdalam ||

avarjyaṃ bilvapatraṃ syādavarjyaṃ jalajaṃ tathā |
puṣpaiḥ paryuṣitairdevi ! nārcayet svarṇajairapi ||

bilvapatraṃ ca māghyañca tamālāmalakīdalam |
kahlāraṃ tulasīpatraṃ padmañca munipuṣpakam ||

etaṃ paryuṣitaṃ na syāt yaccānyat kalikātmakam |
tiṣṭheddinatrayaṃ śuddhaṃ padmamāmalakaṃ tathā ||

dinaikaṃ karavīrāṇi yogyāni ca tapodhana |
padmāni sitaraktāni kumudānyutpalāni ca ||

eṣāṃ paryuṣitā śaṃkā kāryā pañcadinordhvataḥ |
anyeṣāṃ kusumānāṃ ca yāvad gandhaviparyayaḥ ||

puṣpaṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ ca upacārāṃstathā'parān |
ghrātvā nivedya deveśi ! naro narakamāpnuyāt ||

aṃga saṃspṛṣṭamāghrātaṃ tyājyaṃ paryuṣitaṃ budhaiḥ |
keśakīṭāpaviddhāni śīrṇaparyuṣitāni ca ||

svayaṃ patitapuṣpāṇi tyajedupahatāni ca |
śephālī bakulaṃ caiva svayaṃ śīrṇaṃ na dūṣyati ||

sarvaṃ bhūmigataṃ tyājyaṃ śephālībakulaṃ vinā |
kṛmibhakṣyāṇi bhagnāni varjyāni patitaṃ bhuvi ||

tamālasya ca padmasya chinnabhinnaṃ na dūṣyati |
viṣṇukrāntā-javā-nāgakeśaraṃ nāgavallabham ||

bandhūkaṃ caiva mandā(kahlā)raṃ savṛntaṃ śastamarcane |
svayaṃ vikasitaṃ puṣpaṃ tyājyañca patitaṃ bhuvi ||

nāgavallabhaṃ nāgacampakamityarthaḥ | svayaṃ vikasitaṃ puruṣeṇa 
vikasita mityarthaḥ ||

māghamāse tu deveśi ! pūjyapuṣpāṇi dvādaśa |
kundaṃ kuruvakaṃ jhiṇṭīṃ ketakaṃ niculaṃ tathā ||

nīlañca vikaṭaṃ śīrṣaṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ ca kṣudrakam |
bakulaṃ raṅganaṃ caiva nānyamāse yajet kvacit ||

nākṣatairarcayed viṣṇuṃ na tulasyā vināyakam |
na dūrvayā yajed durgāṃ bilvapatrairdivākaram ||

dūrvā niṣiddheti yaduktaṃ, tat śvetadūrvāparam | tathocoktaṃ
