puṣpābhāve yajed patraiḥ patrābhāve tu tatphalaiḥ |
akṣatairvā jalairvāpi na pūjāṃ vyatilaṅghayet ||

śive vivarjayet kundamunmattañca harestathā |
devīnāmarkamandārau sūryasya tagaraṃ tathā ||

	devīnāmiti ādyetaradevīnāmityarthaḥ | arkapuṣpairādyāpūjāyāḥ 
prāśastyakathanāt, sahasraṃ tvarkāṇāmiti karpūrādistavācca | tagaraṃ 
kāṣṭhatagaramityarthaḥ ||

śivapūjāyāṃ yāmale �

bakulaṃ mālatīṃ jātīṃ kundaṃ śephālikāṃ javām |
na dadyācca mahādeve yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ ||

mālatī mallikā jātī yūthikā mādhavī tathā |
tagaraṃ karṇikāraśca droṇaścotpalacampakau ||

aśokaḥ kumudaścaiva śephālikākadambakau |
ketakī vanamālā ca kusumbhakiṃśukau tathā ||

kahlārabakulaṃ caiva lavaṅga nāgakeśarau |
etānyapi priyāṇi syurna patrairarcayecchivām ||

javābhiścaiva gandhāḍhyairdūrvayā śrīphalacchadaiḥ |
vinā vai dūrvayā devīpūjā nāsti ca karhicit ||

p.279) tasmāda dūrvā grahītavyā sarvapuṣpamayī hi sā |
devebhyaḥ sarvagandhāḍhyamabhāve tulasīdalam ||

tulasyā pūjayed devān nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
vinā tulasyā snānādi śrāddhaṃ yajñañca na priye ||

sarvañca niṣphalaṃ prāhuḥ sarvatraivaṃ viniścitam |
dūrvā vā tulasī tasmāt grahītavyā ca sādhakaiḥ ||

sundarī bhairavīkālī brahmavighna vivasvatām |
tulasīvarjitā pūjā sā pūjā'viphalā bhavet ||

tulasīpatraiḥ śakterarcanam

śaktiviṣaye yāmale �

sāvitrīṃ ca bhavānīṃ ca durgādevīṃ sarasvatīm |
yo'rcayet tulasīpatraiḥ sarvakāmaiḥ samṛdhyate ||

yāmale �

rātrāvāsāṃ tu pūjāyāṃ tulasīṃ varjayet sadā |
tulasīghrāṇamātreṇa kruddhā bhavati caṇḍikā ||

tulasī brahmarūpā ca sarvadevamayī śubhā |
sarvadevamayī sā tu gaṇeśasya priyā nahi ||

lakṣmīdevyāścāpriyā hi tārādevyāstathaiva ca |
aṃguṣṭhatarjanī yogairdakṣiṇe puṣpapātanam ||

puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ phalaṃ neṣṭamadhomukham ||

duḥkhadaṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ yathotpannaṃ tathārpayet |
puṣpāñjaliṃ vinā devi ! tathotpannaṃ tathārpayet ||

puṣpādicayanakālaḥ

yāmale �

snānaṃ kṛtvā tu ye kecid puṣpaṃ gṛhṇanti vai dvijāḥ |
devatāstanna gṛhṇanti na cāpi pitarastathā ||

	etat tu madhyāhnasnāna param | prātaḥsnānāntaraṃ tu 
puṣpādicayanaṃ kartavyamiti sampradāyikāḥ | tantre �

snātvā madhyāhnasamaye na chindyāt kusumaṃ budhaḥ |
tatpuṣpairarcayandevīṃ niraye paripacyate ||

devītyupalakṣaṇam | nānyadevānapi yajediti |

prātaḥsnānādikaṃ kṛtvā puṣpāṇyapi samāharet |
tatpuṣpairarcayandevīṃ sa pāpairmucyate kṣaṇāt ||

devītyupalakṣaṇamanyadevānapi arcayediti |

napuṣpacchedanaṃ kuryāddevārthaṃ vāmahastataḥ |
na dadyāttena tebhyo vā saṃsthāpya vāmahastataḥ ||

dhūpaprakaraṇam

agurūśīraguggulaśarkarāmadhucandanaiḥ |
sāmānyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ ||

sarveṣāmeva dhūpānāṃ durgāyāḥ gugguluḥ priyaḥ |
ghṛtayukto viśeṣeṇa satataṃ prītivardhanaḥ ||

dhūpabhājanamastreṇa prokṣyā'bhyarcya hṛdāṇunā |
astreṇa pūjitāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ vādayan gugguluṃ dahet ||

dhūpasthānaṃ samabhyarcya tarjanyā vāmayā spṛśan |
jaya dhvani tato me mātaḥ? svāhetyudīrayan ||

abhyarcya vādayan ghaṇṭāṃ tairdhūpairdhūpayettataḥ |
madhyamānāmikābhyāñca madhyaparvaṇi deśikaḥ ||

aṃguṣṭhāgreṇa deveśi ! dhṛtvā dhūpaṃ nivedayet |
uttolya mūrdhni paryantaṃ ghaṇṭāvādena dhūpakam ||

dhūpayedājyasaṃmiśraṃ nīcairdevasya deśikaḥ |
na bhūmau vitared dhūpaṃ nāsena na ghaṭe tathā ||

yathā tathādharagataṃ dhṛtvā taṃ vinivedayet |
rāśīkṛtairna caivātra tvetairdhūpairvidhūpayet ||

tuṣāgnivat tathā kṛtvā na tatphalamavāpnuyāt |

dīpaprakaraṇam

na miśrīkṛtya dadyāttu dīpasnehaghṛtādikān |
dattvā miśrīkṛtaṃ snehaṃ tamisraṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||

vartyā karpūragarbhiṇyā sarpiṣā tilajena vā |
āropya darśayeddīpānuccaiḥ saurabhaśālinaḥ ||

uccairiti devasya mastakaparyantamityarthaḥ |

uttolanaṃ tridhā kṛtvā gāyatrīmūlayogataḥ |
tato nīrājanaṃ kuryāddaśavāraṃ tu dīpakaiḥ ||

dātavyaḥ pātre dīpastu na tu bhūmau kadācana |
kurvantaṃ pṛthivītāpaṃ yo dīpamutsṛjennaraḥ ||

tamisraṃ narakaṃ ghoraṃ prāpnotyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
sarvaṃsahā vasumatī sahate na tvidaṃ dvayam ||

akāryapādadhātaṃ ca dīpatāpaṃ tathaiva ca |
tasmātkurvīta pṛthivītāpaṃ nāpnoti vai yathā ||

naiva nirvāpayeddīpaṃ devārthamupakalpitam |
dīpahartā bhavedandhaḥ kāṇo nirvāpako bhavet |
na tena vyavahāro'pi kartavyaḥ sādhakottamaiḥ ||

naivedyaprakaraṇam

naivedyamāha � kandupakvaṃ snehapakvaṃ ghṛtasaṃyukta pāyasam |
manaḥ priyañca naivedyaṃ dadyāddevyai punaḥ punaḥ ||

kandupakvamiti mṛṣṭataṇḍula pṛthukādīni deyānītyarthaḥ |
yadyad hi vācchitaṃ vastu daddadyātdevapūjane |
balapriyaṃ ca naivedyaṃ dattvā devīṃ prapūjayet ||

ātmā'priyaṃ ca naivedyaṃ na dadyāddevapūjane |
strīṇāṃ prītikaraṃ yacca taccāpi vinivedayet |
tāmbūlasya pradānena devī prītimatī bhavet ||

pradakṣiṇavidhiḥ

śaṅkhahastena sarvatra sakṛd dvirvā pradakṣiṇam |
veṣṭanañca tataḥ kṛtvā praṇameddaṇḍavad bhuvi ||

tathā tridhācaret samyak devatāyāḥ pradakṣiṇam |
ekaṃ caṇḍyāṃ ravau sapte trīṇi kuryādvināyake ||

catvāri keśave kuryācchive cārddhapradakṣiṇam |
dakṣiṇādvāyavīṃ gatvā diśastasyāśca śāmbhavīm ||

tato'pi dakṣiṇāṃ gatvā namaskārastrikoṇavat |
trikoṇo'yaṃ namaskārastripurāprītivarddhanaḥ ||

natistrikoṇākārā ca tārādevyāḥ samīritā |
darśayandakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ manasāpi ca dakṣiṇaḥ ||

sa ca pradakṣiṇo jñeyaḥ sarvadevaughatuṣṭaye |
paścātkṛtvā tu yo devaṃ bhrabhitvā praṇamennaraḥ |
tasyeha ca phalaṃ nāsti na paratra durātmanaḥ ||

praṇāmavidhiḥ

namanaṃ mānasaṃ proktaṃ vācikaṃ kāyikaṃ tathā |
trividhaśca namaskāraḥ kāyikaścottamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

kāyikaiśca namaskārairdevāstuṣyanti nityaśaḥ |
jānubhyāmavanīṃ gatvā śirasā spṛśya medinīm ||

kriyate yo namaskāra uttamaḥ kāyikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
padbhayāṃ karābhyāṃ jānubhyāmurasā śirasā dṛśā ||

vacasā manasā caiva praṇāmo'ṣṭāṅga īritaḥ |
padbhayāṃ karābhyāṃ jānubhyāmurasā śirasā'pi ca ||

pañcāṅgo'sau namaskāraḥ sarvatrāyaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
puṭīkṛtya karau śīrṣe dīyate ca yathā tathā ||

aspṛṣṭvā śīrṣajānubhyāṃ kṣitiṃ so'pyadhama smṛtaḥ |
kāyikastrividhaḥ prokto hyaṣṭāṅgādivibhedataḥ ||

aṣṭāṅga uttamaḥ proktaḥ pañcāṅgo madhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
adhamaṃ karaśīrṣābhyāṃ namaskāraṃ vivarjayet ||

ayameva namaskāro daṇḍavaditi nāmataḥ |
praṇāma iti vijñeyaḥ sa pūrvaṃ pratimāditaḥ ||

yā svayaṃ gadyapadyābhyāṃ ghaṭitābhyāṃ namaskṛtiḥ |
kriyate bhaktiyuktena vācikī tūttamā smṛtā ||

paurāṇikaivaidikairvā mantrairyā kriyate natiḥ |
sa madhyamo namaskāro bhavedvācanikaḥ sadā ||

yattu mānuṣavākyena namanaṃ kriyate sadā |
sa vāciko'dhamo jñeyo namaskārastu pārvati ! ||

upacāraprakaraṇam

athopacārānkurvīta tantroktānāsanādikān |
āsanaṃ kusumaṃ dadyāt svāgataṃ kuśalaṃ vadet ||

devasya vāmabhāge tu dadyānmūlena cāsanam |
pauṣpaṃ dārumayaṃ vāstraṃ cārmaṃ kauśaṃ ca taijasam ||

ṣaḍvidyaṃ cāsanaṃ proktaṃ devatāprītikārakam |
namo'ntaṃ pādayoḥ pādyaṃ śiromantreṇa deśikaḥ ||

arghyaṃ dadyāttato mūrdhni svadhetyācamanaṃ mukhe |
svadhetyācamanīyañca tivāraṃ mukhapaṅkaje ||

svadhāntenaiva manunā madhuparkaṃ mukhāmbuje |
snānaṃ gandhaṃ hṛdā dadyāt puṣpāṇi vauṣaḍityapi ||

snānārthamudakaṃ dadyātsarvāṅge parameśvari ! |
tāyena prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā dukūlaṃ vinivedayet ||

snāne vastre ca naivedye dadyādācamanīyakam |
sarvālaṅkaraṇaṃ dadyādyatra yat tu virājate ||

pratimādau yathāyogyaṃ gātre dadyāttu tatvagaḥ |
sarvāṅge candanaṃ dadyātpuṣpaṃ dadyācchiropari ||

namo'ntakau dhūpadīpau savyadakṣakrameṇa tu |
dadyāttu yogyaṃ purato naivedyaṃ bhojanādikam ||

naivedyañca sudhāntaṃ hi kalpayāmi namo vadet |
nivedayāmi naivedyaṃ yaddravyaiḥ parikalpitam |
tato nivedayāmīti sarvaṃ dadyānmaheśvari ||

naivedyādīnāmācchādanāvaśyakatvam

gāndharve �

sunaivedyādikaṃ yattu gandhapuṣpādikañca yat |
sarvasyācchādanaṃ kāryaṃ yāvadāvāhayetparām ||

rākṣasāḥ pratigṛhṇanti nirācchādanakaṃ yataḥ |
devyā naivedyadānte tu yo vidhiḥ sa tu kathyate |
asaṃskṛtaṃ na dātavyaṃ devatābhirna gṛhyate ||

naivedyadānavidhiḥ

saṃskāramāha yāmale �

ānīya devīpurataḥ saṃprokṣya cārghyavāriṇā |
astramantreṇa cābhyukṣya dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet ||

tasyopari mūlamantramaṣṭavāraṃ japet sudhīḥ |
cakramudrāṃ vidhāyaiva cintayettatsurakṣitam ||

yaṃ mantraiḥ śoṣayed doṣaṃ raṃ mantrairdāhayecca tam |
vaṃ mantraiścāmṛtaṃ bhāvyaṃ ṭhaṃ mantraiḥ plāvayecca tat ||

sarvatra bhakṣyadravyeṣu evaṃ saṃskāramācaret |
amṛto'pastaraṇamasi svāheti jalamarpayet ||

amukīdevyai etajjalaṃ oṃ amṛtopastaraṇamasi svāhā iti mantreṇa dadyāt |

āpośānaṃ jalaṃ dattvā mahādevyai nivedayet |
idamannaṃ sopakaraṇaṃ mahādevyai sva(su) dhāpi ca ||

praṇavādyairuktamantrairdevīvaktre hunet guruḥ || guruvityupalakṣaṇam 

agre devasya hastābhyāmutthāpya mukhasannidhau |
jaganmātarjagaddhātryamuki devi tataḥ param ||

nivedayāmi yatkiñcid juṣā(gṛhā)ṇedaṃ havirmama |
anena manunā devi ! nivedya praṇavādinā ||

vāme vā dakṣiṇe vāpi pañcamudrāḥ pradarśayet |
aṃgulyaḥ kuṭilībhūtā viralāgrāḥ parasparam |
grāsamudrā samākhyātā savye pāṇau niyojayet ||

prāṇādimudrā

prāṇāpāna samānāścodānavyānau ca vāyavaḥ |
samānaḥ pañcamo jñeyaḥ prāṇāḥ pañca samīritāḥ ||

prāṇamudrā samākhyātā prāṇe havanakarmaṇi |
tarjanī madhyamāṃguṣṭhaistribhirekīkṛtaṃ yadi ||

syādapānāhutau mudrā tathā'nāmikamadhyame |
kaniṣṭhena samāyuktā niyuktā vyānahomake ||

niṣkaniṣṭhena yā mudrā sodāna havane smṛtā |
sarvābhiḥ saṃskṛtā mudrā samānāhutikarmaṇi ||

kṣaṇaṃ vilambya devyā tu svīkṛtaṃ tad vibhāvayet |
yāvad bhuṅkte havirdevi ! tāvanmūlaṃ japetsudhīḥ ||

tato mūlena salilaṃ dattvā havīṃṣi sādhakaḥ |
tasmāttejaḥ samunnatyai dattvā'pośānamuttaram ||

etajjalam amṛtāpidhānamasi svāheti dadyāt |

tataḥ ācamanaṃ toyaṃ dadyācca mukhavāsanam |
sthānaṃ viśodhya tanmantrī tāmbūlaṃ ca nivedayet ||

ukteṣveteṣu dravyeṣu yatkiñcid durlabhaṃ yadi |
tatkalpanīyaṃ deveśi manasā bhāvanena tu |
sarvatraiva jalaṃ deyamupa cārāntarāntare ||

dravyāṇāṃ nirmālyatākālaḥ

dravyaviśeṣāṇāṃ nirmālyatāniyamamāha yoginīhṛdaye �

maṇimuktāsuvarṇāni deve dattāni yāni vai |
na nirmālyaṃ dvādaśābdaṃ tāmrapātraṃ tathaiva ca ||

paṭī śāṭī ca ṣanmāsaṃ naivedyaṃ dattamātrataḥ |
modakaṃ kṛṣaraṃ caiva yāmārddhena ca sundari ||

paṭṭavastraṃ trimāsācca yajñasūtraṃ tvahaḥ smṛtam |
yāvaduṣṇaṃ bhavedannaṃ paramānnaṃ tathaiva ca ||

mastakaṃ rudhiraṃ caiva ahorātreṇa pārvati ! |
muhūrtaṃ dadhi dugdhaṃ ca tvājyaṃ yāmena śaṅkari ! ||

karavīramahorātraṃ bilvapatraṃ tathaiva ca |
javāraktaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ bhavet sārddhaikayāmake ||

yāmārddhenāpa īśāni ! tāmbūlaṃ dattamātrataḥ |
na nirmālyañca dāḍimbaṃ tathā bilvaphalaṃ priye ! ||

iti śrīparivrājakācārya paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ mupacārādi nirṇayo nāma 
caturdaśollāsaḥ || 14 ||




pañcadaśollāsaḥ

atha śāktācāraḥ

kulacūḍāmaṇau � devyuvācaḥ

śṛṇuputra ! rahasyaṃ meṃ samayācāra sambhavam |
yena hīnāṃ na siddhyanti janmakoṭiśatairapi ||

anityakarma saṃtyāgī nityānuṣṭhāna tatparaḥ |
parasyāṃ devatāyāntu sarvakarma nivedayet ||

vṛthā na kālaṃ gamayedyuta krīḍādinā sudhīḥ |
nayet tu devatāpūjājapayajñādikarmabhiḥ ||

antaḥ śāktā vahiḥ śaivāḥ sabhāyāṃ vaiṣṇavā matāḥ |
sarvadā viṣṇu bhāvastu bhavet sādhakapuṅgavaḥ ||

yadi paśyet kulataruṃ praṇamet sādhakastadā ||

kulavṛkṣāḥ

kulavṛkṣamāhatantre �

aśokaḥ keśaro bilvaḥkarṇikāraścūtastathā |
namerustathā priyālaśca sinduvāra kadambakau |
maruvakaścampakaśca bilvaśca dvādaśa smṛtāḥ ||

	nameru rudrākṣaḥ | piyālo vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ | sindhuvāro 
niśundākhyayākhyātaḥ | maruvako jhiṇṭikā | ete dvādaśa kulavṛkṣā 
jñātavyāḥ | 

	anyatrāpi �

śleṣmākaḥ karañjākhyo nimbāśvattha kadambakāḥ |
bilvo'śokaścampakaśca ityaṣṭau kulapādapāḥ ||

śleṣmāṃtako baheḍāvṛkṣa itikhyātaḥ |

tiṣṭhanti kulayoginyaḥ sarveṣveteṣu sarvadā |
na svapet kulavṛkṣādhona copadravamācaret ||

yāmale �

ārāme parvate caiva nirjane śūnyamaṇḍape |
catuṣpathe kalāmadhye yadi daivād gatirbhavet ||

kṣaṇaṃ sthi(dhyā)tvā manuṃ japtvā natvā gacched yathāsukham 
catuṣpathe devyāḥ pīṭhe ityarthaḥ |

tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

catuṣpathaḥ sa vijñeyo yatra syāt tāriṇī śubhā |

taraṇakartṛtvāt tāriṇītyarthaḥ ||

pīṭhanirūpaṇam

pīṭhamāha gāndharve �

kāmarūpaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ vārāṇasīṃ tataḥ param |
nepālañca mahāpīṭhaṃ pauṇḍuvarddhanakaṃ tathā ||

purasthiraṃ mahādevi ! carasthiramataḥ param |
pūrṇaśailaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ arbudaṃ ca tataḥ param ||

kāśmīrañca tathā pīṭhaṃ kānyakubjamatho bhavet |
āmrāta keśvaraṃ pīṭhamekāmrañca tathā śive ! ||

tisrota pīṭhamuddiṣṭaṃ kāmakoṭimataḥ param |
kailāsaṃ bhūtanagaraṃ kedāraṃ pīṭhamuttamam ||

śrīpīṭhañca tathoṅkāraṃ jālandharamataḥ param |
mālavañca kalāntañca devamātṛka meva ca ||

gokarṇañca tathā devi ! māruteśvara meva ca |
aṭṭahāsaṃ ca virajaṃ rājagirimataḥ param ||

pīṭhaṃ kolvagiriñcaiva elāpuramataḥ param |
kāleśvaraṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ praṇavañca jayantikām ||

pīṭhamujjayinīṃ caiva kṣīrikāpīṭhameva ca |
hastināpurakaṃ pīṭhaṃ pīṭhamuḍḍīśameva ca ||

prayāgaṃ caiva ṣaṣṭhīśaṃ māyāpurajaleśvarau |
malayañca mahāpīṭhaṃ śrīśailaṃ ca tathā priye ||

merugiri mehendrañca vāmanañca maheśvari |
hiraṇyapurakaṃ pīṭhaṃ mahālakṣmīpuraṃ tathā ||

uḍḍīyānaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ chāyāpura (pīṭha)mataḥ param |
(pīṭhānyetāni deveśi ! śastāni japakarmasu ||)

pīṭhasthānajapaphalam

phalamāha yoginī hṛdaye �

vārāṇasyāṃ sadāpūjā sampūrṇaphaladāyinī |
tatasta dviguṇā proktā puruṣottama sannidhau ||

tata'pi dviguṇā proktā dvārāvatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
sarvakṣetreṣu tīrtheṣu pūjā dvārāvatīsamā ||

vindhyośataguṇā proktāgaṅgāyāmapi tatsamā |
āryāvarte madhyadeśe brahmāvarte tathaiva ca ||

vindhyavat phaladā proktā prayāge puṣkare tathā |
tataścaturguṇā proktā karatoyā nadītaṭe ||

tataścaturguṇā proktā nadīkuṇḍe ca bhairave |
tataścaturguṇā proktā vālmīkīśvara sannidhau ||

tatra siddheśvarīyonau tato'pi dviguṇā smṛtā |
tataścaturguṇā proktā lauhityanadakuṇḍake ||

tatsamā kāmarūpetu sarvatraiva jale sthale |
devīpūjā tathā śastā kāmarūpe surālaye ||

devīkṣetraṃ kāmarūpe vidyate'nyanna tatsamam |
anyatra viralā devī kāmarūpe gṛhe gṛhe ||

tataścaturguṇā proktā nadīkuṇḍasya mastake |
tato'pi dviguṇā proktā dāruke śivaliṅgake ||

tato'pi dviguṇā proktā śailaputryāḥ svayoniṣu |
tataḥśataguṇaṃ proktaṃ kāmākhyā yonimaṇḍalam ||

kāmākhyāyāṃ mahāmāyā pūjāṃ yadi sakṛccaret |
sa cehalabhate kāmaṃ paratra śivarūpatām ||

eṣu sthāneṣu deveśi ! yadi daivād gatirbhavet |
tadā pūjādikaṃ kṛtvā natvā gacched yathā sukham ||

	kalāmadhye kalā prakṛtistasyāḥ samūhamadhye gatvā pūjādikaṃ 
kṛtvā natvā sukhaṃ gacchedityarthaḥ |

	tathācoktaṃ samayātantre �

strīsamīpe kṛtā pūjā japaśca parameśvari ! |
kāmarūpācchataguṇo'vyayaśca samudīritaḥ ||

kulārṇave'pi �

ekaliṅge śmaśānañca samūhaṃ yoṣitāmapi |
nārīñca raktavasanāṃ dṛṣṭvā vandeta bhaktitaḥ ||

gṛdhraṃ vīkṣya mahākālīṃ jambukīṃ yamadūtikām |
kṛṣṇamārjārabhūkākau śyenaṃ kṣemaṅkarī tathā ||

kurarañca namaskuryādidaṃ mantraṃ paṭhennaraḥ |
kṛśodari ! mahācaṇḍe ! muktakeśi ! balipriye |
kulācāraprasannāsye namaste śaṅkarapriye ||

pitṛbhūmiṃ vyasuṃ dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇamanuvrajan |
praṇabhyā'nena manunā mantrīsukhamavāpnuyāt ||

oṃ ghoradaṃṣṭre ! karālāsye ! kiṭiśabda vi(pra)nādini ! |
gurughoraravāsphāle ! namaste citivāsini ||

raktavastraṃ tathā puṣpaṃ vilokyaṃ tripurāmbikām |
praṇameddaṇḍavad bhūmāvimaṃ mantraṃ paṭhennaraḥ ||

oṃ bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśe tripurebhayanāśini |
bhāgyodaya samutpanne ! namaste varavarṇini ||

kṛṣṇavastraṃ tathā puṣpaṃ rājānaṃ rājaputraka(ruṣa)m |
hastyaśvarathaśastrāṇi phalakān vīrapuruṣān ||

mahiṣe kuladevaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā mahiṣamardinīm |
praṇamed jayadurgāṃ ca sa ca vighnairna lipyate ||

phalakonaṭa iti khyātāḥ | oṃ jayadevi ! jagaddhātri ! tripurādye ! tridaivate !

bhaktebhyo varade devi ! mahiṣaghni ! namo'stute |
madyabhāṇḍaṃ samālokyaṃ matsyaṃ māsaṃ varastriyam ||

dṛṣṭvā ca bhairavīṃ devīṃ praṇamedvimṛśan manum |
oṃ ghoravighna vināśāya kulācāra samṛddhaye ||

namāmi varade ! devi ! muṇḍamālā vibhūṣite |
raktadhārā samākīrṇavadane tvāṃ namāmyaham ||

sarvavighnahare devi ! namaste haravallabhe ! |
yaḥ śivāruditaṃ śrutvā śivadūtīṃ śubhapradām ||

praṇamet sādhako bhaktyā tasya kāmaḥkare sthitaḥ |
eteṣāṃ darśanenaiva yadi naivaṃ prakurvate ||

śaktimantraṃ puraskṛtya tasya siddhirna jāyate |
eteṣāṃ māraṇoccāṭa hiṃsanaṃ vāgurādibhiḥ ||

kurute yadi pāpatmā sa madbhaktaḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
etat kartuṃ prasaktoyasyārthaṃ tamasā likhet ||

nityasaṃketaḥ stavaḥ

kulacūḍāmaṇau nityasaṃketastavamāha �

śrīdevyuvāca �

oṃ tripurā tripureśī ca sundarī purasundarī |
śrīmālinī ca siddhāmbā mahātripurasundarī ||

prakaṭāsyā tathā nidrā guptā guptarāparā |
sampradāyakulā kaularahasyātirahasyagā ||

parāpararahasyā ca tathā kāmeśvarī śubhā |
bhagamālā tathā klinnā bheruṇḍā vahnisundarī ||

mahāvidyeśvarī dūtī tvaritā kulasundarī |
nityā nīlapatākā ca vijayā sarvamaṅgalā ||

jvālāṃśumālinī citrā vaśinī śubhagā kulā |
pūrṇākhyā ca tathā vatsa ! kāmeśī modinī tathā ||

vimalā aruṇā devī jayantī kulabhairavī |
sarveśvarī tathā kaulī vāgiśī sarvakāminī ||

siddheśvarī tathā cogrā durgā mahiṣamardinī |
svapnāvatī śūlinī ca mātaṅgī surasundarī ||

mahākālī mahogrā ca citrarūpā mahodarī |
prāṇavidyā tathaikākṣī caikapādā mahāṃkuśā ||

vāmā śivā tathā jyeṣṭhā surūpā cāruhāsinī |
trikhaṇḍā triśirāsaurī gaurī vindhyanivāsinī ||

kṣobhiṇī nādinī bhadrā lalitā bahurūpikā |
sarvasampatkarī tārā bhāvānī viśvavāsinī ||

kūṭeśvarī mahāvidyā kathitā tava bhairava ! |
upāsakān mahādeva ! śṛṇu caikamanāḥ svayam ||

manuścandraḥ kuberaśca manmathastadanantaram |
lopāmudrāpati(muni)rnandī śatruḥ skandaḥ śivastathā ||

krodhabhaṭṭārakaścaiva śaktirnāma prakīrtitā |
durvāsā vyāsasūryau ca vaśiṣṭhaśca parāśaraḥ ||

aurvo vahniryamaścaiva nir-ṛtirvaruṇastathā |
vāyurviṣṇuḥ svayambhūśca bhairavo gaṇakastathā ||

aniruddho bharadvājo dakṣiṇāmūrti reva ca |
gaṇapāḥ kulāpāścaiva lakṣmīrgaṅgā sarasvatī ||

dhātrī śeṣaḥ pramattaśca unmattaḥ kulabhairavaḥ |
kṣetrapālo hanūmāṃśca dakṣo garuḍa eva ca ||

śukadevaḥ prahlādaśca rāmo rāvaṇa eva ca |
kāśyapaḥ kautsakumbhau ca jamadagnirbhṛgustathā ||

bṛhaspatiryaduśreṣṭhau dattātreyoyudhiṣṭhiraḥ |
arjuno bhīmasenaśca droṇācāyarṇo vṛṣākapiḥ ||

duryodhanastathā kuntī sītā ca rukmiṇī tathā |
satyabhāmā draupadī ca urvaśī ca tilottamā ||

puṣpadanto mahābuddho bālaḥ kālaśca mandaraḥ |
kailāsaḥ kṣīrasindhuśca udadhirhimavāṃstathā ||

nāradaśca mahāvīrāḥ kathitā vīrasādhakāḥ |
mahāvidyāprasādena svasvakarma samāhitāḥ ||

eteṣāṃ vatsa ! nāmāni nityavidyopasevinām |
prātaḥkāle śucirbhūtvā yaḥ paṭhet prayatātmavān ||

pūjākāle śucirbhūtvā prapaṭhet stotramuttamam |
aśucirvā nirālambāmālambya ca kulāntike ||

nityapūjāphalaṃ tasya dadāmi varamīpsitam |
cakrasaṃketakaṃ caiva gurusaṃketakaṃ tathā ||

mantra saṃketakañcaiva nāmasaṃketakaṃ tathā |
samayācāra saṃketaṃ na jñātvā yo'tra vartate ||

japapūjārcanā homastvabhicārāya kalpate |
idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhitvā tu bhavet saṃketavān dhruvam ||

iti kulacūḍāmaṇau nityasaṃketastavarājaḥ samāptaḥ |
dehapāte'pi mokṣaḥ syāt samayācāra pālanāt |

iti phalaśruteḥ kāmyamapi ||

atha śivābaliḥ

viśvasāre � śivābaliṃ nivedyātha toṣayed jagadambikām |
na dadāti baliṃ yastu śivāyāḥ śivatāptaye ||

sa pāpiṣṭho na sahyate kuladevyāḥ prapūjayen | iti yāmala vacanāt tathā 
ca yāmale �

paśurūpāṃ śivāṃ devīṃ yo nā'rcayati nirjane |
śivārāveṇa tasyā'śu sarvaṃ naśyati niścitam ||

japapūjā vidhānāni yatkiñcit sukṛtānica |
gṛhītvā śāyamādāya śivā roditi nirjane ||

tantre �kālikānīti vaktavye tatromā śivarūpiṇī |
śivārūpadharā'yāti parivāragaṇaiḥ saha |
avaśyamannadānena niyataṃ toṣayecchivām ||

śivābalernityatvam

nityaśrāddhe yathā sandhyā � vandane pitṛtarpaṇe |
tathaiva balidāne'pi nityatā kulapūjanam ||

yāmale �

bilvamūle nadītīre śmaśāne vāpi sādhakaḥ |
māṃsapradhānaṃ naivedyaṃ sandhyākāle nivedayet ||

śivabalidānamantraḥ

balimantramāha �

oṃ gṛhya devi ! mahābhāge ! śive ! kālāgnirūpiṇi ! |
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ vyaktaṃ brūhigṛhya baliṃ tava ||

evamucārya dātavyo baliḥ kulajanaiḥ priye ! |
ekayā bhujyate yatra sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca ||

tadaiva sarvaśaktīnāṃ prītiḥ paramadurlabhā |
bhuktvā rauti yadaiśānyāṃ mukhamuttolya susvaram ||

tadaiva maṅgalaṃ devi ! nānyathā bhavati dhruvam |
yadā na gṛhyate nyūnaṃ tadā naiva śubhaṃ bhavet ||

evaṃ jñātvā maheśāni ! śāntisvastyanaṃ caret |
paśuśaktiḥ pakṣiśaktirnaraśaktiryathākramāt ||

pūjanādviguṇaṃ karmasaguṇaṃ sādhayedyataḥ |
tena sarvaprayatnena kartavyaṃ pūjanaṃ mahat ||

śivāpūjādiphalam 

pūjājapādeḥ phalamāha �

dahet tṛṇaṃ yathā vahnistathā śatrūn jayet sadā |
svayaṃ brahmā svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ svayaṃ rudro na saṃśayaḥ ||

ante nirāmayaṃ brahma mantrī bhavati nānyathā |
yā nārī prajapedvidyāṃ sā bhavet parameśvarī ||

kākabandhyā ca yā nārī bandhyā vā bhṛtamugiṇī |
pūjayitvā labhet putraṃ satyaṃ sucirajīvinam ||

svāminī durlabhā sā syād dhanadhānyasamanvitā |
ante na jāyate gaurī satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

yoginīhṛdaye �

mahāvidyāṃ japennityaṃ smaredvāpi samāhitaḥ |
tasya gehe vasellakṣmīrjihvāyāṃ ca sarasvatī ||

hṛdaye ca vaseddevā nārāyaṇa iti śrutiḥ |
brahmā'sti kaṇṭhadeśe ca ahaṃ tiṣṭhāmi sanmukhe ||

ekībhūya samastaiśca devī rakṣati sādhakam |
lakṣamekaṃ japanmantrī mahāpāpāt pramucyate ||

lakṣadvayena pāpāni saptajanmakṛtānyapi |
hanti lakṣatrayeṇaiva janma sāhasrikāṇyapi ||

caturlakṣajapānmantrī vāgīśvarasamo bhavet |
pañcalakṣāddaridro'pi sākṣād vaiśravaṇo bhavet ||

japtvā ṣaḍalakṣakaṃ devi mahāvidyādharo bhavet |
prajapansaptalakṣāṇi khecarīsiddhibhāg bhavet ||

aṣṭalakṣapraṇāṇāntu japtvā vidyāṃ maheśvari |
aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhīśo jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

navalakṣajapāddevi ! rudramūrtirivā'paraḥ |
kartā hartā mahādevi ! loke'pratihataḥ prabhuḥ |
daśalakṣaphalaṃ devi ! varṇituṃ naiva śākyate ||

śrīkrame'pi �

mantrapāśena deveśi ! devatāmānayed dhruvam |
sādhakasya kāryasiddhiṃ kṛtvā devī gamiṣyati ||

atha devīpraṇāmaphalam 

aṣṭottaraśataṃ kuryāt kālikāyāḥ pradakṣiṇam |
sarvakāmaṃ samāsādya paścānmokṣamavāpnuyāt ||

atra pūrvāparārddheyoryathākramaṃ yaḥ sa iti yojyam |

ye namanti narā durgāṃ śraddhayā parayā'nvitāḥ |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya durgālokaṃ vrajanti te ||

ṣāṭhyenāpi namaskāraṃ yaḥ karoti sakṛnnaraḥ |
bhagavatyai tathā'bhaktyā sa gacchati surālayam ||

sarvayajñopavāseṣu sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam |
tatphalaṃ labhate lokaḥ praṇamya śirasā satīm ||

saṃprasāritadeho yo daṇḍavat patito bhuvi |
caṇḍikāpurato bhaktyā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||

śrīmadbhāgavate daśamaḥskandhe rukmiṇīvacanam �

kātyāyani ! mahāmāye ! mahāyoginyadhīśvari ! |
nandagopasutaṃ devi pati me kurute namaḥ ||

	he kātyāyani ! kātyāyanamuninimittaprādurbhūte | he mahāmāye ! 
mahatī cāsau māyā ceti mahāmāyā brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāmapi 
mohahetutvāt mahāmāyā |

viṣṇuḥ śarīragrahaṇamahamīśāna eva ca |
kāritāste yato'tastvāṃ kaḥ stotuṃ śaktimān bhavet ||

	iti mārkaṇḍeya purāṇāt | he mahāyogini ! mahāyogo

jagatsṛṣṭyādikāraṇatriguṇātmakamāyā vidyate yasyāḥ sā mahāyoginī | 
he adhīśvari ! īśvarāṇāṃ śivabhaktibrahmaṇāmīśvaro | śaiva 
sarveśvareśvarī ti mārkaṇḍeyavacanāt | nandagopasutaṃ 
nandanandanatvenābhimataṃ parameśvaraṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ tathaiva 
devatārūpaṃ me mama patiṃ pāṇigrahītāraṃ kuru | tvatprasādaṃ vinā 
kimapi kāryaṃ na siddhayedataste tubhyaṃ namaḥ | 
kāyikavācanikamānasiko namaskāraḥ ||

iti parivrājakācāryaparamahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānandagiri-
kṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ japādiphalanirṇayo nāma 
pañcadaśollāsaḥ || 15 ||




ṣoḍaśollāsaḥ

japādiphalābhāvahetunirṇayaḥ 

mahiṣamardinītantre � śrīdevyuvāca

kena vā japyate vidyā kena vā na prajapyate |
phalābhāvaśca niyataḥ kathaṃ nātha prajāyate ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca

tavaiva viditaṃ sarvaṃ jagadetaccarācaram |
tathāpi śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! rahasyaṃ parameśvari ! ||

saṃsargadoṣaḥ

kalikāle maheśāni ! pāṣaṇḍā bahavo janāḥ |
saṅgadoṣān maheśāni ! tatkṣaṇāddhānitāṃ vrajet ||

tasmātprayatnato devi ! saṃsargaṃ varjayet sudhīḥ |
varaṃ cāṇḍāla saṃsparśaṃ kuryāt tu sādhakottamaḥ ||

tathāpyasiddhijanakaṃ sarvadā taṃ parityajet |
dūṣitāḥ kalikāleṣu bhārate vividhāḥ prajāḥ ||

ata eva maheśāni ! sarve saṃsargadūṣitāḥ |
ghaṭakaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ devi ! saṃsparśe yatnatastyajet ||

bhārate bahavo doṣāḥ kalikāle surārcite ! |
brāhmaṇāḥ kalikāle tu śūdragehe varāṅgane ! ||

purāṇavācanāsaktā dambhamātsaryatatparāḥ |
pāpiṣṭhā brāhmaṇāste tu cāṇḍālasadṛśāḥ priye ! ||

na tūccaret purāṇāni kalau śūdragṛhe dvijaḥ |
śūdragehe maheśāni ! purāṇaṃ prapaṭhedyadi ||

etasya saṅgamātreṇa sarvāvasthā bhavanti hi |
saṃsargāt siddhihāniḥ syāt na sidhyanti kadācana ||

kalau ca bhārate devi ! nindakā bahavo janāḥ |
śivanindāparāḥ kecid viṣṇunindāparāḥpare ||

sarveṣāṃ devatānāñca devīnāñca tathaiva ca |
satataṃ kurvate nindāṃ nātra kuryu vicāraṇām ||

parastrīsaṅgamāccaiva putramutpādayanti ca |
ātmānaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ matvā adhamā bhārate kalau ||

karṇe kaṇṭhe tathā haste hṛdaye naganandini ! |
vidhṛtya tulasīmālāṃ tilakaṃ harimandiram ||

gṛhṇīṣurharināmāni susvarāṇi gṛhe gṛhe |
anyasya sañcayaṃ kuryaḥ pāṣaṇḍā mānavādhamāḥ ||

teṣāṃ pāpaṃ maheśāni ! varṇituṃ naiva śakyate |
adharmanirato bhūtvā harernāma vadedyadi ||

tadā pāpānyaśeṣāṇi nāśayatyeva niścitam |
vihāya sandhyāṃ gāyatrīṃ harināma smaredyadi ||

yānyakṣarāṇi nāmnyeva vasanti ca śucismite ! |
tāvat saṃkhyānyanekāni pāpāni ca pade pade ||

annaṃ jalaṃ tathā puṣpaṃ yaddattaṃ viṣṇave priye ! |
annaṃ viṣṭhāsamaṃ tasya jalaṃ mūtrasamaṃ smṛtam ||

gehe gehe maheśāni ! vaiṣṇavī vaiṣṇavā janāḥ |
saṅkaśa vaiṣṇavā yatra sa deśaḥ patitaḥ sadā ||

gītamattā vādyamattā brāhmaṇā nṛtyatatparāḥ |
gīteṣu jāyate bhāvo brāhmaṇānāṃ gṛhe gṛhe ||

sadbhāvo nahi cārvaṅgi ! narakasya padaṃ dhruvam |
bhārate brāhmaṇāḥ sarve pṛthivyāṃ pādatāḍanam ||

ye kariṣyanti cārvaṅgi ! viṣṇoragre dvijādhamāḥ |
pādatāḍanasaṃkhyātāṃstatpūrvapuruṣān bahūn ||

svargācca narakaṃ devi ! pātayanti na cānyathā |
pūjākāle tu cārvaṅgi ! dhyānānando bhavedyadi ||

tadaiva gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ ca ye kurvanti dvijātayaḥ |
viṣṇudrugāśivāgre tu tadā pāpaṃ vinaśyati ||

gītabhāvamayo bhūtvā yadi nṛtyaṃ karoti hi |
koṭivaṃ śyān samādāya sa dvijo narakaṃ brajet ||

kalikāle bhārate yā brāhmaṇyo gītatatparāḥ |
tathā vādyaratā bhūtvā nṛtyanti cādhamā dvijāḥ ||

tāsāṃ saṃsargamātreṇa sarvaṃ ca hānitāmiyāt |
tasmāt tu yatnato devi ! saṃsargaṃ tairna kārayet ||

kalau tu bhārate varṣe saṃsargānnahi sidhyati |
yadi sidhyati cārvaṅgi ! tadā bahudine gate ||

bhārataṃ kalikāle ca sarvadoṣamayaṃ yataḥ |
tatraikaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi ! vartate mokṣasādhanam ||

mahāmāye ! mahāvidyāmekadhā yadi coccaret |
sarvapāpavinirmukto mahāmokṣaṃ sa gacchati ||

varṇasaṅkarajātīnāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ saha priye |
śāktaḥ śaivo vaiṣṇavaśca saṃsargaṃ yatnatastyajet ||

atha prāyaścittam

pāpamātranāśakaṃ karma prāyaścittam | yathāha tantre �

dehasthasarvapāpasya nāśanaṃ yadi cecchati |
kāmaṃ māyāṃ tathā devi ! manmathaṃ parameśvari ! ||

vidyāmetāṃ japed devi ! tatpāpasyāpanuttaye |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā tasmāt pāpād vimucyate ||

yāmale ca �

jāmbūnadasya mālinyaṃ pariśuddhaṃ yathā'gninā |
anācārasya kaluṣa prāyaścittāgninā tathā ||

prāyaścittaṃ tu pāpānāṃ mūlamaṣṭasahasrakam |
gāyatrīṃ vā japed devi ! sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm ||

	aṣṭasahasrakamiti aṣṭottarasahasramityarthaḥ | gāyatrīṃ 
vaidikagāyatrīm ! śūdrasya tatrā'nadhikārād mūlamaṣṭottarasahasraṃ 
tāntrikagāyatrīṃ vā japet | strīṇāntu śūdratulyatvāt athaivācāraḥ ||

atha dhṛtakavacanāśa-prāyaścittam

yāmale �

vidhṛtaṃ kavacaṃ devi ! yadi naśyati karhicit |
tadupāyaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalānane ||

upaviśya tathācamya bhūtaśuddhimathā caret |
ṣaṭcakrāṇi vicintyā'tha guruṃ śirasi cintayet ||

anuloma-vilomābhyāṃ mātṛkābījasaṃpuṭam |
kavacaṃ tatpaṭhed devi ! hyarkāvṛttamanukramāt ||

tato japen mahāvidyāṃ sahasraṃ vā śataṃ kramāt |
vilikhya kavacaṃ devi ! raktasūtreṇa veṣṭayet ||

svarṇenā'pi punardevi ! veṣṭayet tat sudurlabham |
veṣṭayitvā mahādevi ! svarṇaiḥ paramadurlabham |
dhārayetat tpratiṣṭhāpyaṃ nūtanaṃ kavacaṃ tataḥ ||

naṣṭakavacapratiṣṭhākramaḥ

pratiṣṭhākramamāha �

pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyaiḥ snāpayitvā śubhe'hani |
prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantreṇa prāṇāstatra niveśayet ||

sampūjya devatārūpaṃ kavacaṃ sarvakāmadam |
tato japenmahāvidyāṃ sahasraṃ vā śataṃ kramāt |
dhārayet tanmahādevi ! yathāsthāneṣu sādhakaḥ ||

iti kavacanāśaprāyaścittam |

atha yantranāśaprāyaścitam

navaratneśvare �

yadi pratiṣṭhataṃ yantraṃ daivād devi ! vinaśyati |
upoṣaṇamahorātramādareṇa samācaret ||

yena svarṇādinā yantraṃ dravyeṇa parinirmitam |
vilikhya yantraṃ tatpatre devatāṃ paripūjayet ||

upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śaktitaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
ayutaṃ prajapenmantraṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi ||

mantrī viloḍya tattoyaṃ pītvā bhakṣaṇamācaret |
tāvat kālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ yāvadyantraṃ na kārayet ||

punaryantraṃ navaṃ ramyamāharecchuddhayā'nvitaḥ |
āhṛtya ca navaṃ yantraṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ tasya kārayet |
tataḥ pratiṣṭhite tasmin pūrvavat pūjanaṃ caret ||

atha pūjākāle yantrādipatanaprāyaścittam

yantraṃ yadi pated devi ! pūjākāle kadācana ||

liṅgaṃ vāpi śivo vāpi tatphalaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
āyurhānirdhanaglānirbandhunāśastathaiva ca ||

bhavatīti viniścitya prāyaścittamathācaret |
trirātramekarātraṃ vā upavāsaṃ samācaret ||

mūlavidyāṃ japed devi ! sahasraṃ sāṣṭakaṃ tathā |
japāpuṣpaiśca juhuyācchatamaṣṭottaraṃ tathā ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayedbhaktyā yajedyantraṃ samāhitaḥ |

yantramiti śivāderapyupalakṣaṇam ||

mālāpatanaprāyaścittam

mālā yadi pateddhastāt tathaiva ca vinaśyati |
sahasraṃ tatra saṃjapya brāhmaṇān bhojayettataḥ ||

bhojanaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tu sarvāniṣṭasya nāśanam |
gāyatrīṃ vā japed devi ! śataṃ sāṣṭaṃ samāhitaḥ ||

gāyatrīṃ japediti ! tattad devatāyā gāyatrīṃ japedityarthaḥ |

tataḥ sampūjya tāṃ mālāṃ gṛhṇīyāt punareva hi |
evaṃ kṛte varārohe ! na vighnairabhibhūyate ||

atha mālāvināśaprāyaścittam

mālā yadi vinaṣṭā syāt pūrvavat sakalaṃ caret |
tataścāpyaparāṃ mālāṃ tajjātīyāṃ varānane ||

samāhṛtya pratiṣṭhāpya gṛhṇīyāt punareva hi |
yāmale � mahāpatakayukto'pi gāyatrīṃ prajayedyadi |
satyaṃ satyaṃ mahādevi ! mukto bhavati tatkṣaṇāt ||

aśucirna spṛśenmālāṃ karabhraṣṭāṃ na kārayet |
śabde jāte bhavedrogaḥ karabhraṣṭā vināśakṛt |
chinne sūtre bhavenmṛtyustasmādyatnaparo bhavet ||

tantrāntare �

hastāt patati cenmālā na japtavyā tu sā budhaiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ vidhātavyaṃ japtvā mantraṃ sahasrakam ||

sahasrakamiti | aṣṭādhikasahasramityarthaḥ |

jīrṇe sūtre punaḥ sūtrairgrathayitvā śataṃ japet |

śatamiti � mūlamantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japedityarthaḥ |

chinnā bhavati cenmālā pūjāṃ kuryāt tato'dhikām |
punargrathitvā tāṃ mālāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ pūrvavaccaret |
tatastu prajapenmālāṃ na tatra doṣabhāgbhavet ||

atha gurukrodhopasamanaprāyaścittam

śive ruṣṭe gurustrātā gurau ruṣṭe na kaścana |
upavāsaṃ gurukrodhe kṛtvā taṃ tu prasādayet ||

yāvat prasādaṃ nāyāti tāvadvai bhojanaṃ tyajet |
gurau prasanne bhuñjīta evaṃ doṣā na jāyate ||

athā'niveditabhojanaprāyaścittam

matsyasūkte �

anivedya na bhuñjītabhakṣya bhojyādikaṃ ca yat |
annaṃ viṣṭhā payo mūtraṃ yadviṣṇoraniveditam ||

viṣṇupadaṃ svasvasādhyadevatā param | anyatrāpi �

adattaṃ naiva bhoktavyamabhakṣyeṇa samaṃ smṛtam |
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ mūlamannapānauṣadhaṃ priye |
anivedyaṃ na bhuñjīta bhuñjītaiva niveditam ||

kālikāpurāṇe �

mahādhīro munirvāpi brāhmaṇaścetaro'pi vā |
yadyadbhakṣyaṃ samarthastu prakṛṣṭaṃ syādyathā tathā |
pradadyādiṣṭadevebhyo gṛhṇīyācca tathā svayam ||

yāmale �yadyathā bhakṣyate bhakṣyaṃ tattathaiva pradāpayet |
anyathā tat pradānena na tat phalamavāpnuyāt ||

	yadyad dravyaṃ yena prakāreṇa bhoktavyaṃ tad dravyamanyathā 
prakāreṇa na dātavyam |

anivedya harerbhuñjan saptajanmani nārakī |

harerityupalakṣaṇam | tathācoktaṃ kālikāpurāṇe �

phalaṃ puṣpaṃ ca tāmbūlamannapānādikañca yat |
adattvā tanmahādevyai na bhoktavyaṃ kadācana ||

anivedya na bhuñjīta prāyaścittībhavennaraḥ |
devyāścāṣṭaśataṃ mantraṃ japtvā pūto bhavennaraḥ ||

devyā ityupalakṣaṇaṃ svasvopāsita devatānām | tathācoktaṃ yāmale �

anivedya maheśāni ! bhuñjānaḥ pātakī bhavet |
iṣṭamantraṃ śataṃ japtvā tasmāt pāpād viśudhyati ||

	na ca � yo yad devārcanarataḥ sa tannaivedyabhug bhavet | iti vacanād 
devatāntaranaivedyabhakṣaṇaṃ na kartavyamiti vācyam | agrāhyaṃ 
śivanirmālyamitivacanamajñāninām, jñāninām tu 
prasādabhakṣaṇamevāvaśyakam |

tathā coktam yāmale �

śivadattaṃ viṣṇudattaṃ girijādattameva ca |
prāptimātreṇa bhoktavyamanyathā pātakī bhavet ||

agnipurāṇe �

śivadattaṃ viṣṇudattaṃ pārvatīdattameva ca |
naivedyamudare kṛtvā naraḥ sāyujyamāpnuyāt ||

laiṅge �

liṅge tyaktvā tu naivedyaṃ bhuṃkte mohādvimūḍhadhīḥ |
kumbhīpāke ca narake pacyate nātra saṃsayaḥ ||

etattu śivamastakadattaṃ � naivedyaparam | skandapurāṇe �

bāṇaliṅge svayambhūve sphāṭike mṛdi saṃsthite |
ataḥ śatakratoḥ puṇyaṃ śambhornaivedya bhakṣaṇāt ||

ādityapurāṇe �

nirmālyaṃ dhārayedyastu śirasā pārvatīpateḥ |
rājasūyasya yajñasya phalamāpnotyanuttamam ||

tathā ca liṅgārcanatantre �

brahmatvaṃ prāptavān brahmā yogaṃ cānye maharṣayaḥ |
viṣṇutvamapi viṣṇuśca śivaḥ kena na sevyate ||

nirmālyaṃ sarvapāpāni śambhorharati niścitam |
niveditañca naivedyaṃ bhuñjīta nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

na hi ye bhuñjate mūrkhā narakaṃ taiḥ prapadyate |
naivedyaṃ copabhuñjīta dattvā tadbhaktiśāline |
anyathā naiva siddhiḥ syādarcako narakaṃ vrajet ||

	ityādi nānātantrapurāṇavacanaiḥ niveditamātraṃ bhoktavyaṃ na tu 
aniveditamiti niścayaḥ ||

naivedyanindakaṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛtyanti yoginīgaṇāḥ |
raktapānodyataḥ sarvā māṃsāsthicarvaṇodyatāḥ ||

tasmānniveditaṃ devyai dṛṣṭvā śrūtvā ca mānuṣaḥ |
na nindet manasā vācā kuṣṭhavyādhiparāṅmukhaḥ ||

iti kālīkulasarvasvavacanāt | kumārītantre �

devatānāñca naivedyaṃ strībhyo dadyānna kutracit |

tantre � svaśaktibhyo'nyaśaktibhyo dattvā ca svayamāharet |

yāmale � anekadhā paśorannaṃ bhuñjate ye ca sādhakāḥ |
tebhyaḥ prakupyate devī tat saṃsargaṃ na kārayet ||

tantre �

anasthiprāṇisaṃghātaṃ hattvā ca daśakaṃ japet |
hatvā ca pakṣiṇaṃ sarvaṃ trirekādaśakaṃ japet ||

yāmale �

parvaṇyapūjya deveśīṃ guruṃ śaktiñca śaktitaḥ |
adattvā ca baliṃ tatra mūlamaṣṭaśataṃ japet ||

varṇasaṅkarajātīyairvaiṣṇavaistu saha priye ! |
śāktaḥ śaivo vaiṣṇavaśca saṃsargaṃ yatnatastyajet ||

teṣāṃ mukhaṃ samālokya sūryadarśanamācaret |
iṣṭamantraṃ śataṃ japtvā tasmāt pāpādviśudhyati || iti ||

vaidikakarmamātram iṣṭadevatāprītyarthaṃ kāryam | tantre �

devatāprītikāmastu karma kuryāt sadāśive |
anyakāmastu cetkarma karoti vidhimohitaḥ ||

phalaṃ na jāyate tasya devastasmai prakupyati |

anyacca �

ye tvakāmā narāḥ samyagkarma kurvanti śobhane ! |
teṣāṃ dadāti viśveśo bhagavān muktimīśvaraḥ ||

	sakāmānāṃ sāyujyādi muktiḥ, sāyujyaṃ na parāmuktiḥ 
śarīrasambandhāt | akāmānāṃ nirvāṇameva muktiḥ, paramapururthatvāt 
| na ca punarāvartate iti śruteḥ |

nanu � avaśyameva bhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubham |
māmuktaṃ kṣīyate karma kalpakoṭiśatairapi ||

	iti vacanāt karmamātrasya bhoganāśyatve 
bahujanmārjitānantakarmaṇāt | bhogena vinivartanāsambhavād jīvasya 
kathaṃ muktiriti cet | ucyate �

devatā prītikāmastu karma kuryāt sadāśive |
devastu prītimāpanno bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ ||

akāmaḥ sāttviko loke yatkiñcid vinivedayet |
sa tatsthānamāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati |
atyantaduḥkhaviraho muktirityucyate budhaiḥ ||

yāmale �

dharmādharmāviti proktau dvau pāśau karmasaṃjñitai |
devatāprītikarmāṇi na bandhāya vimuktaye |
mūrdhnā pratīchate devastatkāmena kṛtaṃ tu yat ||

	ityādivacanāt vartamāna � karmaṇām īśvara 
prītimātrasādhakatvenā'dṛṣṭo janakatvāt 
prārabdhātiriktakarmaṇāmīśvaraprasādalabdha jñānena nāśāt 
prārabdhānāñca bhogādeva kṣayālliṅgadehanāśe vimuktiḥ syāditi || 
liṅgadehamāhagāndharve �

pañcaprāṇamanobuddhirdaśendriyasamanvitam |
śarīraṃ saptadaśakaṃ sūkṣmaitalliṅgamucyate ||

śrībhāgavate dvādaśe �

ghaṭe bhinneghaṭākāśamākāśaṃ syādyathā purā |
evaṃ dehe mṛte jīvo brahma sampadyate punaḥ ||

dehe mṛte liṅgadehe dhvaste ityarthaḥ | anyathā punaḥ 
punarjanmamṛtyurbhavatyeva |

tathā coktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare �

tatkṣaṇādeva gṛhṇāti śarīramātivāhikam |
kevalaṃ tanmanuṣyāṇāṃ nānyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ kvarcit ||

tataḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe bāndhavaiḥ sa kṛte naraḥ |
pūrṇe saṃvatsare dehamato'nyaṃ pratipadyate ||

tataḥ na narake yāti svargaṃ vā svena karmaṇā |

	tatkṣaṇāt mṛtyukṣaṇāt | pretaśarīrañca 
pūrvadeharūpamatyantagatimat | tadāha mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam �

vāyuprasārite dehamato'nyaṃ pratipadyate |
tatpramāṇa-vayovasthasaṃsthānaṃ prāgbhavaṃ yathā ||

iti śrīparivrājakācāryaparamahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ saṃsargadoṣādinirṇayo nāma 
ṣoḍaśollāsaḥ || 16 ||




saptadaśollāsaḥ

atha kuṇḍavidhiḥ

govindavṛndāvane �

bhūmeḥ parigrahaṃ kuryādyāvadāyatanaṃ bhavet |
gururācamya vidhivadāsane upaviśya ca ||

māṣabhaktibaliṃ dadyādyathoktavidhinā tataḥ |
oṃ svargapātālamadhye ca ye santi vāstudevatāḥ ||

gṛhṇantvimaṃ baliṃ dattaṃ tuṣṭāyāntu svamandiram |
mātaro bhūtavetālā ye cānye balikāṃkṣiṇaḥ ||

devyāḥ pāriṣadā ye ca te ca gṛhṇantvimaṃ balim |

maṇḍapanirmāṇam

śāradāyām �

puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu maṇḍapaṃ racayecchubham |
pañcabhiḥ saptabhirhastairnavabhirvā mitāntaram ||

ṣoḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ catvārasteṣu madhyagāḥ |
aṣṭahastasamucchrāyāḥ saṃsthāpya dvādaśā'mitaḥ ||

caturviṃśāṃgulaṃ hastaṃ tantravedavido viduḥ |
gṛhādikuṇḍakaraṇe vedikāmaṇḍape tathā |
mānāṃgulena kartavyaṃ nānyairvāpi kadācana ||

mānāṃgulilakṣaṇam

mānāṃgulimāha tantre �

karturdakṣiṇahastasya madhyamāṃguliparvaṇaḥ |
madhyasya dairghyamānena mānāṃgulirudāhṛtā ||

maṇḍapasthānaparimāṇam 

siddhāntaśekhare �

sthalādarkāṃgulocchvāyaṃ maṇḍapasthānamīritam |
nārikeladalairvaśaiśchādayen maṇḍapaṃ tataḥ |
caturdvāraiḥ samāyuktaṃ kadalīstambhasaṃyutaiḥ ||

āmrapatrasamāyukta rajjubhiḥ pariveṣṭhitam |
aṣṭadikṣu dhvajānaṣṭau caturdikpālavarṇataḥ ||

dikpālavarṇaḥ

dikpālavarṇamāha śāradāyām �

pīto rakto sito dhūmraḥ śuklo dhūmraḥ sitāvubhau |
gauro'ruṇaḥ kramādete varṇataḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

kuṇḍaśarīram

nāsti homo vinā kuṇḍaṃ tasmāt kuṇḍaṃ praśasyate |
kuṇḍasya rūpaṃ jānīyāt paramaṃ prakṛtervapuḥ ||

prācyāṃ śiraḥ samākhyātaṃ bāhu dakṣiṇasaumyayoḥ |
udaraṃ kuṇḍamityuktaṃ yoniḥ pādau tu paścime ||

caturasrakuṇḍalakṣaṇam

pūrvāparāyataṃ sūtraṃ vinyaseddhastamānataḥ |
dakṣiṇottaragaṃ sūtraṃ tathaiva ca pravinyaset ||

tadagrayoḥ pravinyasya tathā sūtracatuṣṭayam |
caturasraṃ mahākuṇḍaṃ sarvayoga prakīrtitam ||

muṣṭimātramitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ śatārdhe saṃpracakṣate |
śatahome'ratnimātraṃ hastamātraṃ sahasrake ||

dvihastamayute lakṣe caturhastamudīritam |
niyute ṣaṭkaraṃ proktaṃ koṭyāmaṣṭakaraṃ smṛtam ||

khātaparimāṇam

yāvān kuṇḍasya vistāraḥ khananaṃ tāvadīritam |
caturviṃśatyaṃgulañca yavaśūnyaṃ sahasrake ||

tato dvihastamāne tu triṃśadaṃgulakaṃ smṛtam |
caturhaste madhyamānamaṣṭatriṃśatprakalpitam ||

aṃgulaṃ yavaśūnyaṃ syāllakṣahome prakīrtitam |
ṛtuhaste tathā mānaṃ catvāriṃśattrayādhikam ||

aṃgulaṃ niyute proktamadhikaṃ yavacatuṣṭayam |
catvāriṃśadaṣṭayutaṃ yavasaptasamanvitam |
vasuhaste tathā mānamaṃgulaṃ kathitaṃ budhaiḥ ||

śobhanaṃ kamalaṃ kuryāt kuṇḍamadhye sarandhrakam |
sarveṣāmeva kuṇḍānāṃ mekhalāstisra eva ca ||

ekāṃgulaṃ vihāyā'nte mekhalāstasya kārayet |
arddhāṃgulapramāṇena kaṇṭhañca varddhayet kramāt ||

tantrāntare �

koṇasūtrapramāṇena dvihastaṃ kuṇḍamuddharet |
evaṃ lakṣādike jñeyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tatra vidhānataḥ ||

	ekahasta kuṇḍasya koṇasūtreṇa īśānakoṇasūtreṇa parito 
yanmānaṃ tadeva pāribhāṣikaṃ dvihastādikuṇḍamānaṃ na tu 
prakṛtahastād dvaiguṇyādikamiti ||

mekhalānirūpaṇam

idānīṃ mekhalādīnāṃ mānaṃ tasya nigadyate |
kuṇḍānāṃ yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ mekhalānāñca tādṛśam ||

kuṇḍānāṃ mekhalāstisro muṣṭimātre tu tāḥ kramāt |
utsedhāyāmato jñeyā dvayekārddhaṃgulisammitāḥ ||

yugāṃgulaṃ yonimānaṃ yonyagramekamaṃgulam |
yugāṃgulaṃ nābhipadmaṃ śatārddhe saṃpracakṣate ||

aratnimātrakuṇḍe tāstridvayekāṃgulikātmikāḥ |
kartavyā mekhalā yoniścaturaṃgulasammitā ||

ekāṃgulaṃ tu yonyagraṃ kuryādīṣadadhomukham |
aṃgulitritayaṃ caiva nābhipadmaṃ suśobhanam ||

ekahastamite kuṇḍe vedāgninayanāṃgulāḥ |
kartavyā mekhalā yoniṃ kuryāccaiva ṣaḍaṃgulam ||

vedāṃgulaṃ nābhipadmaṃ yonyagraikāṃgulaṃ smṛtam |
kuṇḍe dvihaste tā jñeyā rasavedaguṇāṃgulāḥ ||

yoniḥ saptāṃgulopetā yonyagraṃ cāṃgulidvayam |
pañcāṃgulaṃ nābhipadmaṃ kuryāccaiva manoharam ||

caturhastamite kuṇḍe vasutarkayugāṃgulāḥ |
kartavyā mekhalāstisro yonyagraṃ cāṃgulitrayam ||

yoniraṣṭāṃgulopetā nābhipadmaṃ ṣaḍaṃgulam |
kuṇḍe rasakare tāḥ syurdaśāṣṭartvaṃgulānvitāḥ ||

yonirna vāṃgulopetā yonyagraṃ caturaṃgulam |
saptāṃgulaṃ nābhipadmaṃ kuryācca sumanoharam ||

aṣṭahastamite kuṇḍe bhānupaṃktyaṣṭakāṃgulāḥ |
yonirdaśāṃgulopetā kartavyā'dhomukhī tathā ||

pañcāṃgulaṃ tu yonyagraṃ kuryādaṣṭāṃgualṃ tathā |
nābhipadmaṃ lakṣahome tantravit parikalpitam ||

hoturagre tu tāṃ yoniṃ mekhalānāṃ paristhitām |
gajakumbhavadākārāṃ kuryādoṣadadhomukhīm ||

paścimābhimukhīṃ yoniṃ kuṇḍakoṇeṣu nārpayet |
evaṃ samastakuṇḍānāṃ vyavastheyaṃ prakīrtitā ||

nālanirūpaṇam

sthalādārabhya nālaṃ syādyonyā madhye sarandhrakam |

sarandhrakamityubhayatra sambadhyate | tathā coktaṃ rudrayāmale �

yonyā madhye bilaṃ kuryāt tadājyagrāhisaṃjñakam |

sthalaniyamamāha kriyāsāre �

homasthānād bahiḥsthānaṃ sthalamityabhidhīyate |

gautamīye �

sūkṣmāgraṃ sthūlamūlañca sarandhrakaṃ nālamiṣyate |

sammohanatantre �

mūlaṃ madhyaṃ tathā cāgraṃ kramācca ṣaṭcatustrikam |

tathā ca trayodaśāṃgulīdīrghaṃ nālamityarthaḥ |

nālamekhalayormadhye paridheḥ sthāpanāya ca |
randhraṃ kuryāttato vidvān dvitīyamekhalopari ||

kuṇḍadoṣāḥ

kuṇḍadoṣamāha viśvakarmā �

khātādhike bhaved rogī hīne caiva dhanakṣayaḥ |
vakrakuṇḍe ca santāpo maraṇaṃ chinnamekhale ||

mekhalārahite śoko hyadhike vittasaṃkṣayaḥ |
bhāryāvināśakaṃ kuṇḍaṃ proktaṃ yonyā vinā kṛtam ||

apatya dhvasanaṃ proktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ yatkaṇṭhavarjitam |
kuṇḍamevaṃ vidhaṃ na syāt sthaṇḍilaṃ vā samāśrayet ||

sthaṇḍilalakṣaṇam

yāmale �

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ sthaṇḍile vā samācaret |
hastamātre tu tatkuryād bālukābhiḥ suśobhanam |
aṃgulotsedhasaṃyuktaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ ||

caturasraṃ catuṣkoṇamityarthaḥ |

iti śrīparivrājakācāryaparamahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānanda-
girikṛtāyāṃ śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ kuṇḍanirṇayo nāma 
saptadaśollāsaḥ || 17 ||



aṣṭādaśollāsaḥ

atha homavidhiḥ

athā'gnijananaṃ vakṣye sarvatantrānusārataḥ |
gomayena samālipya kuṇḍaṃ sarvatra mantravit |
sāmānyārghyaṃ prakalpyā'tha pañcagavyairviśodhayet ||

aṣṭādaśakuṇḍasaṃskārāḥ

śāradāyām �

aṣṭādaśoktāḥ saṃskārāḥ kuṇḍānāṃ tantradeśitāḥ |
vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ matam ||

tenaiva tāḍanaṃ darbhairvarmaṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ smṛtam |
astreṇa khananoddhārau hṛnmantreṇa prapūraṇam |
samīkaraṇamastreṇa secanaṃ varmaṇā matam ||

kuṭṭanaṃ hetimantraṇa varmamantreṇa mārjanam |
vilepanaṃ kalārūpakalpanaṃ tadanantaram ||

trisūtrīkaraṇaṃ paścād hṛdayenā'rcanaṃ matam |
astreṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ hṛnmantreṇa kuśaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||

catuṣpathaṃ tanutreṇa tanuyādakṣapāṭanam |
yāge kuṇḍāni saṃskuryāt saṃskārairebhirīritaiḥ ||

	asyārthaḥ � kuṭṭanaṃ dṛḍhīkaraṇam | vilepanaṃ gomayodakena | 
kalārūpakalpanaṃ somasūryāgnikalātmakacintanam | trisūtrīkaraṇaṃ 
raktasūtreṇa triḥpariveṣṭanam | vajrīkaraṇaṃ vajrarūpeṇa cintanam | 
catuṣpathaṃ caturasrīkaraṇam | akṣapāṭanamindriyodghāṭanam iti |

prakārāntarasaṃskāraḥ

athavā tāni saṃskuryāccaturbhirvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |
tisrastisro likhellekhā hṛdā prāgudagagrikāḥ |
prāgagrāṇāṃ smṛtā devā mukundeśapurandarāḥ ||

rekhāṇāmudagagrāṇāṃ brahmavaivasvatendavaḥ ||

athavā trikoṇaṃ tadvahiḥ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tadbahiraṣṭadalapadmaṃ parikalpayet |

caturasraṃ caturdvāramevaṃ vā vahnimaṇḍalam |
kuṇḍasyottarabhāge ca trirekhā hastamānataḥ ||

dakṣiṇottaratastadvallikhedrekhātrayaṃ śubham |
arghyādbhiḥ prokṣya sarvaṃ hi pañcaśuddhiṃ samācaret ||

sarvāṇi tāreṇā'bhyukṣyeti śeṣaḥ ||

pañcaśuddhiḥ

pañcaśuddhimāha śāradāyām �

vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ matam |
tāḍanaṃ hetimantreṇa kavacenā'tha lepayet ||

astreṇa rakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ saṃskāramācaret |
tato vahnegaryopīṭhamarcayet karṇikopari |
dharmaṃ jñānañca vairāgyamaiśvaryamagnito yajet ||

pūrvādi dikṣu cāpūrvānatha dharmādikāna yajet |
madhye ca pūjayedvahnernavaśaktīrvidhānavit ||

pītā śvetā'ruṇā kṛṣṇā dhūmrā tīvrā sphuliṅginī ||

rucirā jvālinī proktā kramaśo nava śaktayaḥ |
pūjayenmaṇḍalaṃ teṣāṃ kalābhiḥ sahamantravit ||

aṃ arkamaṇḍalaṃ ṅentaṃ tathā uṃ somamaṇḍalam |
maṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ tadvadarcayed gandhapuṣpakaiḥ ||

vāgīśvarīmṛtusnātāṃ nīlendīvarasannibhām |
vāgīśvareṇa sahitāmupacāraiḥ samarcayet ||

agnipraṇayanam

vihitāgnimāha tantre �

sūryakāntādisambhūtaṃ yadvā śrotriyagehajam |

agnipraṇayanamāha �

pātrāntareṇa pihite tāmrapātrādike śubhe |
agnipraṇayanaṃ kuyāccharāve vāpi tādṛśe ||

yattu smṛtisāre �

śarāve bhinnapātre vā kapāle volmuke'pi vā |
nāgnipraṇayanaṃ kuryād vyādhihāni-bhayāvahema || it ||

tasya � mukhyapātrasambhave śarāvo na grāhya ityatra tātparyam |
ānīyāstreṇa nai-ṛtyāṃ kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet |
astreṇaiva ca tatkāṣṭhaṃ nair-ṛtyāṃ varjayet priye ||

śāradāyām �

saṃskuryāt taṃ yathānyāyaṃ deśiko vīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |
odarya vaindavāgnibhyāṃ bhaumasyaikaṃ smaran vasoḥ ||

yojayedvahnibījena caitanyaṃ pāvake tadā |
tāreṇa mantritaṃ kṛtvā dhenumudrāmṛtīkṛtam ||

astreṇa rakṣitaṃ paścāt tanutreṇā'vaguṇṭhitam |
arcitaṃ triḥparibhrāmya kuṇḍasyopari deśikaḥ |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tadā tāramantroccāraṇapūrvakam ||

ātmano'bhimukhaṃ vahniṃ jānuspṛṣṭamahītalaḥ |
śivabījadhiyā devyā yonāvenaṃ vinikṣipet ||

samayātantre �

kuśenācchādya tadyoniṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ paṭaṃ nyaset |
tato devāya devyai ca dadyādācamanīyakam |
garbhanāḍyā dhṛtaṃ dhyāyedvahnirūpaṃ hariṃ guruḥ ||

harirityupalakṣaṇaṃ svasveṣṭadevānām | samayātantre �

devyā vāmakare dadyādrakṣārthaṃ darbhakaṅkaṇam |
bhūṣābhirbhūṣayeddevīṃ trailokyotpattimātṛkām |

jihvāmantraḥ

rephavāyvarghīśairyuktā nādabinduvibhūṣitāḥ |
sādiyāntāśca jihvānāṃ manavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

pāyau liṅge ca nāmau ca hṛdaye kaṇṭhamūlataḥ |
lambikāyāṃ bhruvormadhye jihvā jvālāruco nyaset ||

jihvāstastrividhāḥ proktā guṇabhinneṣu karmasu |
hiraṇyā gaganā raktā kṛṣṇā'nyā suprabhā matā ||

bahurūpā suvarṇānyā tṛtīyā bhadralohitā |
lohitā'nantaraṃ śvetā dhūminī ca karālikā ||

rājasyo rasanā vahne vihitā kāmyakarmasu |
viśvamūrtisphuliṅginyai dhūmravarṇā manojavā ||

lohitānyā karālāsyā kālī tāmasya īritāḥ |
etāḥ sapta niyujyante krūrakarmasu mantribhiḥ ||

jihvādhipati devatā

amartya-pitṛ-gandharva-yakṣa-nāga-piśācakāḥ |
rākṣasāḥ sapta jihvānāmīritā hyadhidevatāḥ ||

vahneraṅgamanuṃ nyasyeta tanāvuktena vartmanā |
sahasrārciḥ svastipūrṇaṃ uttiṣṭhapuruṣastathā ||

dhumavyāpī saptajihvo dhanurdhara itīritāḥ |
ṣaḍaṅgamanavaḥ proktā jātibhiḥ saha saṃyutāḥ ||

mūrtinyāsaḥ

śāradāyām �

mūrtiraṣṭau tanau nyasyeddeśiko jātavedasaḥ |
mūrdhniskandhe vāmapārśve kaṭyandhu kaṭipārśvake ||

tathā skandhe ca vinyasyet prādakṣiṇyakrameṇa tu |
jātavedāḥ saptajihvo havyavāhanasaṃjñakaḥ ||

aśvodarajasaṃjño'nyaḥ punarvaiśvānarāhvayaḥ |
kaumāratejāḥ syād viśvamukho devamukhaḥ smṛtāḥ ||

tārāgnaye padādyoḥ syurnatyantā vahnimūrtayaḥ |
evaṃ vinyastadehaḥ san jvālayenmanunā'munā ||

jvālayediti jvālinīmudrāṃ pradarśya ityarthaḥ | tallakṣaṇaṃ rāghavīye �

maṇibandhau samau kṛtvā karau tu prasṛtāṃgulī |
madhyame milite kṛtvā antaraṃguṣṭhakau kṣipet |
mudrā sā jvālinī proktā vahnerjvālanakarmaṇi || iti ||

vahniprajvālanamantraḥ

śāradāyām � citpiṅgalaṃ hana daha paca yugmānudīrya ca |
sarvajñājñāpaya svāhā mantro'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ ||

agniṃ prajvālitaṃ vande jātavedaṃ hutāśanam |
suvarṇavarṇamamalaṃ samiddhaṃ viśvato mukham ||

upatiṣṭheta vidhivanmanunā'nena pāvakam |

sāradāyām � pariṣiñcet tatastoyai viśuddhairmekhalopari |
darbhaiḥ kāṣṭhaiśca śuddhaiśca mūlamadhyāgracchāditaiḥ ||

saṃstaredvidhivanmantrī pradakṣiṇāvaśaktataḥ |
evaṃ saṃstaraṇaṃ kuryādvarjayitvātmano diśam ||

gaṇeśvaravimarśinyām � prāgagrairudagraiśca darbhairvahniṃ paristaret |
yajñavṛkṣodbhavaṃ tadvat kāṣṭhaiśca paridhitrayam ||

madhye tu mekhalāyāntu saṃstaret tantravittamaḥ |
atha cet sthaṇḍile mantrī bhūmau sarvaṃ paristaret ||

paridhilakṣaṇam

yajñakāṣṭhasamudbhataḥ prādeśapramitaḥ śubhaḥ |
paridhiḥ kathitaḥ sarvairdeśikaistantravittamaiḥ ||

nikṣepeddikṣu paridhīn prācīvarjyaṃ gurūttamaḥ |
prādakṣiṇyena sampūjyāsteṣu brahmādimūrtayaḥ |
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya vahnidevaṃ vibhāvayet ||

vahnidhyānaṃ

vahnerdhyānaṃ yathā � trinayanamaruṇābhaṃ baddhamauliṃ tu śuklāṃ
śukamaruṇamanekā kalpamambhojasaṃstham |
abhimatavaraśaktiṃ svastikābhītimuccai-
rnamatakamalamālālaṃkṛtāṃśaṃ kṛśānum ||

evaṃ hi manasā dhyāyecchāntikādau gurūttamaḥ ||

kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇagatervarṇaṃ dhyāyenmāraṇakarmaṇi |
mūrtiraṣṭau samabhyarcya ṣaṭkoṇe tu ṣaḍaṅgakam |
madhye ṣaṭsvapi koṇeṣu jihvā jvālāruco yajet ||

keśareṣūktamārgeṇaḥ pūjayedaṅgadevatā |
daleṣu pūjayenmūrtīḥ śaktisvastikadhāriṇīḥ |
lokapālāṃstato dikṣu pūjayeduktalakṣaṇām ||

śāradāyām �

dhyātaṃ vahniṃ yajenmadhye gandhādyairmanunā'munā |
vaiśvānarajātavedapade paścādihāvaha ||

lohitākṣapadasyā'nte sarvakarmāṇi sādhaya |
vahnijāyāvadhiḥ prokto mantraḥ pāvakavallabhaḥ ||

kulārṇave �

brahmāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe'bhyarcya ghṛtasthālīṃ prapūjayet |
ājyasthālīṃ samānīya kṣālayedastramantrataḥ ||

kuṇḍāṅgārāmsamuttolya nyaset tatrā'stramantrataḥ |
tasyāmājyaṃ vinikṣipya jānīyāt tapanaṃ hi tat ||

śāradāyām �

tasyāmājyaṃ vinikṣipya saṃskṛtaṃ vīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |
niruhya vāyavya'ṅgārān hṛdā teṣu niveśayet |
idaṃ tāpanamuddiṣṭaṃ deśikaistantravedibhiḥ ||

arṇave �

prajvālya kuśagucchantu ājye kṣiptvā'nale kṣipet |
abhidyotanamityuktaṃ sarvatra sarvakarmasu ||

śāradāyām �

dīptena darbhayugmena bhīrājyājyaṃ sa varmaṇā |
agnau visarjayed rdarbhamabhidyotanamīritam ||

punaḥ kṛśān samujvālya nikṣipedājyamadhyataḥ |
mūlamantreṇa matimānājyasaṃskāra īritaḥ ||

sandīpya darbhayugalamājye kṣiptvā'nale kṣipet |
gururhṛdayamantreṇa pavitrīkaraṇaṃ tvidam ||

abhimantrya ca mūlena rakṣayedastramuccaran |
pradarśya dhenuyonī ca tadājyamamṛtātmakam ||

homavidhiḥ

prādeśamātraṃ sagranthidarbhayugmaṃ dhṛtāntare |
nikṣipya bhāgau dvau kṛtvā pakṣau śukletarau smaret ||

vāme nāḍīmiḍāṃ bhāge dakṣiṇe piṅgalāṃ punaḥ |
suṣumnāṃ madhyato dhyātvā kuryāddhomaṃ yathāvidhi ||

sruk sruvau ca samādāya vidhinā nirmitau guruḥ |
paścādādāya pāṇibhyāṃ sruksruvau tāvadhomukhau ||

triśaḥ pratāpayedvahnau darbhānādāya deśikaḥ |
tadagramadhyamūlāni śodhayet tairyathākramam ||

gṛhītvā vāmahastena prokṣayeddakṣiṇena tau |
punaḥ pratāpya tau mantrī darbhānagnau vinikṣipet ||

sruvamādāya matimān dhārayet tu tribhāgataḥ |
vedāṃgulaṃ parityajya dhārayecchaṅkhamudrayā ||

śāradāyām �

sruveṇa dakṣiṇādbhāgādādāyājyaṃ hṛdā guruḥ |
juhuyādagnaye svāhetyagnerdakṣiṇalocane ||

vāmatastadvādādāya vāme vahnivilocane |
juhuyādatha somāya svāheti hṛdayāṇunā ||

madhyādājyaṃ samādāya vahnerbhālavilocane |
juhuyadāgnīṣomābhyāṃ svāheti manunā guruḥ ||

hṛnmantreṇa sruvenājyaṃ bhāgādādāya dakṣiṇāt |
juhuyādagnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti tanmukhe ||

ityagnernetravaktrāṇāṃ kuryāccodghāṭanaṃ guruḥ |
satārābhirvyāhṛtibhirājyena juhuyāt punaḥ |
vaisvānareṇa mantreṇa trivāraṃ juhuyād guruḥ ||

samayātantre-

ekaikāgutibhiḥ kuryād garbhādhānādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
krameṇa devadeveśi ! svāhāntamūlavidyayā ||

garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā |
jātakarma nāmakṛtirūpaniṣkramaṇaṃ tathā ||

cūḍopanayanaṃ bhūyo vedādhyayanameva ca |
godānaṃ ca vivāhaśca saṃskārāḥ śubhakarmaṇi ||

tataśca pitarau vahnaiḥ sampūjya hṛdayaṃ nayet |
vahnimantreṇa vidhivat kuryādāhutipañcakam ||

juhuyāt samidhaḥ pañca mūlāgraghṛtasaṃplutāḥ |
gururhṛdayamantreṇa vidhivat svāhayā vinā ||

śāradāyām �

mantrairjihvāṅgamūrtīnāṃ kramād vahneryathāvidhi |
pratyekaṃ juhuyādekāmāhutiṃ mantravittamaḥ ||

avadāya sruveṇājyaṃ catuḥ sruci pidhāya tām |
sruveṇa tiṣṭhannevā'gnau deśiko yatamānasaḥ |
juhuyādvahnimantreṇa vauṣaḍantena sampade ||

mādhavīyasaṃhitāyām-

palāśasyāparā vāpi yajñīyā dvādaśāṃgulāḥ |
avakrāśca svayaṃ śuṣkāḥ satvaco nirvraṇāḥ samāḥ ||

daśāṃgulā vā vihitāḥ kaniṣṭhāṃgulisammitāḥ |
prādeśamāttasyā'lābhe hotavyāḥ sakalā api ||

gautamīye-

mahāgaṇeśamantreṇa hunedekādaśāhutīḥ |
sāmānyaṃ sarvadevānāmetadagnimukhaṃ smṛtam ||

bahurūpākhyajihvāyāmājyañca parameśvari |
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya juhuyāt ṣoḍaśāgutīḥ |
mūlamantreṇa vidhivadvaktraikīkaraṇaṃ tvidam ||

śāradāyām-

tataḥ pīṭhaṃ samabhyarya devatāyā hutāśane |
arcayedagnirūpāṃ tāṃ devatāmiṣṭadāyinīm ||

tanmukhe juhuyānmantrī pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
ājyena mūlamantreṇa vaktraikīkaraṇaṃ tvidam ||

vahnidevatayoraikyamātmanā saha bhāvayan |
mūlamantreṇa juhuyādājyenaikādaśāhutīḥ ||

nāḍīsandhānamuddiṣṭametadāgamavedibhiḥ |
aṅgādiparivārāṇāmekaikāmāhutiṃ hunet ||

punarvyāhṛtibhirhutvā homaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi |
tilenājyena juhuyāt sahasrādi yathāvidhi ||

anukte tu havirdravye tilājyaṃ havirucyate |
alpaṃ tu juhuyādvahneḥ paṇḍitaḥ sarvakarmasu |
tathā sampātayedbhāgeṣvājyasyānvāhutiṃ kramāt ||

viśeṣamāha tantrāntare-

agnau svāheti tadbhāge śeṣamagnau viniḥkṣipet |

	oṃ bhūragnaye pṛthivyai mahate ca svāhā | oṃ bhuvo vāyave 
antarīkṣāya ca dive mahate ca svāhā | oṃ svaścandramase nakṣatrebhyo 
digbhyo mahate ca svāhā | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsvaścandramase nakṣatrebhyo 
digbhyo mahate ca svāhā |

sruvañcaiva samādāya ghṛtenāpūryate punaḥ |
homadravyāṇi nikṣipya nābhau saṃsthāpyate punaḥ ||

agnernāmakṛtiṃ kṛtvā dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ priye |
brahmārpaṇena na manunā dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ punaḥ |
yojayed hṛdaye dhāmni sveṣṭaṃ sādhakasattamaḥ ||

agnimukhanirūpaṇam

śāradāyām �

yataḥ kāṣṭhaṃ tataḥ śrotraṃ yatau dhūmo'tra nāsikā |
yatrālpajvalanaṃ netraṃ yato bhasma tataḥ śiraḥ |
yatra prajvalito vahnistanmukhaṃ jātavedasaḥ ||

śrotrādiṣu homaphalam

phalamāha śāradāyām �

vadhiratvaṃ karṇahome netra kṣatamavāpnuyāt |
nāsikāyāṃ manaḥpīḍā śivohomo hi śūladaḥ |
mukhe sindūravālārka vahnerhomaḥ śubhāvahaḥ ||

bherīvāridahastīndradhvanirvahneḥ śubhapradaḥ ||

candracandanakundābho dhūmaḥ sarvārthasiddhidaḥ |
kharavāyasavacchabdo vahniḥ sarvavināśakṛt ||

kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇagatervarṇau yajamānaṃ vināśayet |
nāgacampakapunnāgapāṭalāyūthikānibhaḥ ||

padmendīvarakahlārasarpirguggulasannibhaḥ |
pāvakasya śubho gandha ityuktastantravedibhiḥ ||

pūtigandho hutabhujo hoturduḥkhaprado bhavet |
evaṃ vidheṣu doṣeṣu prāyaścittāya deśikaḥ |
mūlenājyena juhuyāt pañcaviṃśatikāhutīḥ ||

iti homavidhinirṇayaḥ |

sarvamaṅgalādināmārthaḥ

sarvamaṅgalādināmnāṃ yogārthānāha �

maṅgalā'rhasi sarveṣāṃ tena tvaṃ sarvamaṅgalā |
varadā'si ca martyānāṃ varadā tena kīrtyase ||

aśeṣaṃ jayase durgaṃ durgā tena nigadyase |
bhaktānāṃ śaṃ karosīti śaṅkarī tvaṃ tu gīyase ||

saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmiha |
durgaikā hi paraḥ poto narāṇāṃ muktaye sadā ||

saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ durgaikā paramaṃ padam |
durgaikā devatāḥ sarvā durgaikā karma vaidikam ||

durgaikā paramaṃ jñānaṃ durgaikā paramaṃ balam |
na tvayā rahitaṃ kiñcid bhūtaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam ||

durgaikā paramā devī durgaikā paramaṃ padam |
durgaikā paramaṃ jñānaṃ durgaikā jñānameva ca ||

durgaikā paramaṃ satyaṃ durgaikā paramā gatiḥ |
durgaikā paramaṃ daivaṃ durgaikā paramauṣadham ||

durgaikā sukhamatyantaṃ durgaikā nirvṛtiḥ parā |
durgaikā paramā tuṣṭi durgaikā paramaṃ yaśaḥ ||

durgaikā paramaṃ tatvaṃ durgābhinnamidaṃ jagat |
prāṇaprayāṇapātheyaṃ saṃsāravyādhibheṣajam |
durgārṇavaparitrāṇaṃ durgānāmākṣaradvayam ||

iti śrīparivrācakācārya 
paramahaṃsatīrthāvadhūtaśrīmadbrahmānandagirikṛtāyāṃ 
śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ homādinirṇayo nāmāṣṭādaśollāsaḥ 
samāptaḥ || 18 ||

samāpto'yaṃ granthaḥ
